# WHO 2024 - Chăm sóc trước sinh cho trải nghiệm thai kỳ tích cực

## Lịch chăm sóc trước sinh (ANC)

### Tối thiểu 8 lần tiếp xúc với nhân viên y tế
Đối với thai kỳ bình thường, khỏe mạnh, WHO khuyến nghị **tối thiểu 8 lần tiếp xúc ANC toàn diện**. Lần đầu nên diễn ra trong tam cá nguyệt I, tốt nhất **8–12 tuần**. Lịch được nêu trong tài liệu: **lần 1: 8–12 tuần; lần 2: 20 tuần; lần 3: 26 tuần; lần 4: 30 tuần; lần 5: 34 tuần; lần 6: 36 tuần; lần 7: 38 tuần; lần 8: 40 tuần**. Nếu có bệnh lý/biến chứng như malaria hoặc HIV, cần lịch dày hơn hoặc điều chỉnh. Nếu thai phụ đến muộn, phải điều chỉnh lịch để vẫn cung cấp đủ thành phần chăm sóc thiết yếu trước sinh.

### Kế hoạch sinh và cấp cứu
Ngay từ lần khám đầu, cần cùng thai phụ xây dựng **birth and emergency plan (kế hoạch sinh và cấp cứu)**: nơi sinh có người đỡ sinh có kỹ năng, phương tiện di chuyển, vật dụng cần chuẩn bị, cơ sở sẽ đến khi cấp cứu, địa chỉ/số điện thoại, và phương án người hiến máu nếu cần truyền máu. Gia đình cần được hướng dẫn nhận biết dấu hiệu nguy hiểm và tìm trợ giúp ngay.

## Đánh giá nhanh và dấu hiệu cần cấp cứu

### Quick check ở mọi lần tiếp xúc
Mỗi lần thai phụ đến cơ sở chăm sóc nên bắt đầu bằng đánh giá nhanh để nhận diện tình trạng cần xử trí ngay. Các dấu hiệu được tài liệu liệt kê gồm: đang được cáng/đẩy vào, **chảy máu âm đạo, co giật, bất tỉnh, trông rất nặng, đau bụng dữ dội, đang chuyển dạ/sắp sinh, sốt, rỉ/chảy nước ối, đau đầu hoặc rối loạn thị giác, nôn ói nặng**. Khi có dấu hiệu nguy hiểm, ưu tiên cấp cứu thay vì để thai phụ chờ theo quy trình thường quy.

## Chăm sóc dự phòng thường quy

### Tiêm phòng, sắt-folate, tẩy giun và phòng sốt rét
Gói dự phòng được nêu gồm **vaccine chứa giải độc tố uốn ván**, **sắt + folate**, tẩy giun bằng **mebendazole**, **IPTp (Intermittent Preventive Treatment in pregnancy - điều trị dự phòng sốt rét ngắt quãng trong thai kỳ)** và ngủ màn tẩm hóa chất tại vùng lưu hành sốt rét; đồng thời dự phòng lây truyền mẹ-con HIV, viêm gan virus và giang mai. Nếu chưa tiêm uốn ván hoặc không rõ tình trạng tiêm, tài liệu hướng dẫn 2 liều: liều đầu càng sớm càng tốt trong thai kỳ và liều thứ hai ít nhất 4 tuần sau.

### Sắt và axit folic
Bắt đầu sắt/folate từ lần khám đầu; công thức trong tài liệu là **1 viên = 60 mg sắt + 400 mcg axit folic mỗi ngày** để dự phòng. Nếu thiếu máu thai kỳ, tài liệu sử dụng **1 viên hai lần/ngày**. Cần tư vấn cách dùng, tác dụng phụ và tuân thủ; cấp bổ sung ở các lần khám sau.

### Tẩy giun và IPTp-SP
Mebendazole **500 mg một lần** trong tam cá nguyệt II hoặc III theo tài liệu. Với **IPTp-SP (sulfadoxine-pyrimethamine)**, bắt đầu từ tam cá nguyệt II; các liều cách nhau ít nhất 1 tháng, mục tiêu ít nhất 3 liều. Liều được nêu: **3 viên**, mỗi viên chứa **sulfadoxine 500 mg + pyrimethamine 25 mg**. Việc áp dụng phụ thuộc vùng lưu hành và hướng dẫn quốc gia.

## Thiếu máu trong thai kỳ

### Ngưỡng chẩn đoán và mức độ
Tài liệu nêu thiếu máu khi Hb <11,0 g/dL trong tam cá nguyệt I hoặc <10,5 g/dL trong tam cá nguyệt II; phần thuật toán quản lý sử dụng ngưỡng **Hb <11 g/dL = thiếu máu thai kỳ**. Thiếu máu vừa được mô tả ở khoảng **Hb >7 và <11 g/dL**; thiếu máu nặng **Hb <7 g/dL**. Mọi thai phụ cần được sàng lọc thiếu máu trong ANC và tìm nguyên nhân nền.

### If-Then: xử trí thiếu máu
- **Nếu Hb <7 g/dL hoặc có triệu chứng thiếu máu ở bất kỳ tuổi thai:** chuyển khẩn đến bệnh viện có khả năng truyền máu để nhập viện và xử trí thích hợp.
- **Nếu Hb >7 và <11 g/dL:** dùng sắt + folate điều trị, tư vấn dinh dưỡng, tẩy giun sau tam cá nguyệt I, dự phòng sốt rét phù hợp, theo dõi Hb và tăng trưởng thai, đồng thời tìm và điều trị nguyên nhân khác.
- Đáp ứng mong đợi với điều trị sắt uống được tài liệu mô tả là Hb tăng khoảng **1,0 g/dL sau 2 tuần** nếu điều trị hiệu quả.

## Đái tháo đường và siêu âm

### Ngưỡng chẩn đoán đái tháo đường trong thai kỳ
Tài liệu nêu chẩn đoán diabetes mellitus in pregnancy nếu đạt ít nhất một tiêu chí: **glucose huyết tương lúc đói >7,0 mmol/L (126 mg/dL)**; **glucose 2 giờ >11,1 mmol/L (200 mg/dL) sau tải 75 g glucose**; hoặc **glucose ngẫu nhiên >11,1 mmol/L (200 mg/dL) kèm triệu chứng đái tháo đường**. Thai phụ có đa niệu, khát nhiều, tiểu đêm, tiền sử gia đình đái tháo đường, tiền sử bất dung nạp glucose/đái tháo đường, thai chết lưu hoặc thai to cần được xem xét sàng lọc.

### Siêu âm sớm
Tài liệu đào tạo nhắc khuyến nghị **một lần siêu âm trước 24 tuần** để hỗ trợ xác định tuổi thai và đánh giá phù hợp theo khuyến nghị WHO. Kết quả siêu âm phải được tích hợp với đánh giá lâm sàng, không thay thế theo dõi toàn diện.

### Nguồn gốc: World Health Organization — đoạn 028

### Nguồn gốc: Foreword — đoạn 029

“The World Health Organization (WHO) envisions a world where every pregnant woman and newborn receives quality care throughout the pregnancy, childbirth and the postnatal period. Within the continuum of reproductive health care, antenatal care (ANC) provides a platform for important health care functions, including health promotion, screening and diagnosis and disease prevention. It has been established that by implementing timely and appropriate evidence-based practices, ANC can save lives.

### Nguồn gốc: Foreword — đoạn 030

Crucially, ANC also provides the opportunity to communicate with and support women, families and communities at a critical time in the course of a woman’s life.” (WHO recommendations on ANC for a positive pregnancy experience 2016) To respond to the complex nature of antenatal care organization and delivery and ensure a woman-centred approach to services, the World Health Organization (WHO) released its comprehensive recommendations on routine antenatal care (ANC) for pregnant women and adolescent girls on 7 November 2016.

### Nguồn gốc: Foreword — đoạn 031

These recommendations included several changes, such as the eight-contact schedule, the health systems approach to implementation, the recommendation on one ultrasound for all pregnant women before 24 weeks, and the concept of a “positive pregnancy experience.” The lack of ANC training materials adapted to the African context is often cited by countries in the African Region as an impediment to the uptake and scale-up of quality antenatal care services. This is all the more so in view of the overwhelming evidence of their added value, including mortality reduction through the interventions recommended in the 2016 WHO ANC recommendations, and the introduction of new concepts that need to be understood for better implementation. To address this, WHO AFRO has developed a regional ANC training package with the aim of providing countries with generic training materials that can be adapted and used both for in-service and pre-service training on antenatal care. This package comprises a facilitator manual, a participant manual and a set of PowerPoint slides.

### Nguồn gốc: Foreword — đoạn 032

The target audience for this material includes programme managers, trainers and ANC service providers in both public and private sectors in the WHO African Region. The materials are intended to be tailored to countryspecific contexts based on selected core interventions and approaches within national ANC guidelines. It is assumed that countries will have already adapted and aligned their ANC guidelines using the WHO ANC recommendations adaptation toolkit: https://www.researchgate.net/publication/342360077_. It is our hope that these training materials will be rolled out and used in pre-service and in-service training of health service providers in all countries within the African Region to improve their capacity and in turn the quality of ANC provided to pregnant women in Africa and beyond. We believe that you will find these materials extremely useful.

### Nguồn gốc: WHO Regional Office for Africa — đoạn 033

vii

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for the course — đoạn 034

Welcome to the facilitator manual for training in antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience! This guide will enable you to prepare for and conduct the training, but also to facilitate post-training activities in order to effectively achieve the goals and objectives of this course.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for the course — đoạn 035

This training course on antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience was developed to support the strengthening of health provider skills in the provision of antenatal care. It can be used in both pre-service and in-service training of ANC service providers. The estimated duration of this course is five days. However, the actual time frame will depend on factors such as the learning needs and abilities of participants and the resources available.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for the course — đoạn 036

The course is based on adult learning principles to achieve the competencies required to care for pregnant women. It includes interactive lectures, group discussions, individual and group exercises, role plays and skills practice. The course may be adapted at country level to ensure that it is consistent with national policies and care standards. It is clearly recognized that training is only one component of an effective antenatal care programme.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for the course — đoạn 037

Following this training, participants will undergo a post-training follow-up to ensure that the skills learned are applied at the workplace. Regular supportive supervision will also be provided to ensure that consistent and high-quality antenatal services are provided.

### Nguồn gốc: About the training course — đoạn 038

This training course “Providing antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience” addresses routine antenatal care and includes specific management of clinical conditions relating to the ANC period. It is based primarily on the WHO document titled Pregnancy, childbirth, postpartum and newborn care: a guide for essential practice (hereinafter the PCPNC guide). It also refers to relevant technical briefs and guidelines on illnesses that have an impact on pregnancy outcomes, including malaria, anaemia, HIV, hepatitis B, and STIs.

### Nguồn gốc: Structure of the training course — đoạn 039

This training course deals with antenatal care, including the common clinical conditions associated with the antenatal period. It includes 14 sessions plus a follow-up session after the training:

### Nguồn gốc: Session 10: Prevention and management of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 040

Session 11: Preventing mother to child transmission of HIV, syphilis and hepatitis B

### Nguồn gốc: Session 13: Skills practice: selected clinical skills — đoạn 041

Session 14: Implementing antenatal care in your facility (follow-up after training) viii

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing to conduct the course — đoạn 051

The key steps in preparing this training course for ANC providers are briefly described below: 1.

### Nguồn gốc: Involve policy-makers — đoạn 052

National policy-makers, including reproductive and maternal health programme managers, should be involved in adapting the course to ensure that the objectives and content of the course are consistent with national policies, standards and guidelines. The course may need to be adjusted depending on the country strategy regarding the scope of practice for nurses, midwives and other ANC providers; as well as the state of health systems, including available infrastructure, drugs and commodities, training, and supportive supervision.

### Nguồn gốc: Involve policy-makers — đoạn 053

2.

### Nguồn gốc: Involve supervisors — đoạn 054

It is important to involve the supervisors of ANC providers in this training. They should understand the content of this course and the tasks that ANC providers will perform so that they are able to provide supportive supervision. This may entail organizing an orientation session for supervisors prior to the training of ANC providers and their involvement as observers or facilitators. Similarly, it is important to involve facility directors to facilitate change in how ANC services are organized.

### Nguồn gốc: Involve supervisors — đoạn 055

3.

### Nguồn gốc: Select participants — đoạn 056

Participants in this course must have basic knowledge and skills in pregnancy and childbirth care if they are to participate actively. Those who are well motivated and see the relevance of the course to their daily work are more likely to benefit and to implement their newly acquired knowledge and skills. The recommended number of participants per training session is 16−20.

### Nguồn gốc: Select participants — đoạn 057

4.

### Nguồn gốc: Select facilitators — đoạn 058

The recommended facilitator-to-participant ratio is 1:4–6. If this ratio is not possible, the time required may be extended slightly or additional facilitators may be called in for specific sessions – for example, group work, skills practice (sessions 5 and 12). A facilitator should be tasked to arrange the logistics for clinic visits and skills practice exercises. The chart below shows the recommended number of facilitators based on the number of participants.

### Nguồn gốc: Facilitators — đoạn 066

The facilitators to be selected for this course should have technical knowledge and clinical skills in maternal and newborn health issues. They should have some experience in training and mentorship of ANC providers. Finally, they should have attended this course previously as participants when it was conducted by expert facilitators.

### Nguồn gốc: Facilitators — đoạn 067

A facilitator should have: • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Facilitators — đoạn 068

experience in antenatal care evidence of competency in the clinical skills being taught the interest and time to conduct ANC training courses successfully completed the course on “Providing antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience” been trained as a facilitator

### Nguồn gốc: Facilitators — đoạn 069

Training of facilitators: This is very important and should include a review of the content of the training materials. The training should also demonstrate the various teaching methods and steps in line with the content and process of the training course (see III. How a facilitator teaches and assesses learning progress). The facilitator trainees should take turns practising the teaching steps with guidance and feedback from experienced facilitators.

### Nguồn gốc: Facilitators — đoạn 070

5.

### Nguồn gốc: Select the training venue — đoạn 071

It is important to assess and select a training venue with the following criteria in mind: • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Select the training venue — đoạn 072

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Select the training venue — đoạn 073

6.

### Nguồn gốc: Select the training venue — đoạn 074

Does the facility have a plenary meeting room large enough to accommodate all participants, facilitators and projection equipment and screen? Does the facility have enough rooms, or space in the plenary room, for small group work? Is the venue within easy access for facilitators and participants?

### Nguồn gốc: Select the training venue — đoạn 075

Does the venue have reasonable access to two or more antenatal clinics that may be visited for observation? (Because a site visit is part of the training, the choice of the venue should consider both the classroom facilities available and their proximity to health facilities and a population of pregnant women).

### Nguồn gốc: Select the training venue — đoạn 076

Does the facility have the necessary equipment and supplies, or can they be obtained? Is there a nearby hospital or clinic that would assist in providing clinical supplies and specimens for skills practice?

### Nguồn gốc: Ensure availability of training materials, equipment and supplies — đoạn 120

The training materials are described below. Ensure that they are budgeted for and secured during planning. • •

### Nguồn gốc: Ensure availability of training materials, equipment and supplies — đoạn 122

Facilitator guide (one for each facilitator): The Facilitator guide provides step-by-step guidance for facilitators on how to conduct interactive lectures using PowerPoint slides and exercises such as small group work, case studies, role plays, and drills.

### Nguồn gốc: Ensure availability of training materials, equipment and supplies — đoạn 123

Participant manual (one for each participant and one for each facilitator): The Participant manual provides basic information on antenatal care of pregnant women. It also contains exercises (with instructions and worksheets) that participants will be asked to do during the sessions. After the training, participants can use the manual as reference material in the course of their work.

### Nguồn gốc: Ensure availability of training materials, equipment and supplies — đoạn 124

Updated national ANC guidelines and protocols: Since the generic ANC recommendations include context-specific recommendations, this is an important resource in identifying what has been included in national ANC guidelines and adjusting the training content accordingly.

### Nguồn gốc: Ensure availability of training materials, equipment and supplies — đoạn 125

Pregnancy, childbirth, postpartum and newborn care: a guide for essential practice (PCPNC), 3rd edition, WHO 2015 (one for each participant and facilitator): This is the basic reference for this training. Since this guide is updated regularly, ensure that you have the latest edition.

### Nguồn gốc: Ensure availability of training materials, equipment and supplies — đoạn 126

PowerPoint slides for facilitators: For each session, PowerPoint slides are provided.

### Nguồn gốc: Session 13 — đoạn 171

9.

### Nguồn gốc: Organize site visits and skills practice sessions — đoạn 172

In addition to the usual activities performed in a classroom, this training course includes a site visit to one or two antenatal clinics to observe the process of routine ANC service delivery and internalize how the competencies learned can be applied in a real-time setting. It also includes a skills session to practice using common rapid diagnostic tests used during ANC, including the performance of several critical ANC skills such as EDC calculation using a pregnancy wheel (Session 12).

### Nguồn gốc: Organize site visits and skills practice sessions — đoạn 173

An organizer or one of the facilitators should be designated to plan and organize the site visits and skills sessions. (a)

### Nguồn gốc: Organizing health facility visits — đoạn 174

Organize visits to nearby health facilities where more than 10 antenatal clients are seen each day. The purpose is for participants to see the ANC setups and how antenatal care is provided. If time allows, arrange for each participant to see two different clinics. You may divide the participants into two groups, to reduce the number of participants in a facility at any given time and minimize the disruption to regular clinic operations.

### Nguồn gốc: Organizing health facility visits — đoạn 175

Before the training, the organizer should: • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Organizing health facility visits — đoạn 176

(b)

### Nguồn gốc: Organizing health facility visits — đoạn 177

visit the health facility (or facilities in the area) to assess the case load of pregnant women and the possibility for participants to visit and observe; visit the health facility before the course, to meet with the ANC personnel, explain the purpose of the visit, and request their assistance; and ensure the availability of transportation for participants, facilitators, supervisors and any other observers to and from the health facilities, according to the course schedule.

### Nguồn gốc: Organizing practice sessions on selected clinical skills — đoạn 178

This session should be conducted in a large classroom. Various skills stations should be set up, each supervised by a facilitator. • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Organizing practice sessions on selected clinical skills — đoạn 179

Select the skills to be included in your training. For each selected skill, ensure availability of adequate supplies (urine dipsticks, rapid test for HIV, rapid test for syphilis, rapid test for hepatitis B, etc.) to afford all participants an opportunity to practice the skill.

### Nguồn gốc: Organizing practice sessions on selected clinical skills — đoạn 180

Arrange for the delivery of clinical specimens (blood, urine). These should be kept cool and disposed of safely in line with biological waste management protocols. Assign facilitators to supervise the stations. They will need to prepare themselves to give an initial introduction, explain and demonstrate the skill, and then supervise participants as they practice the skill.

### Nguồn gốc: Organizing practice sessions on selected clinical skills — đoạn 181

10.

### Nguồn gốc: Specific preparations for particular sessions — đoạn 182

(a)

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for Session 9: Malaria prevention and control in pregnancy — đoạn 183

Find out what antimalarial medicines and regimens (dosage and duration) are nationally recommended for the treatment of malaria in clinics where participants work. The text does not specify the medicines but tells participants to follow national protocols. Be ready to state the recommendations and answer any questions about the regimens.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for Session 9: Malaria prevention and control in pregnancy — đoạn 184

(b)

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for Session 9: Malaria prevention and control in pregnancy — đoạn 185

Preparing for Session 11: Preventing mother-to-child transmission of HIV, syphilis, hepatitis B and TB

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for Session 9: Malaria prevention and control in pregnancy — đoạn 186

Familiarize yourself with the background information on the pathogenesis and clinical presentations of the four diseases. Find out about the national guidelines on ARV regimens for HIV and HBV, including other treatment protocols that participants should follow: are ARVs supplied to clinics? What treatment regimens are recommended for women with hepatitis B, syphilis and tuberculosis? What treatment regimens are recommended for their newborns? Are CD4 measurements available and are they used to determine the appropriate regimens?

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for Session 9: Malaria prevention and control in pregnancy — đoạn 187

xiv

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for Session 9: Malaria prevention and control in pregnancy — đoạn 188

Be ready to share the national recommendations and answer any questions about treatment regimens. Make copies of the protocols and recommended regimens for participants. Also find out whether midwives are allowed to initiate treatment with ARVs and anti-tuberculous drugs; when they must refer a pregnant woman for assessment and initiation of treatment, etc,. This information will influence how much attention you will give to these details during the session.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for Session 9: Malaria prevention and control in pregnancy — đoạn 189

(c)

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for Session 12: Use of ultrasonography in antenatal care — đoạn 190

If the facilitator is not familiar with the basics of ultrasound scan technology, read the session materials and do some additional reading until you feel comfortable presenting the topic. You should also explore its availability and use in the area where participants work.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for Session 12: Use of ultrasonography in antenatal care — đoạn 191

To prepare for the debate at the end of this session, search the internet for two or more articles that support routine ultrasound in pregnancy and two or more others that take the opposite view. Make several copies of these articles for the group of participants assigned to that point of view. Where additional information on ultrasonography is of great interest or needed or requested, arrange for participants to visit an ultrasonography clinic if possible.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for Session 12: Use of ultrasonography in antenatal care — đoạn 192

11.

### Nguồn gốc: Plan post-training follow-up activities — đoạn 193

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Plan post-training follow-up activities — đoạn 194

Participants develop a plan of action for implementing antenatal care at their own facilities. A follow-up contact with each participant should be planned four to six weeks after the training. During post-course follow-up, track the progress made in implementing individual plans of action

### Nguồn gốc: Plan post-training follow-up activities — đoạn 195

The follow-up will be conducted during a site visit by the facilitator to each participant’s workplace. The national supervision team will ensure long-term follow-up.

### Nguồn gốc: Role of the facilitator in teaching and assessing progress — đoạn 196

As a facilitator, you help participants learn; you motivate them; and you manage the training session. To facilitate learning: • • • • • • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Role of the facilitator in teaching and assessing progress — đoạn 197

give information through interactive lectures using PowerPoint slides; ask questions during the lectures to keep participants actively engaged; use your personality and communication skills to maintain energy during the sessions and keep participants interested; make sure that each participant understands how to work through the materials and what he or she is expected to do in each exercise; answer questions and explain what seems confusing; lead group discussions, manage presentations of small group reports, conduct drills, supervise exercises, and observe role plays; assess each participant’s work and contributions; help each participant identify how to apply the skills taught in the course to their work; explain what to do and model good clinical and communication skills in the skills practice session; and give guidance and feedback as needed during classroom sessions, site visits and skills practice sessions.

### Nguồn gốc: Role of the facilitator in teaching and assessing progress — đoạn 198

To motivate: • •

### Nguồn gốc: Role of the facilitator in teaching and assessing progress — đoạn 199

praise participants and the group for improving their performance and developing new skills and knowledge; and encourage participants to move past the initial difficulties in learning and implementing new skills.

### Nguồn gốc: Role of the facilitator in teaching and assessing progress — đoạn 200

To manage: • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Role of the facilitator in teaching and assessing progress — đoạn 201

plan ahead and obtain all supplies needed each day; monitor the progress of each participant; and work with the facilitator team to identify improvements each day. xv

### Nguồn gốc: Teaching methods — đoạn 202

The teaching methods are described below. Not all steps or methods are used in all sessions. Details on the specific methodology used are provided in the sessions.

### Nguồn gốc: Illustrative lectures — đoạn 203

Illustrative lectures are used to introduce new information and review content that is familiar to participants. The abridged version of the lectures has been provided in PowerPoint format. Facilitators are encouraged to read the facilitator’s notes for each slide to make their lectures as interesting as possible. To maximize their participation in lectures, participants should be encouraged to read their manuals in preparation for the sessions. This can be done by giving earlier assignments or by asking them to read out selected sections during presentations.

### Nguồn gốc: Illustrative lectures — đoạn 204

Facilitators may use the following effective presentation skills to maintain a conducive learning climate: • • • • • • • • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Illustrative lectures — đoạn 205

communicate in a way that is easy to understand maintain eye contact with learners during presentations project your voice so all can hear clearly avoid the use of slang and abbreviations display enthusiasm about the subject being presented move around the room use appropriate audiovisuals ask both simple and challenging questions to obtain feedback learn and use participants’ names as much as possible display positive humour provide smooth transitions between topics be an effective role model (for example, be on time, dress appropriately, follow established norms for the training)

### Nguồn gốc: Illustrative lectures — đoạn 206

Facilitators are encouraged to augment the content of the training materials with information from other reliable sources. They may however simply follow the outline provided. In either case, it is important to prepare in advance for each lecture by reading the relevant content, reviewing the slides, and ensuring that resources required for the session are available.

### Nguồn gốc: Discussions — đoạn 207

Dedicate some time for discussion at certain points during each session and at the conclusion of the session. This will allow participants to clarify information that is unclear to them and make contributions based on their knowledge and experience. It will also provide an opportunity for the facilitator to appreciate the views and level of knowledge and understanding of the participants.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work and feedback — đoạn 208

Many of the exercises involve group work – that is, groups of participants formally working together on an assignment, exercise or task. The groups should be kept as small as possible (preferably not more than six participants per group). This will provide an opportunity for all participants to contribute to discussions. Each group should choose a chairperson and a rapporteur. A facilitator should sit in with each group to: observe and intervene as necessary, correct misunderstandings, or make essential points.

### Nguồn gốc: Case studies — đoạn 209

In the case study method, the facilitator uses descriptive situations or scenarios. ‘The purpose of the case study method is to make trainees apply what they know, develop new ideas to manage a situation, or solve a problem. As a training tool, the case study method can be used to develop decision-making skills, enhance team spirit, improve communication and interpersonal skills and strengthen the analytical skills of trainees.’ (IUP Journal of Soft Skills). Case studies may be conducted individually or as a group.

### Nguồn gốc: Case studies — đoạn 210

xvi

### Nguồn gốc: Individual work followed by individual feedback — đoạn 211

Individual work followed by facilitator feedback gives the participant an opportunity to discuss their work alone with a facilitator, ask any questions, receive feedback, and if needed, receive instruction to clarify points or misunderstandings. It also gives the facilitator an opportunity to see the work of each participant and get to know them better, particularly in terms of their progress.

### Nguồn gốc: Role plays — đoạn 212

Role plays provide an opportunity to practice and observe the use of communication skills in the provision of antenatal care. In the role plays, participants take the part of specific individuals such as the midwife, the pregnant woman, the distressed relative or an observer who will give feedback. This provides them with an opportunity to review messages, messaging techniques and to practice applying the principles of counselling in relevant situations.

### Nguồn gốc: Drills — đoạn 213

Included are two classroom quizzes designed to be conducted as drills. A drill is not a test. It is an opportunity to review and recall what has been learned in order to develop speed and confidence. A drill works best when chairs are arranged in a circle.

### Nguồn gốc: Drills — đoạn 214

The following method can be used to conduct drills: the facilitator asks a participant a question. The participant should answer the question right away. If they cannot answer, they say “pass”. If the participant says ‘pass’ or answers incorrectly, the facilitator asks the next person the same question without comment or pause. If they answer correctly, the facilitator asks a new question to the next participant. This cycle continues around the circle for the duration of the activity. The pace should stay lively and the mood cheerful.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit — đoạn 215

The site visit aims to help participants understand how the principles of antenatal care should be applied in a clinic setting. The site visit (to one or preferably two sites) must be planned and organized well in advance, including the choice of appropriate clinic sites.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit — đoạn 216

A sample “Site visit guide and checklist” form designed to facilitate participants’ observations during the visit is available in Appendix 2.

### Nguồn gốc: Debate among participants — đoạn 217

Debate refers to a discussion in which two or more people advocate opposing positions on a topic or question in an attempt to make an audience (or the other advocates) accept their position. A participant debate provides a chance for participants to play an active part in critically thinking about an issue, preparing arguments for or against it, and practicing presentation skills.

### Nguồn gốc: Skills practice — đoạn 218

Skills practice (in Session 13) provides an opportunity for participants to review their knowledge and practice of a clinical skill. Facilitators must clearly explain the steps and demonstrate the skill to help participants understand precisely what they are being asked to do. Facilitators then monitor the work of participants and offer guidance when required until all participants have achieved competence. A skills checklist, which provides a clear step-by-step description of the tasks, is included for each skill to be learned.

### Nguồn gốc: Skills practice — đoạn 219

xvii

### Nguồn gốc: Pre-test and post-test knowledge — đoạn 220

Pre-tests provide a useful means of establishing a baseline for participants’ theoretical knowledge. The same questions used in the pre-test are used again in the post-test, after the relevant content has been taught, to determine the change in theoretical knowledge. For the purpose of this training, a set of pre- and post-test questions have been included in the appendixes of this manual along with answer keys.

### Nguồn gốc: Pre-test and post-test knowledge — đoạn 221

It should be noted, however, that during the training, other assessment methods should be used to determine the progress made by each participant during the course.

### Nguồn gốc: Quizzes — đoạn 222

Quizzes help students identify what they do and do not know. Rather than only testing students on the big picture, quizzes give teachers a chance to test students on the smaller pieces. The students then have a better idea of how well they are grasping the material, hopefully motivating them to study more and helping them allocate their study time effectively by focusing on the information that still needs more practice.

### Nguồn gốc: Quizzes — đoạn 223

Two quizzes have been included in this training package: the first, after sessions 1 to 6; and the second, after sessions 7 to 11. The questions are included in the participant manual and the answers in the appendix of the facilitator’s guide.

### Nguồn gốc: Assessment of clinical skills — đoạn 224

Selected clinical skills will be evaluated at the skills stations using a skills checklist. Facilitators should observe and evaluate each participant to ensure they understand the procedure and can perform it correctly.

### Nguồn gốc: Course evaluation (daily and at the end of the course) — đoạn 225

Daily course evaluations should be conducted. At the end of the whole course, a comprehensive evaluation should be undertaken. Evaluations should include assessments of course organization, facilitator performance and the overall view of participants, including their satisfaction with each session and the entire programme. A sample session evaluation form and an end-of-course evaluation form can be found in the appendix.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for each training session — đoạn 226

Before each day of training, the lead facilitator must discuss with other facilitators the requirements of planned sessions and who will prepare for which activities. For each session, the facilitator giving the lecture must review all session slides and practice using them before giving the interactive lecture.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing for each training session — đoạn 227

The most important preparation for all facilitators is to familiarize themselves with the session slides and related familiar with navigating the PCPNC, particularly the ANC-relevant charts. Where there is a reference to specified flowcharts in the PCPNP guide, review those charts beforehand so as to be ready to describe how to use them.

### Nguồn gốc: Other important points to consider before you begin — đoạn 228

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Other important points to consider before you begin — đoạn 229

•

### Nguồn gốc: Other important points to consider before you begin — đoạn 230

Ensure that the required training materials, equipment and supplies are available for the session (See section II, steps 9 and 11). Ensure that the necessary arrangements have been made for the site visit and for Session 13 – the skills practice session. The lead facilitator or the organizer should brief the facilitating team on the arrangements.

### Nguồn gốc: Other important points to consider before you begin — đoạn 231

Be mindful of the possible need to adjust the time frame indicated in the plan. Depending on the knowledge and abilities of participants, and on their learning needs, the actual time required for an activity may be longer or shorter than the time specified in the schedule.

### Nguồn gốc: Other important points to consider before you begin — đoạn 232

Review the results of the pre-test to identify areas that need specific reinforcement.

(Note that the participant manual does not contain slides but contains all the text that appears in the slides plus additional information).

When teaching the various course sessions, it is important that you first review the relevant slides and adapt / update them with country-specific information as needed, or with any new evidence / data that may be available.

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 251

o facilitator guide, participant manual o PCPNC manual o national ANC protocols and guidelines o reference documents (MCPC manual, RCP, WHO recommendations for a positive pregnancy experience 2016)

### Nguồn gốc: Preparation — đoạn 256

Presentation of the session:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 2: ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 257

Every year, women become pregnant and hope for a positive outcome for themselves and their unborn babies. In most cases, women will have a normal pregnancy and can hope for childbirth without any complications. As antenatal care providers, we have the opportunity to work with the woman and her family to promote a healthy, normal pregnancy and, where needed, to identify and manage any complications or problems that may occur.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: What are pregnant women dying from? — đoạn 258

chart. Ask if these causes are preventable and if antenatal care can contribute substantially to reducing these deaths. Ask why these causes continue to be major contributors to maternal death even though in many countries ANC coverage is high and increasing?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: What are pregnant women dying from? — đoạn 259

List the major causes of maternal death emphasizing their magnitudes and the fact that they are largely preventable. Reasons why they continue to be major killers include: poor quality of care provided, late booking with few contact visits, and low levels of supervised deliveries.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: What are pregnant women dying from? — đoạn 260

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: What are pregnant women dying from? — đoạn 261

Slide 4: Emphasize benefits of quality ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: The availability of routine antenatal care, including antenatal screening and — đoạn 262

diagnosis, plays a major part in reducing maternal death rates and miscarriages as well as birth defects, low birth weight, neonatal infections and other preventable health problems. The high coverage of ANC and repeated contacts between the woman and the health services offer many opportunities for providing evidence-based interventions related to health promotion, disease prevention, screening, and treatment likely to affect maternal, foetal, and neonatal health and survival.

### Nguồn gốc: The availability of routine antenatal care, including antenatal screening and — đoạn 263

ANC is also an entry point for the provision of integrated care as it provides a platform for multiple interventions and programmes such as prevention of sexually transmitted illnesses and mother-to-childtransmission of HIV/hepatitis B/syphilis; prevention and case

### Nguồn gốc: management of malaria in pregnancy; prevention of maternal and — đoạn 264

neonatal tetanus; prevention of maternal anaemia and malnutrition, etc.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Providing quality ANC is critical — đoạn 265

High antenatal coverage rates on the African continent have not resulted in the desired reductions in maternal and foetal mortality and morbidity. Link this fact to the issues of poor-quality antenatal care, emphasizing that quality ANC is imperative and must be universally available.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Benefits of quality ANC — đoạn 266

Quality ANC is an opportunity for the health provider to establish partnerships with pregnant women and their families that will endure beyond the pregnancy. It is important to provide quality ANC services to encourage pregnant women and their families to use services during pregnancy, labour and childbirth, the postpartum period, and beyond.

### Nguồn gốc: Contacts with pregnant women also offer opportunities to promote healthy — đoạn 267

pregnancies, educate women and their families to make informed choices about their care, and support them to prepare for childbirth and care of the newborn.

### Nguồn gốc: Contacts with pregnant women also offer opportunities to promote healthy — đoạn 268

Slide 7: Women want a positive pregnancy experience during ANC. Note that these are the findings from a systematic review of women’s views.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive pregnancy experience is defined as maintaining physical and — đoạn 269

sociocultural normality, maintaining a healthy pregnancy for mother and baby (including preventing or treating risks, illness and death), having an effective transition to positive labour and birth, and achieving positive motherhood (including maternal self-esteem, competence and autonomy).

### Nguồn gốc: A positive pregnancy experience is defined as maintaining physical and — đoạn 270

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: The concept of “antenatal care for a positive pregnancy — đoạn 271

experience” Slide 8: The new ANC model for a positive pregnancy experience The existing ANC model of care is being revised to achieve the goals of creating a positive pregnancy experience for women. Discuss the listed care components areas in the slide that are being strengthened in the new ANC care model.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Why the WHO recommendations on ANC ? — đoạn 272

Present the evidence informing the new guideline as listed in the slide. You may ask a participant to read the slide aloud. Refer participants to the relevant references in the manual. “WHO recommendations on antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience”

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: The WHO ANC model — đoạn 273

• Briefly present the eight-contact schedule in the new model and inform

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: The WHO ANC model — đoạn 274

participants that this schedule will be further discussed in Session 3: Providing antenatal care. •

### Nguồn gốc: Evidence also shows that women want flexible appointment systems and — đoạn 275

continuity of provider care where they are given privacy and time to build authentic and supportive relationships with maternity care providers (high confidence in the evidence).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Early ultrasound for all pregnant women — đoạn 276

• This recommendation was based on evidence showing the benefits of a

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Early ultrasound for all pregnant women — đoạn 277

single ultrasound done early (before 24 weeks) on maternal and foetal outcomes. •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Early ultrasound for all pregnant women — đoạn 278

The evidence was derived from a Cochrane review of 11 RCTs conducted in Australia, Norway, South Africa, Sweden, the United Kingdom and the USA, involving 37 505 women.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Early ultrasound for all pregnant women — đoạn 279

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Early ultrasound for all pregnant women — đoạn 280

An ongoing multi-country trial in low-resource settings is under way and will contribute further evidence on health effects, care utilization and implementation-related information on ultrasound in these settings.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: A health systems approach to ANC — đoạn 281

• The new ANC model highlights health system changes that are required to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: A health systems approach to ANC — đoạn 282

create a positive care environment for care throughout pregnancy. •

### Nguồn gốc: It includes integrated care, good client–provider communication, continuity — đoạn 283

of care and creating ideal ANC clinic settings.

### Nguồn gốc: It includes integrated care, good client–provider communication, continuity — đoạn 284

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: The overarching aim of antenatal care is to provide pregnant — đoạn 285

women with respectful, individualized, person-centred care at every contact, with implementation of effective clinical practices (interventions and tests), and provision of relevant and timely information, and psychosocial and emotional support, by practitioners with good clinical and interpersonal skills within a well-functioning health system.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: The goal of this ANC training course — đoạn 286

• The goal of this training course is to strengthen the capacity of ANC service

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: The goal of this ANC training course — đoạn 287

providers to provide their clients with quality antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: The goal of this ANC training course — đoạn 288

• This course is intended for use in both pre-service and in-service training of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: The goal of this ANC training course — đoạn 289

ANC providers.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Course objectives — đoạn 290

The objectives of the training course are to: • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Course objectives — đoạn 291

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Course objectives — đoạn 292

build the capacity of ANC providers in the organization of, and systems-based approach to, ANC services; increase ANC providers’ knowledge and capacity to provide evidence-based practices for quality antenatal care; enhance the ability of ANC providers to provide appropriate counselling and health education messages based on an improved awareness of the cultural and social environments of the pregnant women who seek care; improve health care providers’ attitudes and capacity to provide quality client-centred antenatal care; and strengthen the interpersonal and communication skills of health care providers to meet the emotional, physical and support needs of ANC clients.

### Nguồn gốc: Structure of the training course — đoạn 293

Slides 16 and 17: Structure of the training course This training course deals with antenatal care, including the common clinical conditions associated with the antenatal period. It includes 14 sessions plus a post-training follow-up session: Session 0: Session 1: Session 2: Session 3: Session 4: Session 5: Session 6: Session 7: Session 8: Session 9:

experience

Session 10: Session 11: Session 12: Session 13: Session 14:

### Nguồn gốc: Prevention and management of anaemia in — đoạn 297

pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Preventing mother-to-child transmission of HIV, — đoạn 298

syphilis and hepatitis B

### Nguồn gốc: Post-course follow-up and support for ANC — đoạn 299

implementation

### Nguồn gốc: Post-course follow-up and support for ANC — đoạn 300

The estimated duration of the full training course is five days.

### Nguồn gốc: Introduction to the PCPNC guide — đoạn 307

A primary reference for this course is the WHO document titled Pregnancy, childbirth, postpartum and newborn care: a guide for essential practice (hereinafter the PCPNC guide), which provides evidence-based standards for the provision of high-quality pregnancy and childbirth care.

### Nguồn gốc: Introduction to the PCPNC guide — đoạn 308

Explain the purpose and structure of the manual and how it is used. Answer any questions the participants may have using the information provided below.

### Nguồn gốc: Using the PCPNC guide — đoạn 309

The PCPNC is a guide for clinical decision-making. It facilitates the collection, analysis, classification and use of relevant clinical information by suggesting key questions, observations and/or examinations to perform, and then recommends appropriate evidence-based interventions.

### Nguồn gốc: Using the PCPNC guide — đoạn 310

Help trainees familiarize themselves with the clinical flow chart frameworks in the PCPNC guide. The PCPNC content is presented in a series of coloured flow charts supported by treatment and information charts which give further details of care. These flow charts include guidance for routine care, early detection and

### Nguồn gốc: management of complications, preventive measures, and advice and counselling. The framewor — đoạn 311

based on a syndromic approach whereby the skilled attendant identifies a limited number of key clinical signs and symptoms, enabling him / her to classify the condition according to severity and give appropriate treatment. Flow charts in the PCPNC include the following information: (see also chart below) 1.

### Nguồn gốc: management of complications, preventive measures, and advice and counselling. The framewor — đoạn 312

2. 3. 4. 5.

### Nguồn gốc: Important observations and examinations to conduct — đoạn 313

Possible findings (signs) based on the information gathered from the questions, observations and examinations.

### Nguồn gốc: Ask, check — đoạn 314

record

### Nguồn gốc: Look, listen, — đoạn 315

feel

### Nguồn gốc: Treat and — đoạn 316

advise

### Nguồn gốc: Treat and — đoạn 317

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Treat and — đoạn 318

Use of colour in the flow charts: the severity of a condition is marked in colour: 6. 7. 8.

### Nguồn gốc: Treat and — đoạn 319

Green usually indicates no abnormal condition and therefore normal care is given (including appropriate advice for home care and follow-up). Yellow indicates that there is a problem that can be treated without referral. Red highlights an emergency which requires immediate treatment and, in most cases, also urgent referral to a higher level facility.

### Nguồn gốc: Treat and — đoạn 320

In the flow charts, “treat, advise” means giving the treatment indicated (performing a procedure, prescribing drugs or other treatments, advising on possible side-effects and how to overcome them) and giving advice on other important practices. The treat and advice column is often cross-referenced to other treatment and/or information charts.

### Nguồn gốc: Treatment and information pages — đoạn 321

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Treatment and information pages — đoạn 322

The flow charts are often linked (cross-referenced) to relevant treatment and/or information pages in other parts of the guide. These pages include information which is usually too detailed to include in the flow charts and listed under such headings as “Treatments”, “Advice and counselling”, “Preventive measures” and “Relevant procedures”.

### Nguồn gốc: Treatment and information pages — đoạn 323

The PCPNC guide also contains individual sheets with simplified messages on care during pregnancy and childbirth, to be given to the pregnant woman, her partner and family. These can be accessed for use during client counselling.

### Nguồn gốc: Treatment and information pages — đoạn 324

Remind participants that the PCPNC manual will be referred to and used for the management of identified problems during antenatal care and client counseling. Encourage them to practice and familiarize themselves with the use of the manual

### Nguồn gốc: Treatment and information pages — đoạn 325

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024
