### Nguồn gốc: Preparation — đoạn 330

• Gather training materials in advance

### Nguồn gốc: Preparation — đoạn 331

• Read through the content of this course, including the information in the participant manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Introduction and welcome — đoạn 332

Slide 1: Welcome and introduce participants to the Session.

### Nguồn gốc: Introduction and welcome — đoạn 333

• Ask participants to open Session 1 of their training manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Introduction and welcome — đoạn 334

• Using slide 2, describe the outline of the Session.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Goal and objectives — đoạn 335

• Read and explain the goal and objectives of the Session.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Goal and objectives — đoạn 336

• Provide participants with information on the historical background of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Goal and objectives — đoạn 337

antenatal care using the information provided below.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Goal and objectives — đoạn 338

• Ask participants what they know about the history of ANC in their

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Goal and objectives — đoạn 339

respective counties. How has it evolved over the years?

### Nguồn gốc: Historical background of ANC — đoạn 340

Specialized care for pregnant women through a public health care system started in the United Kingdom of Great Britain and Northern Ireland in the late 1930s. Formalized ANC was primarily initiated to address deaths due to eclampsia which had remained high. Deaths from sepsis, obstructed labour and haemorrhage had all been reduced by the institutionalized deliveries system. The new ANC practice was subsequently adopted by many other countries during the second half of the 20th century. Developing countries also adopted these ANC practices with little or no modifications after colonization and independence.

### Nguồn gốc: Historical background of ANC — đoạn 341

Institutionalized ANC is considered one of the most successful maternal health programmes in terms of its acceptance and patronage. Worldwide, over 70% of pregnant women have had at least one ANC visit (developed (98%), developing (68%)). Indeed, providing access to ANC for women has been easy to implement in many developing settings and has become the platform for the implementation of many other programmes, such as PMTCT and malaria prevention and control.

### Nguồn gốc: Historical background of ANC — đoạn 342

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Historical background of ANC — đoạn 343

In recent years, practitioners have questioned the scientific basis and benefits of many of the practices of “traditional” ANC, including number of visits and risk categorization, as these did not appear to significantly reduce the high maternal mortality ratios in many countries. In fact, some approaches have been associated with increased perinatal mortality.

### Nguồn gốc: Historical background of ANC — đoạn 344

Evidence from recent randomized controlled trials and systematic reviews have shown that a newer model of ANC based on eight high-quality contacts yields optimal pregnancy outcomes for mother and baby. This new evidence-based ANC approach is what is being advocated in this training.

### Nguồn gốc: Defining antenatal care (10 minutes) — đoạn 345

Ask the participants to define antenatal care. Write their responses on the blackboard or on a flip chart. Then present Slides 4 and 5 and compare their answers with the definition provided. Discuss any discrepancies or gaps in participants’ knowledge.

### Nguồn gốc: Defining antenatal care (10 minutes) — đoạn 346

Slides 4 and 5: Definition of antenatal care:

### Nguồn gốc: Defining antenatal care (10 minutes) — đoạn 347

• Enrich the discussions on the definition by providing the following additional

### Nguồn gốc: Defining antenatal care (10 minutes) — đoạn 348

information if necessary:

### Nguồn gốc: Defining antenatal care (10 minutes) — đoạn 349

• Antenatal care is preventive health care provided to a pregnant woman

### Nguồn gốc: Defining antenatal care (10 minutes) — đoạn 350

with the goal of ensuring regular check-ups that allow health providers to treat and prevent potential health problems throughout the course of the pregnancy, while promoting healthy lifestyles that benefit both mother and child.

### Nguồn gốc: Defining antenatal care (10 minutes) — đoạn 351

• Antenatal care should focus on both the medical and psychosocial needs

### Nguồn gốc: Defining antenatal care (10 minutes) — đoạn 352

of each pregnant woman, within the context of the health care system and the sociocultural environment in which the woman lives. Periodic visits to a health care provider during pregnancy are necessary for the following reasons: o early detection and treatment of complications o communication and establishment of a supportive relationship between the woman and the health care provider o development of a birth and emergency plan with the woman o provision of preventive measures, and o provision of advice and counselling based on the woman’s

### Nguồn gốc: Defining antenatal care (10 minutes) — đoạn 353

individual needs and circumstances.

### Nguồn gốc: Why is antenatal care important? (5 minutes) — đoạn 354

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Why is antenatal care important? (5 minutes) — đoạn 355

Ask the participants: why is antenatal care is important? Listen actively to their answers. When there are no more contributions, ask the participants to read in turn (a paragraph at a time), from the participant manual, the section “Why is antenatal care important?” Also refer to and use the information below to reinforce correct answers.

### Nguồn gốc: Why is antenatal care important? (5 minutes) — đoạn 356

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6 and 7: Why ANC is important — đoạn 357

The SDGs have set ambitious health-related targets for mothers, newborns and children under the umbrella of universal health coverage by 2030. ANC is an entry point for the provision of integrated care as it provides a platform for evidence-based interventions capable of affecting maternal, foetal and neonatal health and survival, such as prevention of mother-to-child transmission of HIV, prevention and case management of malaria in pregnancy, prevention of maternal and neonatal tetanus, and prevention of maternal anaemia and malnutrition.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6 and 7: Why ANC is important — đoạn 358

Antenatal care is also a pillar of safe-motherhood and a key component in the continuum of maternal and newborn health care (from pre-pregnancy through pregnancy, childbirth and the postpartum period).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 359

Read out aloud or explain what antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience is:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 360

• The WHO recommendations on ANC for a positive pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 361

experience highlight that a woman’s contact with her provider should be more than a simple visit. It should be an opportunity for comprehensive, high quality care, including medical care, support, and the provision of timely and relevant information throughout pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 362

• Depending on the context of the country, the definition of contact may

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 363

include scheduled ANC visits and information sessions for pregnant women with relevant caretakers at the household, community and health facility levels.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Elements of a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 364

Explain each of the listed elements and what has changed from the FANC approach.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Guiding principles of ANC — đoạn 365

• Present the guiding principles listed on the slide

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Guiding principles of ANC — đoạn 366

• Discuss why these five principles are important for a positive pregnancy experience

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Guiding principles of ANC — đoạn 367

• Refer to the text below in facilitating discussions

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Guiding principles of ANC — đoạn 368

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Guiding principles of ANC — đoạn 369

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Guiding principles of ANC — đoạn 370

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Guiding principles of ANC — đoạn 371

A unique feature of care during pregnancy is that a long-term relationship can be established between the care provider and a client. For many women, pregnancy is what first brings them in contact with the formal health care system. Their perceptions of the quality of care and their relationship with the care provider can have long-term effects on whether they come to a health facility for childbirth and their future use of the health care system.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Guiding principles of ANC — đoạn 372

Negative experiences reported by other ANC clients may also have a negative impact on utilization of ANC services by others.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Definition of quality of care — đoạn 373

Ask a participant to read the definition of quality of care: •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Definition of quality of care — đoạn 374

WHO defines quality of care as “the extent to which health care services provided to individuals and patient populations improve the likelihood of desired health outcomes”. To achieve this, health care must be safe, effective, timely, efficient, equitable and people-centred.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Definition of quality of care — đoạn 375

Explain the two aspects of QOC – provision and experience of care using the information below:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Definition of quality of care — đoạn 376

The health systems approach provides the structure for quality improvement in the two linked dimensions of provision and experience of care: •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Definition of quality of care — đoạn 377

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Definition of quality of care — đoạn 378

Provision of care: includes the use of evidence-based practices for routine and emergency care; information systems in which recordkeeping allows review and auditing; and functioning systems for referral between different levels of care. Experience of care: consists of effective communication with women and their families about the care provided, including their expectations and rights; care with respect and preservation of dignity; and access to the social and emotional support of their choice.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Definition of quality of care — đoạn 379

Other prerequisites for good quality of care include the availability of competent, motivated human resources and physical resources in health facilities.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Definition of quality of care — đoạn 380

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 381

Slides 12 and 13: Characteristics of quality of care Present these two slides which describe the characteristics of quality care – linking them to ANC provision. Give examples to highlight the different characteristics:

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 382

• Safe: delivering health care that minimizes risks and harm to service users,

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 383

including avoiding preventable injuries and reducing medical errors.

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 384

• Effective: providing services based on scientific knowledge and

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 385

evidence-based guidelines.

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 386

• Timely: reducing delays in providing and receiving health care. Emphasize the need to negotiate with client for the best time to attend ANC in

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 387

view of their other activities, including time for specialized services such as ultrasound.

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 388

• Efficient: delivering health care in a manner that maximizes resource use

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 389

and avoids waste.

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 390

• Equitable: delivering health care that does not differ in quality based on

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 391

personal characteristics such as gender, race, ethnicity, geographical location or socioeconomic status.

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 392

• People-centred – providing care that takes into account the preferences and

### Nguồn gốc: Characteristics of quality care — đoạn 393

aspirations of individual service users and the culture of their community. Add aspects of experience of care to this discussion, including health worker attitudes and respectful maternal care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: What is person-centred care? — đoạn 394

Ask two or three participants to describe what person-centred antenatal care means to them. Write their answers on the flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: What is person-centred care? — đoạn 395

• Individualized/ person-centred ANC refers to care that is acceptable to the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: What is person-centred care? — đoạn 396

pregnant woman and her family.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: What is person-centred care? — đoạn 397

• During person-centred care, the pregnant woman’s health and survival,

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: What is person-centred care? — đoạn 398

basic human rights and comforts are given clear priority. The woman’s personal desires and preferences are also regarded as important.

### Nguồn gốc: Individualized care means that women are provided with person-centred care — đoạn 399

that meets their personal needs, medical condition, as well as cultural and socioeconomic circumstances.

### Nguồn gốc: Individualized care depends on good rapport and communication between the — đoạn 400

care provider and the pregnant woman. You may use the additional information provided below to further buttress your points.

### Nguồn gốc: Individualized care depends on good rapport and communication between the — đoạn 401

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 402

• Her culture, beliefs, traditions as well as gender roles and relations are respected.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 403

• Family members are included in the care of the woman as she desires.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 404

• The clinic is organized with the convenience, comfort and needs of the clients in mind.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 405

• The layout of the clinic includes a private place for examination and counselling, ensuring

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 406

that discussion between care giver and pregnant woman cannot be overheard.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 407

• The woman’s resources and capabilities are taken into consideration when health messages

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 408

are given, and recommendations made.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 409

• The woman is informed about her health situation and good health practices. Her consent

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 410

is sought in all decisions relating to her health and care.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 411

• The pregnant woman and her family are supported to become active participants in her

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 412

care, particularly in making decisions.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 413

• She is assisted to overcome problems or challenges that impede her well-being.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 414

Compare your explanations on “person-centred care” to those listed earlier by participants and clarify/correct any differences. In giving feedback, also emphasize the following: • •

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 415

•

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 416

Care providers should make all pregnant women feel welcome at their clinic. The opening hours of clinics providing ANC services should be as convenient as possible for women visiting the clinic. (It has been shown that the number of women seeking antenatal care at health facilities increases proportionally with increases in ANC clinic hours of operation).

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 417

Health care providers should make every effort to keep their appointments with women in order to reduce patient waiting time. However, women who come without an appointment should not be turned away even when there is no emergency.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 418

Good rapport and communication between the care provider and the pregnant woman Individualized care requires that, to the extent possible, the ANC provider establishes a unique relationship with their client, and at each visit, assumes responsibility for all decisions taken in respect of the client’s care.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 419

This implies that the care provider should have all necessary information (including sensitive information) about the woman to enable good decisions about her care. Obtaining such information requires good rapport and communication with the client. To foster a good relationship with the client, the ANC provider must: • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 420

know and appreciate the cultural milieu from which the client originates and must provide care within that cultural context; provide a respectful and positive environment to the family member accompanying the pregnant woman; maintain privacy and confidentiality during all contacts with a pregnant woman; and have good communication skills (which are essential to building rapport and effective care) and communicate in a respectful, individualized and person-centred way.

### Nguồn gốc: Person-centred antenatal care — đoạn 421

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 422

Explain that all women want a positive pregnancy experience from ANC. This means effective social, cultural, emotional and psychological support is provided in a respectful way to improve a woman’s perception of her pregnancy and her care. a positive pregnancy experience.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 423

You may also use the additional information below to enrich the discussions.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 424

Effects of culture on communication with clients: •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 425

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 426

Effective communication makes the woman (and her companion) feel welcome. It also makes it easier to: elicit inputs from the woman about her symptoms or concerns; provide information about her needs; and help her understand her options and make decisions.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 427

Explain that the ANC provider must know and appreciate the cultural milieu from which their clients originate and must provide care within that cultural context. in their locality. Use examples of harmless and harmful cultural practices provided by participants to discuss how these could influence how you communicate and provide antenatal care services and whether women are comfortable using the services or not. For example, talking about genitalia in public may be culturally offensive. Women in such cultures may feel reluctant to discuss issues relating to vaginal discharge/ulcers (STIs).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 428

Effect of clinic set-up/organization on communication with clients: •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 429

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 430

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 431

Highlight the fact that clinic set-up and service organization also affect communications between care providers and clients significantly. Describe the “factory assembly care system” and its disadvantages in this regard. “The factory assembly line” approach of antenatal clinics is one in which several care providers are responsible for the different care components. This usually results in poor-quality care, as none of the care providers along the line is in possession of all the information concerning the client to enable good decisions about the woman’s care. For example, when one provider takes blood pressure, another requests laboratory tests, and another provides counselling and preventive measures, good rapport is unlikely. The client is not managed wholly or comprehensively, certain problems are overlooked, and her needs are therefore never entirely met.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Good communication — đoạn 432

affects the quality of care. Ask them a few checking questions to ensure that they understand what a factory assembly line approach to ANC is, as opposed to an individualized and comprehensive provider–client relationship.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Disease detection and not risk categorization — đoạn 433

during assessments of pregnant women was used to classify pregnant women by risk category to determine their chances of complications and the level of care required. However, evidence has shown that all pregnant women remain at risk of complications. Many women with risk factors may not develop complications, while women without risk factors may develop and die from complications.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Disease detection and not risk categorization — đoạn 434

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Disease detection and not risk categorization — đoạn 435

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Disease detection and not risk categorization — đoạn 436

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Disease detection and not risk categorization — đoạn 437

Explain that risk assessment is good for planning management, but not for predicting outcome. Antenatal care should be provided in a simple form that ensures that women with disease or special health conditions and/or those at risk of developing complications are identified early and referred to a higher level of care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Disease detection and not risk categorization — đoạn 438

The higher levels of care to which they are referred must have the expertise to deal with their specific health care needs.

### Nguồn gốc: Evidence-based practices during antenatal care — đoạn 439

Antenatal care provided by skilled health care providers must include only care practices that are proven to promote the health and survival of mothers and babies.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Evidence-based interventions for ANC — đoạn 440

antenatal care? Write their answers on the flip chart. Then present slide 18.

### Nguồn gốc: Evidence-based interventions in antenatal care for a positive pregnancy — đoạn 441

experience include:

### Nguồn gốc: Evidence-based interventions in antenatal care for a positive pregnancy — đoạn 442

• Assessing a client’s birth preparedness and complication readiness; prevention of

### Nguồn gốc: Evidence-based interventions in antenatal care for a positive pregnancy — đoạn 443

malaria through intermittent preventive treatment in pregnancy (IPTp); prevention of mother-to-child transmission of HIV, HBV and syphilis: prevention of anaemia through daily oral iron and folic supplementation; early ultrasound scan to estimate gestational age and monitor foetal development; and involvement of the client’s partner or support person in the process of ANC and in preparation for childbirth.

### Nguồn gốc: Using the PCPNC guide to provide evidence-based care — đoạn 444

•

### Nguồn gốc: Using the PCPNC guide to provide evidence-based care — đoạn 445

•

### Nguồn gốc: Using the PCPNC guide to provide evidence-based care — đoạn 446

The WHO document titled Pregnancy, childbirth, postpartum and newborn care: a guide for essential practice (herein called the “PCPNC guide”) provides evidence-based standards for the provision of high-quality pregnancy and childbirth care. Inform and remind participants that it is the main reference document for evidence-based ANC practices.

### Nguồn gốc: Using the PCPNC guide to provide evidence-based care — đoạn 447

During training sessions on management of identified antenatal problems, you will refer to and facilitate the use of the PCPNC guide and its simplified messages for client care and counselling.

### Nguồn gốc: Using the PCPNC guide to provide evidence-based care — đoạn 448

Help participants familiarize themselves with the PCPNC manual by tasking them to each review the organization and contents of the manual for 15 minutes and then answer the following questions:

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Which sections will provide information on evidence-based practices for management of a — đoạn 449

pregnancy?

### Nguồn gốc: 3. On which page can evidence-based counselling components for developing a birth and emer — đoạn 450

Birth preparedness and complication readiness planning: a unique evidence-based antenatal intervention Most complications leading to maternal and newborn death and ill-health occur around the time of childbirth. These complications are worsened by delays in seeking or receiving care. Antenatal care emphasizes the importance of preparing well for childbirth and possible emergencies as an essential life-saving intervention for women.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. On which page can evidence-based counselling components for developing a birth and emer — đoạn 451

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Birth preparedness and complication readiness — đoạn 452

The care provider assists the client and her family to prepare for childbirth by: identifying the place for childbirth (a facility with a skilled provider is recommended); and planning how to get there and what items to have in readiness.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Birth preparedness and complication readiness — đoạn 453

• The care provider assists the client and her family to prepare for childbirth

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Birth preparedness and complication readiness — đoạn 454

by: identifying the place for childbirth (a facility with a skilled provider is recommended ); and planning how to get there and what items to have in readiness.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Birth preparedness and complication readiness — đoạn 455

• The care provider prepares their client for emergencies by educating the client

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Birth preparedness and complication readiness — đoạn 456

and her family to recognize danger signs and symptoms and to seek immediate help.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Birth preparedness and complication readiness — đoạn 457

• The care provider provides advice on: the facility to visit for emergency care;

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Birth preparedness and complication readiness — đoạn 458

how to get there; the contact address and telephone number of the facility; and the need to identify a blood donor in case a transfusion is needed.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Birth preparedness and complication readiness — đoạn 459

• These plans are documented in the woman’s birth and emergency plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Scheduling antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 460

(5 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Schedule of routine ANC contacts — đoạn 461

• Ask participants what the recommended schedule for routine antenatal care is.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Schedule of routine ANC contacts — đoạn 462

• For a positive pregnancy experience, it is recommended that all healthy pregnant

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Schedule of routine ANC contacts — đoạn 463

women have a minimum of eight scheduled comprehensive antenatal contacts during pregnancy. Refer them also to Figure 1.1 in their manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Schedule of routine ANC contacts — đoạn 464

• The first contact between the pregnant woman and the ANC care provider

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Schedule of routine ANC contacts — đoạn 465

should occur in the first trimester.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Schedule of routine ANC contacts — đoạn 466

• For women who come early in their pregnancy, early initiation of ANC facilitates

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Schedule of routine ANC contacts — đoạn 467

confirmation of the pregnancy and evaluation of whether the woman is in good health without complications – among other key interventions. Figure 1.1: Schedule of routine antenatal contacts for a normal, healthy woman

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 468

1st contact

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 469

As soon as the woman suspects she is pregnant; during the first trimester (preferably 8–12 weeks)

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 470

2nd scheduled contact

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 471

20 weeks

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 472

3rd scheduled contact

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 473

26 weeks

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 474

4th scheduled contact

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 475

30 weeks

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 476

5th scheduled contact

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 477

34 weeks

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 478

6th scheduled contact

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 479

36 weeks

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 480

7th scheduled contact

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 481

38 weeks

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 482

8th scheduled contact

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 483

40 weeks

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 484

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 485

Explain that the above schedule is recommended for uncomplicated pregnancy. More frequent contacts or different schedules are needed if the woman has any problems or any complications that require treatment or additional monitoring or support, such as malaria or HIV.

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 486

Discuss challenges in implementing the eight ANC visit schedule using the information below: In many low-resource settings, some clients come for their first contact during their second trimester of pregnancy. In this case, the contact schedule must be modified to ensure that the client receives all the essential components of care before childbirth.

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 487

•

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 488

For example, a client who first contacts the clinic in her sixth month of gestation (at 25 weeks) should be assessed as comprehensively as possible at this initial visit. If she is found to be healthy, the standard contact schedule should be modified to ensure that she can receive all the essential care interventions before childbirth: two doses of tetanus toxoid and several doses of SP for intermittent preventive treatment for malaria during pregnancy (IPTp). During the last contact, the woman should be asked to return if she does not give birth by 41 weeks of gestation.

### Nguồn gốc: Recommended schedule — đoạn 489

It is important to ensure that the quality of ANC is assured at each visit.

### Nguồn gốc: In this exercise, participants will assess whether the care — đoạn 490

provided by the ANC provider demonstrated the principles of antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Case study — đoạn 491

• Using Slide 21, explain the purpose and instructions for the case study

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Case study — đoạn 492

activity.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Case study — đoạn 493

• Refer participants to the relevant section in their manuals.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Case study — đoạn 494

• Present or ask a participant to read out the case (see below).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Case study — đoạn 495

• When all participants have completed the assignment, discuss the answers

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Case study — đoạn 496

using the answer guide provided below.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Case study — đoạn 497

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Case study: Mrs Q — đoạn 498

Mrs Q is 17 years old. She has missed her menses for the past three months and thinks she is pregnant. This is her first pregnancy. She is well today and has no health complaints except early morning sickness. She and her mother in law live about 2 km from the clinic. She walked here today because she has no money for transportation. Her husband, who recently lost his job as a labourer, has travelled to the big city to look for a new job, leaving her with his mother. Her mother in law, who is a trader, has been giving her a bit of money every now and then to help out.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study: Mrs Q — đoạn 499

When the midwife examined her, she found that she looked healthy. Her uterus was just felt abdominally. The midwife’s impression is that Mrs Q is about 12 weeks pregnant. She performed the following rapid tests and obtained the following results: urine dipstick = Normal; HIV rapid test = Negative; and syphilis rapid test = Negative.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study: Mrs Q — đoạn 500

The midwife also asked her to do some routine tests at the hospital laboratory. She obtained the following findings: Haemoglobin = 9 g/dl; sickling = negative; blood group = O RhD positive; stool exam = hookworm ova present; G6PD screen = negative The midwife also informed Mrs. Q that she was healthy and would only require eight contacts during this pregnancy. Mrs. Q then received a routine supply of iron and folate tablets (one tablet each daily). The midwife then asked her to come for her next visit in one month, when she would be at 20 weeks’ gestation, along with her husband.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study: Mrs Q — đoạn 501

Now think about the answer to the following question. Question: Were all the principles of antenatal care listed in the chart demonstrated during this visit? In the answer sheet provided below, tick yes or no, and write what the midwife did or did not do that supports your answer. When everyone has completed the assignment, the class will discuss the answers.

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback discussions — đoạn 502

When participants have completed the assignment, discuss the answers as follows:

basis and principles for the correct answers.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. If there are differences, discuss the answers; clarify and ensure that participants und — đoạn 504

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Quality of care — đoạn 505

X

### Nguồn gốc: The visit missed several tasks that the midwife — đoạn 506

should have performed, so this was not a good quality visit. She did not undertake a comprehensive assessment, including taking an appropriate history at the first visit; she did not provide counselling; she did not explain the eightcontact schedule.

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Individualized, personcentred care — đoạn 507

X

### Nguồn gốc: She did not give treatment for anaemia (only gave — đoạn 508

insuﬃcient iron supplementation), nor did she provide counsel on nutrition and self-care; she did not ask if the husband would be able to come on her next visit, or if he would be in town.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Disease detection and not — đoạn 509

risk categorization

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Disease detection and not — đoạn 510

X

### Nguồn gốc: She did not detect the woman’s anaemia; she did — đoạn 511

not seem to have noticed that she had hookworm, or perhaps she just did not mention it until she could give mebendazole in the second trimester.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Evidence-based practices — đoạn 512

for antenatal care

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Evidence-based practices — đoạn 513

X

### Nguồn gốc: The visit did not cover assessing the client’s — đoạn 514

emergency preparedness and complication readiness. It is early in the pregnancy, but the provider should have at least taught her emergency signs in pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Birth preparedness and — đoạn 515

complication readiness

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Birth preparedness and — đoạn 516

X

### Nguồn gốc: This was not addressed at all, except perhaps in — đoạn 517

trying to get the husband to come in. However, it seems that it may be the mother-in-law who should be involved if the husband will be away.

messages.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Key messages — đoạn 520

• Link each key message to the session objectives.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Key messages — đoạn 522

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 523

•

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 524

Antenatal care (ANC) seeks to improve quality of care and the well-being of pregnant women.

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 525

•

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 526

For a normal healthy pregnancy, a minimum of eight comprehensive contacts are recommended at around <12, 20, 26, 30, 34, 36, 38 and 40 weeks.

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 527

•

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 528

ANC emphasizes the following principles: quality of care, individualized care, disease detection and not risk categorization, evidence-based practices, and birth preparedness and complication readiness.

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 529

•

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 530

A quality ANC contact is one which is comprehensive and individualized, meaning that the care provider completes all the recommended assessments, detects and treats any complications, maintains a supportive relationship with the woman, develops or updates the birth and emergency plan, provides all appropriate preventive measures, and provides advice and counselling to meet the woman’s own medical and psychosocial needs.

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 531

•

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 532

Person-centred care takes into consideration the cultural milieu of the client and uses good communication skills. Clinic schedules and organization are designed to accommodate the comfort and convenience of clients and respect their rights to privacy and confidentiality.

### Nguồn gốc: Summary and conclusions — đoạn 533

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4 Slides 4–7: Client-friendly ANC — đoạn 563

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4 Slides 4–7: Client-friendly ANC — đoạn 564

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4 Slides 4–7: Client-friendly ANC — đoạn 565

Referring to slide 7, remind participants of other key services that must be linked to antenatal clinic services. Highlight how the negative effects of poor quality of care at these other services can affect client experience and discuss how the quality of these other key requirements can be enhanced and linked to ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4 Slides 4–7: Client-friendly ANC — đoạn 566

their respective settings and how this impacts on the quality of the services they provide to their clients. Encourage trainees to brainstorm ways to improve upon clinic set-ups and promote client-friendly antenatal services.

### Nguồn gốc: Workplace and administrative procedures (10 minutes) — đoạn 567

Slides 8, 9 and 10: Workplace and administrative procedures Explain that the quality of ANC services hinges on application of workplace and administrative procedures.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Discuss the International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk — đoạn 568

Substitutes at the health facility and its rationale.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Discuss the International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk — đoạn 569

• The WHO International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk Substitutes

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Discuss the International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk — đoạn 570

was adopted in 1981 by the World Health Assembly to promote safe and adequate nutrition for infants, through the protection and promotion of breastfeeding, and by ensuring the proper use of breast-milk substitutes, when necessary.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Discuss the International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk — đoạn 571

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Discuss the International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk — đoạn 572

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Discuss the International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk — đoạn 573

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Discuss the International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk — đoạn 574

One of the main principles of the Code is that health care facilities should not be used for the purpose of promoting breast-milk substitutes, feeding bottles or teats. Subsequently, World Health Assembly resolutions have clarified the Code and addressed some related issues.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Discuss the International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk — đoạn 575

Discuss adherence to the Code in their health facilities.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Discuss the International Code of Marketing of Breast-milk — đoạn 576

Ensure that the health facility is tobacco-free.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Standard IPC practices for ANC — đoạn 577

To protect the woman, her baby and themselves from infection with bacteria and viruses, including HIV and HBV, health care providers must observe standard (also called universal) precautions when providing antenatal care as recommended in the PCPNC guide (section A4).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Standard IPC practices for ANC — đoạn 578

• Discuss how care providers can promote adherence to hand washing for

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Standard IPC practices for ANC — đoạn 579

themselves and clients within their clinic settings.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Standard IPC practices for ANC — đoạn 580

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Documentation and data management for effective ANC service delivery — đoạn 581

•

### Nguồn gốc: Documentation and data management for effective ANC service delivery — đoạn 582

Antenatal care provides a unique setting where the continuum of care is initiated during pregnancy and continued long after the birth of the baby. Over the nine-month course of her pregnancy, a client makes several visits to the clinic where the progress of her pregnancy and her health status are checked and recorded.

### Nguồn gốc: Documentation and data management for effective ANC service delivery — đoạn 583

•

### Nguồn gốc: Documentation and data management for effective ANC service delivery — đoạn 584

The recorded findings at each visit provide the basis for judging a client’s well-being at the initial and subsequent visits; and for planning her overall care. Clinical documentation is therefore a vital and very important component of antenatal care. It is also critical for the management, monitoring and evaluation of services provided and their quality.

### Nguồn gốc: Documentation and data management for effective ANC service delivery — đoạn 585

•

### Nguồn gốc: Documentation and data management for effective ANC service delivery — đoạn 586

Emphasize that irrespective of the type of document in use, all notes or entries must be timely, complete, legible and fully recorded. All clinical records and other documentation must be well maintained, stored and/or filed away properly.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Documentation and data management — đoạn 587

• Ask participants to list the various records utilized in the antenatal clinic

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Documentation and data management — đoạn 588

during care provision and their uses.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Documentation and data management — đoạn 589

• Note their answers on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Documentation and data management — đoạn 590

• Compare their answers to those on slide 12 and fill in any gaps.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Documentation and data management — đoạn 591

• Describe the two types of client clinical records: home-based, an facility-based.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Documentation and data management — đoạn 592

• Using the information provided below, discuss the advantages and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Documentation and data management — đoạn 593

disadvantages of each. WHO recommends home-based client records for maternal and child care.

### Nguồn gốc: Client clinical records — đoạn 594

Care provided must be recorded on a clinical record (electronic or paper-based) at the time the woman is seen, including findings, treatments, reasons for referral (if necessary), and follow-up

### Nguồn gốc: Advantages — đoạn 595

• • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Available to client irrespective of clinic location or health — đoạn 596

provider visited

### Nguồn gốc: Improve client’s ownership and involvement in her own care — đoạn 597

Can be a tool for behaviour change communication and patientcentred care

### Nguồn gốc: Improve communication between health providers and women — đoạn 598

/caregivers.

### Nguồn gốc: Disadvantages — đoạn 599

•

### Nguồn gốc: Disadvantages — đoạn 600

•

### Nguồn gốc: Disadvantages — đoạn 601

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Documents may be — đoạn 602

kept more securely

### Nguồn gốc: Full clinical records — đoạn 603

are available for later review and/or analysis

### Nguồn gốc: Disadvantages — đoạn 604

Records may be less securely kept with high risks of breach of confidentiality and poor maintenance (care should be taken when recording confidential information into home-based records if the client does not want this). Client clinical details may not be readily available for later review and data analysis.

### Nguồn gốc: Disadvantages — đoạn 605

•

### Nguồn gốc: Disadvantages — đoạn 606

•

### Nguồn gốc: Retrieval of records — đoạn 607

may be a challenge in settings with poor storage and filing.

### Nguồn gốc: Time spent on record — đoạn 608

retrieval

### Nguồn gốc: Time spent on record — đoạn 609

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Clinic registers and log books — đoạn 610

These documents provide a log of clinic use by clients and are important for the recording of key care components received by clients at each visit. Where home-based client ANC books are used, they often become the only other available source of client clinical care information at each visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Clinic registers and log books — đoạn 611

Analysis of data from these registers allows evaluation of the coverage and quality of antenatal care services. They may be used to calculate the following monitoring and evaluation indicators:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: ANC indicators — đoạn 612

• Ask participants if they know of these indicators and use them in their

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: ANC indicators — đoạn 613

settings.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: ANC indicators — đoạn 614

• Discuss how each of these indicators may be calculated (nominators and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: ANC indicators — đoạn 615

denominators) and their relevance and use in monitoring coverage and quality of care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: ANC indicators — đoạn 616

• Refer participants to the relevant section in their manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: ANC indicators — đoạn 617

• Ask some participants to each read out a segment of the table dealing with

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: ANC indicators — đoạn 618

the respective indicators.

### Nguồn gốc: Group antenatal care — đoạn 619

Slides 14 and 15: Group antenatal care – organizational considerations

### Nguồn gốc: Group antenatal care — đoạn 620

• Conventionally, ANC service provision is organized in a manner that offers

### Nguồn gốc: Group antenatal care — đoạn 621

the pregnant woman an opportunity for a one-on-one consultation with her health care provider in most settings.

### Nguồn gốc: Group antenatal care — đoạn 622

• An alternate and newer approach to care known as group ANC (GANC) is

### Nguồn gốc: Group antenatal care — đoạn 623

practiced in some settings and has been shown to have some benefits.

### Nguồn gốc: Group antenatal care — đoạn 624

• Ask participants if they have heard of group ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: Group antenatal care — đoạn 625

• Use the information provided on slides 14 and 15 to describe the aims,

### Nguồn gốc: Group antenatal care — đoạn 626

components, benefits and key considerations in the organization of group ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: Group antenatal care — đoạn 627

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Establishing links for the continuum of care (10 minutes) — đoạn 628

Quality ANC is provided in a continuum linking the family and community with the health facility. Support from communities and other health care providers can enhance the care provided to pregnant women. Ask them to describe how they establish links with other health care providers, individuals within the community and community groups. List their responses on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Establishing links for the continuum of care (10 minutes) — đoạn 629

Present and review the information on slides 16 and 17, comparing it with the information provided by the participants.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Establishing links for a continuum of care — đoạn 630

• Some antenatal care interventions can be offered through community-based

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Establishing links for a continuum of care — đoạn 631

care providers in a task sharing approach.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Establishing links for a continuum of care — đoạn 632

• WHO recommends that the promotion of health-related behaviour change

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Establishing links for a continuum of care — đoạn 633

education and distribution of preventive interventions can be shifted where necessary to a broad range of cadres, including community-based lay health workers and auxiliary nurses (refer participants to the relevant appendix).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Establishing links for a continuum of care — đoạn 634

• Present the information on slide 16 to highlight important considerations

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Establishing links for a continuum of care — đoạn 635

that must be made during task shifting of these ANC components.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Establishing links for a continuum of care — đoạn 636

• Use the information on the slide to discuss how the other key stakeholders

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Establishing links for a continuum of care — đoạn 637

within the community can partner with health care providers in providing support to pregnant women clients to achieve a positive pregnancy experience.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Brainstorming activity — đoạn 638

• Describe the scenario of a pregnant woman visiting an antenatal clinic and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Brainstorming activity — đoạn 639

then ask each participant in the class to give one answer to the question:

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 640

• Each participant should mention one characteristic of an antenatal

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 641

clinic set-up and service that promotes a positive antenatal experience for the client.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 642

• Compare their answers to the list given in the table below and fill in any gaps.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 643

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 644

A positive antenatal clinic visit means: •

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 645

Clinic days are scheduled to ensure clients can utilize services conveniently.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 646

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 647

A quick check is performed on all women attending the antenatal clinic.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 648

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 649

The waiting area is clean and comfortable and accommodates clients and their support persons.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 650

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 651

Clients are seen promptly and do not have to wait for very long hours.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 652

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 653

There is auditory and visual privacy for antenatal clients.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 654

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 655

There is ready access to toilet facilities.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 656

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 657

There is clean drinking water and access to snacks.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 658

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 659

The consulting cubicles are kept clean, well-ventilated and free from clutter.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 660

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 661

The cubicles have seating for the client and her support person.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 662

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 663

The consulting cubicles are arranged for good eye contact during counselling.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 664

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 665

Clients are able to establish rapport with and see the same care provider at subsequent visits.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 666

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 667

Necessary supplies and medicines are readily available in the cubicle.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 668

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 669

Rapid tests are available in the consulting cubicle.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 670

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 671

The laboratory and pharmacy are easily accessed by antenatal clients.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 672

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 673

Ultrasonography services are available close to the clinic.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 674

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 675

There is an equipped emergency care treatment point nearby.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 676

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 677

The workplace is kept clean and orderly.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 678

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 679

Records of equipment, supplies, drugs and vaccines are kept up to date.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 680

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 681

All pregnant women are checked for anaemia and syphilis at their first antenatal visit.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 682

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 683

All pregnant women are checked for HIV status at their first visit.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 684

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 685

All women are counselled and offered HIV testing.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 686

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 687

A birth and emergency plan is developed with the woman at her first antenatal visit and reviewed at each subsequent visit.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 688

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 689

Preventive measures routinely provided to antenatal women include tetanus immunization, iron folate supplementation, mebendazole, and intermittent preventive treatment for malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 690

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 691

Women can ask questions and get advice about their discomforts and concerns.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 692

•

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 693

Clients are advised and counselled on nutrition and self-care at each visit.

### Nguồn gốc: A positive antenatal clinic visit means? — đoạn 694

•

### Nguồn gốc: Findings and treatments are recorded on a clinical record and a home-based maternal record — đoạn 695

•

### Nguồn gốc: Findings and treatments are recorded on a clinical record and a home-based maternal record — đoạn 696

When clients leave the clinic, they know when they should return for their next visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Findings and treatments are recorded on a clinical record and a home-based maternal record — đoạn 697

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Site visits — đoạn 698

• A key objective of this session is to reinforce the key messages shared in Session 2, to enable

### Nguồn gốc: Site visits — đoạn 699

participants to identify the key requirements for clinic set-ups and practices that promote a positive pregnancy experience at the antenatal clinic. •

### Nguồn gốc: Site visits — đoạn 700

Participants will be tasked to: observe a selected clinic set-up and its practices, identify its strengths and weakness, and make concrete and practical suggestions for transforming it into the ideal setting as discussed earlier in this session.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visits — đoạn 701

•

### Nguồn gốc: Site visits — đoạn 702

A facilitator should visit the selected clinic during the preparation phase of the training to: obtain permission from the facility managers, familiarize himself/herself with the set-up, and inform care providers and managers of the facility of the visit schedule.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visits — đoạn 703

•

### Nguồn gốc: Site visits — đoạn 704

Site visits should take place when the antenatal clinic is open and clients are available and being attended to. This may require organizing the visit at a convenient time period in consultation with the facility managers.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 705

• Present slides 19 and 20 on the objective and guidelines for site visits.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 706

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 707

Provide participants with the tools for site review.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 708

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 709

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 710

Organize participants into groups of 4–6 and assign each group a specific area to observe within the clinic set-up (for example, waiting area, consulting cubicle, documentation, emergency treatment area, and other linked services such as laboratory, pharmacy, infection prevention and access to washrooms). Each group must identify a leader and someone to take notes.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 711

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 712

Assign each group a facilitator to supervise the group activity.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 713

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 714

Allow each group 30–45 minutes to conduct their observations and prepare their reports.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 715

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for site visits — đoạn 716

After the site visits, participants should return to the classroom to report their findings, after which an overall debriefing session will be conducted.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Class activity: Site visit to an ANC clinic — đoạn 717

• Introduce this exercise by saying that it can be a fun exercise but will also

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Class activity: Site visit to an ANC clinic — đoạn 718

cause participants to think critically about their own clinic setting, its set-up, and the way that antenatal care is provided.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Class activity: Site visit to an ANC clinic — đoạn 719

• They will have to compare that to all that they have learned so far about the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Class activity: Site visit to an ANC clinic — đoạn 720

optimal clinic organization for providing focused antenatal care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Class activity: Site visit to an ANC clinic — đoạn 721

• Ask them to be honest with their answers.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Class activity: Site visit to an ANC clinic — đoạn 722

Slide 20: Guidelines for site visits:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Class activity: Site visit to an ANC clinic — đoạn 723

• Using this slide, carefully explain to participants the rule governing site

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Class activity: Site visit to an ANC clinic — đoạn 724

visits, emphasizing the importance of showing the outmost respect for all staff and clients met at the site.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Class activity: Site visit to an ANC clinic — đoạn 725

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 726

Date of visit:

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 727

Name of site:

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 728

Name of participant:

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 729

Site visits are learning trips generally made to observe procedures or practices at a particular site. The general principles guiding such trips include the following: •

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 730

Know the purpose and objectives of the visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 731

•

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 732

Observe all rules and guidelines for the visit. For example, keep only to intended sites of the visit; keep to stated time schedules; obtain permission before operating equipment or managing patients at the site.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 733

•

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 734

Ask questions to clarify issues or observations you are not clear about.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 735

•

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 736

Note your observations on the forms provided for later discussion.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 737

•

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 738

Show outmost respect and be polite to all staff and clients you meet at the site.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 739

•

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 740

Avoid criticizing and making disparaging comments about the staff and/or facility.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 741

•

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 742

Seek the permission of your facilitator and/or tour guide before undertaking any activities outside of the stipulated objectives.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 743

•

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 744

If during the visit to a health facility you encounter a life-threatening emergency, provide first aid if able and immediately hand over the client to facility staff as soon as they are available.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 745

•

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 746

Discuss your comments and observations at the debriefing and feedback session after the trip.

### Nguồn gốc: Site visit guide and checklist — đoạn 747

Goals and objectives of site visit: You will be informed by your facilitator of the specific goals and objectives of this site visit. Note these in the space provided below and use them to guide your observations during this site visit. 1. 2. 3.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Emergency care — đoạn 782

Alternate class activity if a site visit is not possible: group work (20 minutes) •

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Emergency care — đoạn 783

In this exercise, participants will identify any differences between their respective ANC set-ups and the ideal ANC clinic set-up and organization described in Session 2.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Emergency care — đoạn 784

•

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Emergency care — đoạn 785

care is provided. They will have to compare their scenarios to all that they have learned so far about the optimal clinic organization for providing focused antenatal care and make practical suggestions for improving care.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 786

• Divide the class into 4–5 working groups, each comprising 4–6 members.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 787

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 788

Assign each group a facilitator to supervise the group’s activity.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 789

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 790

Instruct each group to identify a chairperson to facilitate group discussion and appoint a member to report the outcome of group work to the class.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 791

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 792

Assign each group an area of the clinic set-up/organization to review, using the five key areas listed on the feedback form as a guide.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 793

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 794

Provide them with the feedback form to use in preparing the group feedback.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 795

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 796

Allow 30 minutes for group work; encourage active participation by all group members.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 797

All groups will present their findings for 10 minutes.

### Nguồn gốc: Debriefing summary and conclusions — đoạn 798

After each group has presented their respective findings and recommendations, briefly summarize the favourable and unfavourable observations made and highlight the key recommendations. Facilitate a brainstorming discussion (5–10 minutes) on major common observations. Ask participants to share experiences on how they have overcome challenges in the following areas:

### Nguồn gốc: Debriefing summary and conclusions — đoạn 799

• improving clinic infrastructure and set-up

### Nguồn gốc: Debriefing summary and conclusions — đoạn 800

• making services more client-friendly

### Nguồn gốc: Debriefing summary and conclusions — đoạn 801

• implementing effective workplace and administrative procedures (such as convenient clinic hours,

### Nguồn gốc: Debriefing summary and conclusions — đoạn 802

infection prevention and cleaning schedules, record keeping, and staff schedules). Create a list of shared experiences/best practices and interventions that have worked and encourage other participants to implement these in their settings, where required and feasible. Also encourage participants to document their good practices so they can be shared more broadly.

### Nguồn gốc: Debriefing summary and conclusions — đoạn 803

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 804

•

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 805

The organization of an antenatal clinic affects the quality of antenatal care and influences a woman’s decision on whether to use the facility. It must provide a client-friendly environment and the space, privacy, equipment, supplies and medicines required to ensure a quality ANC visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 806

•

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 807

Key considerations in organizing a client-friendly antenatal clinic include: o providing auditory and visual privacy o respecting and assuring confidentiality o scheduling clinic days to ensure that clients, their partners and the community are able to utilize services maximally o promoting partner/support person participationo ensuring that clients are able to establish rapport with and see the same care provider at subsequent visits.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 808

•

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 809

The routinely collated summary data on antenatal care can be classified under three domains: (1) services utilization; (2) screening; and (3) interventions. These data are analysed regularly to determine the levels of key monitoring indicators of clinic performance.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 810

•

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 811

Antenatal care providers must work with facility managers and stakeholders within the health care facility, district and community. They should work together to mobilize resources, including community leaders and members, to support a client-friendly antenatal clinic, ensure good quality of care, and encourage pregnant women to come for ANC and childbirth care.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 812

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 823

On completion of this session, participants will be able to:

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 824

1. describe the steps in the provision of ANC

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 825

2. assess the pregnant woman for pregnancy status and screen her for specific problems

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 826

3. assist pregnant women to achieve a healthy pregnancy outcome

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 827

4. list the risk factors (individual, community and health services) related to the antenatal period and identify

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 828

avoidable factors.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 829

5. strengthen care provision to women with special needs: adolescents, survivors of gender-based violence,

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 830

persons with disabilities, and displaced women living in humanitarian settings. Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Quick check — đoạn 831

• Care for a pregnant woman should always begin with a quick check every

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Quick check — đoạn 832

time she is seen.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Quick check — đoạn 833

• The quick check is performed by the first person with responsibility for

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Quick check — đoạn 834

receiving pregnant women seeking care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Quick check — đoạn 835

• A quick check is a brief assessment of the pregnant woman to quickly

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Quick check — đoạn 836

identify any signs that indicate serious problems requiring emergency care and taking immediate appropriate action to minimize delays in providing life-saving care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Quick check — đoạn 837

• Ask participants to name possible danger signs and conditions in pregnancy that

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Quick check — đoạn 838

need emergency care and write the answers on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Refer participants to ‘Quick check’ in PCPNC B2 — đoạn 839

Review the list of conditions and signs indicating the need for emergency care and compare these with the answers provided by participants on the flip chart. Emphasize the following:

### Nguồn gốc: Refer participants to ‘Quick check’ in PCPNC B2 — đoạn 840

• If a woman is very sick, talk to her companion.

### Nguồn gốc: Refer participants to ‘Quick check’ in PCPNC B2 — đoạn 841

• Check for danger signs and conditions indicating the need for emergency care:

### Nguồn gốc: Is she — đoạn 842

o bleeding vaginally o convulsing o unconscious o looking very ill o having severe abdominal pain o in labour o about to deliver o having a fever o draining liquor o having headache/visual disturbances o vomiting severely •

### Nguồn gốc: Is she — đoạn 843

to perform quick check on all women arriving at the ANC clinic might help. o Answer: Enables women who need urgent attention to get it more quickly and

### Nguồn gốc: Is she — đoạn 844

minimizes delays in the provision of essential life-saving care.

co-managed with a haematologist.

### Nguồn gốc: STI epidemics in the subregion with an estimated 1– — đoạn 913

8 million persons infected.

### Nguồn gốc: It presents significant morbidity for pregnant — đoạn 914

mothers and their babies. Syphilis in pregnancy leads to over 300 000 foetal and neonatal deaths each year and places an additional 215 000 infants at increased risk of early death globally.

### Nguồn gốc: The regional target is that at least 95% of pregnant — đoạn 915

women are screened for syphilis and 95% of syphilisseropositive pregnant women are treated with at least one dose of intramuscular benzathine penicillin or another eﬀective regimen.

### Nguồn gốc: Every pregnant woman must be tested for syphilis at — đoạn 916

the first antenatal visit and at each subsequent visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Every pregnant woman must be tested for syphilis at — đoạn 917

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Check for — đoạn 922

hepatitis

### Nguồn gốc: B virus — đoạn 923

(HBV) infection

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 924

reference documents do not contain guidelines for HBV

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 925

Provide the following information:

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 926

• There are over 60 million people chronically infected

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 927

with HBV in Africa, with up to 350 000 infections in children under 5 years (2015).

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 928

• Over 90% of new hepatitis B infections are passed

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 929

from mothers to their children and in early childhood. The risk of chronic infection is higher in these cases.

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 930

• Every pregnant woman should be asked about her

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 931

hepatitis B virus infection status at the first antenatal visit. If this is not known, the woman is counselled and oﬀered HBV testing.

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 932

• Pregnant women who test positive should be given

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 933

prophylactic tenofovir from 28 weeks gestation.

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC — đoạn 934

• All infants should receive their first dose of hepatitis

### Nguồn gốc: B vaccine as soon as possible after birth, preferably — đoạn 935

within 24 hours.

### Nguồn gốc: B vaccine as soon as possible after birth, preferably — đoạn 936

• The infant may also be given hepatitis B

### Nguồn gốc: B vaccine as soon as possible after birth, preferably — đoạn 937

immunoglobulin (HBIG) at the same time if there is a high risk of infection.

### Nguồn gốc: B vaccine as soon as possible after birth, preferably — đoạn 938

• The woman must also be referred and/or evaluated

### Nguồn gốc: B vaccine as soon as possible after birth, preferably — đoạn 939

and followed up for her liver function and well-being.

for other

(C9)

### Nguồn gốc: Refer to PCPNC — đoạn 942

•

### Nguồn gốc: Check for — đoạn 943

tuberculosis (TB)

### Nguồn gốc: Refer to PCPNC — đoạn 944

(C11)

### Nguồn gốc: Assess — đoạn 945

nutritional status

### Nguồn gốc: Refer to slides — đoạn 946

7 and 8

### Nguồn gốc: Sexually transmitted infections (STIs) continue to be — đoạn 947

a highly endemic public health challenge in the African Region.

### Nguồn gốc: Sexually transmitted infections (STIs) continue to be — đoạn 948

• The total number of new cases for the four curable

### Nguồn gốc: STIs (chlamydia, gonorrhoea, trichomoniasis and — đoạn 949

syphilis) in the Region was estimated at 63 million in 2012, representing 18% of the global incidence.

### Nguồn gốc: STIs (chlamydia, gonorrhoea, trichomoniasis and — đoạn 950

• Assessing all pregnant women for abnormal vaginal

### Nguồn gốc: STIs (chlamydia, gonorrhoea, trichomoniasis and — đoạn 951

discharge and ulcers presents an opportunity to identify and treat these curable infections.

### Nguồn gốc: STIs (chlamydia, gonorrhoea, trichomoniasis and — đoạn 952

• The burden of TB infection is high in the sub-Saharan

### Nguồn gốc: African region and often compounded by HIV — đoạn 953

coinfection.

### Nguồn gốc: African region and often compounded by HIV — đoạn 954

• TB infection during pregnancy is associated with

### Nguồn gốc: African region and often compounded by HIV — đoạn 955

poor outcomes for both the mother and her baby, including IUGR, preterm birth and low birth weight for the baby, as well as poor maternal health and death when the disease is not treated.

### Nguồn gốc: African region and often compounded by HIV — đoạn 956

• Maternal care services provide an important

### Nguồn gốc: African region and often compounded by HIV — đoạn 957

platform for screening, detecting and treating TB in pregnant women.

### Nguồn gốc: African region and often compounded by HIV — đoạn 958

• Every pregnant woman attending ANC should be

### Nguồn gốc: African region and often compounded by HIV — đoạn 959

screened for TB using symptom-based screening: (cough lasting >2 weeks, haemoptysis, weight loss, fever, night sweats).

status” and slide 8 on “Recommended weight gain” during pregnancy.
