### Nguồn gốc: Gender-based violence during pregnancy is — đoạn 979

associated with increased risk of trauma, sexually transmitted infections, miscarriage, premature labour, low birth weight, stillbirth and high maternal and infant mortality.

### Nguồn gốc: Women are reluctant to discuss GBV with strangers — đoạn 980

and may not readily divulge this information. A high index of suspicion is required by health care providers to identify this risk.

### Nguồn gốc: Red flags of GBV include reports or observation of — đoạn 981

anxiety, depression or stress, and signs of physical injury.

### Nguồn gốc: The care provider must screen clients by — đoạn 982

establishing good rapport and asking direct questions about GBV.

### Nguồn gốc: Care providers must also be informed about — đoạn 983

psychosocial support and legal services available in their countries for women facing GBV if they are to be able to support and counsel victims.

### Nguồn gốc: Little is known about the nature and extent of — đoạn 984

alcohol and other drug (AOD) use among pregnant women in Africa.

### Nguồn gốc: Alcohol and other substance abuse during — đoạn 985

pregnancy negatively impacts both mother and child.

### Nguồn gốc: The consequences of alcohol abuse during — đoạn 986

pregnancy are classified as foetal alcohol spectrum disorders (FASD).

### Nguồn gốc: Illicit drug use in pregnancy has been associated — đoạn 987

with preterm delivery, low birth weight infants, placental abruption, neonatal abstinence syndrome, etc.

### Nguồn gốc: Routine screening for alcohol, tobacco and — đoạn 988

substance use should be done at each ANC contact and, where appropriate, pregnant women should be counselled and/or referred for treatment and support at detoxification centres.

### Nguồn gốc: Health care providers should ask all pregnant — đoạn 989

women about their alcohol, tobacco and substance use, including exposure to second-hand smoke, as early as possible in the pregnancy and at every ANC visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Health care providers should ask all pregnant — đoạn 990

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

component

gestational diabetes mellitus

### Nguồn gốc: The PCPNC — đoạn 995

guide does not have specific guidance on screening for

### Nguồn gốc: Hyperglycaemia first detected at any time during — đoạn 996

pregnancy should be classified as either gestational diabetes mellitus (GDM) or diabetes mellitus in pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Hyperglycaemia first detected at any time during — đoạn 997

• Women who present with the following: symptoms

### Nguồn gốc: Hyperglycaemia first detected at any time during — đoạn 998

of polyuria, polydipsia, nocturia, a family history of diabetes, past obstetric history of glucose intolerance or diabetes mellitus, stillbirth, and foetal macrosomia, should all be screened for GDM.

### Nguồn gốc: Hyperglycaemia first detected at any time during — đoạn 999

• Screening tests include: urine test for glucosuria,

### Nguồn gốc: Hyperglycaemia first detected at any time during — đoạn 1000

and fasting and/or random blood sugar test

### Nguồn gốc: Hyperglycaemia first detected at any time during — đoạn 1001

•

### Nguồn gốc: Diabetes mellitus in pregnancy should be diagnosed if — đoạn 1002

one or more of the following criteria are met:

### Nguồn gốc: Diabetes mellitus in pregnancy should be diagnosed if — đoạn 1003

• fasting plasma glucose > 7.0 mmol/L (126

### Nguồn gốc: Diabetes mellitus in pregnancy should be diagnosed if — đoạn 1004

mg/dL)

### Nguồn gốc: Diabetes mellitus in pregnancy should be diagnosed if — đoạn 1005

• 2-hour plasma glucose > 11.1 mmol/L (200

### Nguồn gốc: Diabetes mellitus in pregnancy should be diagnosed if — đoạn 1006

mg/dL) following a 75 g oral glucose load

### Nguồn gốc: Diabetes mellitus in pregnancy should be diagnosed if — đoạn 1007

• random plasma glucose > 11.1 mmol/L (200

### Nguồn gốc: Diabetes mellitus in pregnancy should be diagnosed if — đoạn 1008

mg/dL) in the presence of diabetes symptoms.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 7 and 8 — đoạn 1009

• Ask participants to explain why maternal nutritional status is important

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 7 and 8 — đoạn 1010

during pregnancy. Write their answers down on a flip chart and compare those answers to what is in slide 7

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 7 and 8 — đoạn 1011

• Assessing maternal nutritional status includes checking for stunted growth

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 7 and 8 — đoạn 1012

and other important micronutrient deficiencies (such as vitamin A, calcium and iron).

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 7 and 8 — đoạn 1013

• Care providers must take the mother’s height during the first visit, take her

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 7 and 8 — đoạn 1014

weight at all visits, check for vitamin A deficiency (check serum retinol <0.7 mcm / L or ask about night blindness in most recent pregnancy) and enquire about intake of calcium-rich foods.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 7 and 8 — đoạn 1015

• Discuss the recommended daily requirements for vitamin A and

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 7 and 8 — đoạn 1016

highlight the fact that WHO does not recommend routine micronutrient supplementation during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 7 and 8 — đoạn 1017

• Using slide 8, discuss the recommended weight gain during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: A: Respond to observed signs and volunteered problems — đoạn 1018

Most pregnant women experience some problems and discomforts (such as: nausea and vomiting, heartburn, leg cramps, low back and pelvic pain, constipation, varicose veins and oedema). These can be managed conservatively and with readily available home remedies (see the WHO 2016 recommendations on ANC for details). Care providers must recognize serious problems and manage them appropriately in a timely manner.

### Nguồn gốc: A: Respond to observed signs and volunteered problems — đoạn 1019

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 1020

• Refer participants to Appendix 5 in the participant manual – Management of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 1021

common discomforts during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 1022

• Emphasize the importance of differentiating common discomforts from

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 1023

serious and/or life-threatening health conditions requiring medical intervention.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 1024

• Ask participants to read selected examples from the chart to highlight this

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 1025

fact.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Providing preventive measures — đoạn 1026

• The preventive measures to be provided during ANC include: tetanus

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Providing preventive measures — đoạn 1027

toxoid (TT) immunization, iron/folate supplementation, de-worming with mebendazole, intermittent preventive treatment for malaria, and use of insecticide-treated bed nets.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Providing preventive measures — đoạn 1028

• These preventive measures are described in PCPNC C12, F2, F3 and F4.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Providing preventive measures — đoạn 1029

• These interventions will be discussed in detail in Session 6.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1030

• Counselling the client on healthy eating and active living should be done in

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1031

relation to her weight gain relative to pre-pregnancy BMI and detectable nutritional deficiencies which require supplementation (for example, if she is vitamin A deficient or anaemic); or to increase balanced energy and protein intake if she’s not gaining weight adequately.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1032

• The client should also be counselled on rest, avoidance of heavy lifting,

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1033

safer sex, avoidance of non-prescribed medication, the importance of family planning, and on how to care for her newborn, including early and exclusive breastfeeding (refer to PCPNC C13).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1034

• Counselling will be discussed in detail in Session 7.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1035

Ask the class to answer the following questions: •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1036

List local foods that their clients can take to improve their diets and reduce micronutrient deficiencies, including iron, folate, calcium and vitamin A.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1037

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1038

Are there local taboos restricting the diet of pregnant women in the region?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1039

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1040

How can care providers overcome such taboos and improve the diet for their clients?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Advice and counsel on nutrition and self-care — đoạn 1041

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1042

• Emphasize the importance of preparing the client for breastfeeding during

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1043

the antenatal period.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1044

• All pregnant women must have their breast examined to check for

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1045

conditions that can interfere with breastfeeding, such as inverted nipples or other disease conditions.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1046

• Clients must also be counselled on the benefits of breastfeeding not just to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1047

their babies but also to themselves.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1048

Planning for the next visit:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1049

• A client’s ANC visit must end with the care provider advising the client on when to return for her next

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1050

contact. o She should return to the clinic or be seen at community level for her next routine antenatal check. •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1051

An eight-contact schedule is recommended for clients with no existing or anticipated problems. o If, however, an ANC check-up is needed sooner than that, for example, to monitor a treatment or for additional emotional support, the care provider must tell the pregnant woman when to come back for this follow-up.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1052

o As far as possible, the health provider should also call to remind the ANC client to come for such follow-up if she fails to come back.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1053

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1054

A care provider must also emphasize to the pregnant woman that she should come back promptly anytime she is not well or has any danger sign.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1055

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1056

The pregnant woman should be encouraged to bring her partner to at least one antenatal visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1057

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Counselling and preparation for breastfeeding — đoạn 1058

Refer to the PCPNC guide – Advise on routine and follow-up visits (C17).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Develop and update her birth and emergency plan — đoạn 1059

• The woman (with her support person) is helped by her care provider to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Develop and update her birth and emergency plan — đoạn 1060

develop a birth and emergency plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Develop and update her birth and emergency plan — đoạn 1061

• At each subsequent visit, the care provider reviews the plan with the woman

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Develop and update her birth and emergency plan — đoạn 1062

and her support person and updates it according to new findings.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Antenatal protocol chart — đoạn 1063

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Antenatal protocol chart — đoạn 1064

summarized in the antenatal care protocol chart in the participant manual (Figure 3.3).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Antenatal protocol chart — đoạn 1065

• The eight-contact schedule is recommended for healthy clients with no

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Antenatal protocol chart — đoạn 1066

existing or anticipated problems.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Antenatal protocol chart — đoạn 1067

on the protocol by conducting the related class activity below.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Planning client care for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 1070

• Explain that the flow chart summarizes the important steps to take in

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Planning client care for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 1071

planning client care at the first ANC visit and thereafter.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Planning client care for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 1072

• Findings obtained during these assessments at each contact determine

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Planning client care for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 1073

whether the woman needs routine care or additional care, or needs to be referred for special care (see Figure 3.3 below).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Planning client care for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 1074

• When a client has an identified problem, she receives special treatment and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Planning client care for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 1075

care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Planning client care for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 1076

• When the problem is resolved, she may re-join the right side of the diagram

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Planning client care for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 1077

- routine antenatal care

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 1078

• Ask participants to turn to the ANC protocol chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 1079

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 1080

Give participants five minutes to quietly read and study the chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 1081

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 1082

Select a participant each to read aloud the steps for the first visit, and subsequent visits by reading across the chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 1083

•

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 1084

visit goals, history and examination, laboratory test, health promotion, and actions to be taken.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 1085

Check if participants understand the difference between the booking and first scheduled visits. Explain that if the woman comes to you for the first time before 12 weeks gestation, it is considered the booking visit; if she comes for the first time at 12 weeks gestation, it counts as the first scheduled ANC visit. The health care provider should always provide the woman with all the care that is appropriate for the stage of gestation.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 1086

At the end of each contact, the care provider determines when the client should come back next. Her next visit will depend on her health status, the presence of existing or anticipated problems, and the care that she needs. Encourage questions and discussions.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 1087

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Factors associated with poor pregnancy outcomes — đoạn 1815

• There are many factors that can influence the health of a pregnant

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Factors associated with poor pregnancy outcomes — đoạn 1816

woman and her unborn child.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Factors associated with poor pregnancy outcomes — đoạn 1817

• Define the avoidable risk factors. Explain that risk factors include individual

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Factors associated with poor pregnancy outcomes — đoạn 1818

risk factors, community risk factors and even health service-related risk factors.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Factors associated with poor pregnancy outcomes — đoạn 1819

• Ask participants to give examples of risk factors in the three categories that

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Factors associated with poor pregnancy outcomes — đoạn 1820

can influence pregnancy outcomes in their areas and why.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Factors associated with poor pregnancy outcomes — đoạn 1821

• Write down their answers on a flip chart. Compare their answers with the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Factors associated with poor pregnancy outcomes — đoạn 1822

list in the participant manual and fill in the gaps.

### Nguồn gốc: During antenatal care, some women need additional care, counselling and — đoạn 1823

support to ensure optimum pregnancy outcomes.

### Nguồn gốc: Such pregnant women include pregnant teenagers; women with poor — đoạn 1824

partner/family support, physical disability and mental health problems; women experiencing intimate partner abuse; and women in humanitarian crisis situations. Women with special needs are likely to:

### Nguồn gốc: Such pregnant women include pregnant teenagers; women with poor — đoạn 1825

• delay seeking antenatal care

### Nguồn gốc: Such pregnant women include pregnant teenagers; women with poor — đoạn 1826

• not attend ANC

### Nguồn gốc: Such pregnant women include pregnant teenagers; women with poor — đoạn 1827

• not adhere to medications and treatments

### Nguồn gốc: Such pregnant women include pregnant teenagers; women with poor — đoạn 1828

• experience mental health issues

### Nguồn gốc: Such pregnant women include pregnant teenagers; women with poor — đoạn 1829

• be less motivated for self-care such as good nutrition.

### Nguồn gốc: Such pregnant women include pregnant teenagers; women with poor — đoạn 1830

• have generally poor maternal health and/or foetal growth.

### Nguồn gốc: Such pregnant women include pregnant teenagers; women with poor — đoạn 1831

Explain that women with special needs require emotional support from the ANC provider and other care providers and support or community groups. Then ask participants to read aloud the bulleted list in their manual on how to give emotional support

### Nguồn gốc: Other sources of support — đoạn 1832

groups in their areas to whom they can refer women for additional support The care provider needs to link ANC clients with community-based and other support services available. Emphasize that it is important to keep an up-to-date list of support services available to pregnant women in your area and explore additional alternatives for support through:

### Nguồn gốc: Other sources of support — đoạn 1833

• community groups, women’s groups and community leaders

### Nguồn gốc: Other sources of support — đoạn 1834

• peer support groups

### Nguồn gốc: Other sources of support — đoạn 1835

• other health service providers

### Nguồn gốc: Other sources of support — đoạn 1836

• community counsellors

### Nguồn gốc: Other sources of support — đoạn 1837

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 1890

•

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 1891

To summarize, ask participants to read out the key messages on slides 20 to 22. (See text below). Allow clarification questions from participants. (5 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 1892

•

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 1893

When a pregnant woman visits the clinic, her care should always begin with a “Quick check” to quickly identify any serious problems and minimize delays.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 1894

•

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 1895

A routine ANC contact should include these steps: (a) Assess maternal and foetal status and plan care (b) Check for pre-eclampsia/eclampsia (c) Test for anaemia (d) Test for syphilis (e) Test for HIV status (f) Develop or update the woman’s birth and emergency plan (g) Respond to observed signs and volunteered problems (h) Provide preventive measures (TT, IPTp-SP, ITN, iron and folate, mebendazole) (i) Advise and counsel on nutrition and self-care (j) Advise her when to return for the next ANC follow-up.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 1896

•

### Nguồn gốc: The ANC checklist describes the standard procedures for providers to — đoạn 1897

assess a client during her pregnancy. Findings obtained during these assessments will determine whether the woman is eligible for eight routine ANC contacts, needs additional care, or should be referred for special care.

### Nguồn gốc: The ANC checklist describes the standard procedures for providers to — đoạn 1898

•

community and health systems challenges.

•

### Nguồn gốc: Pregnant adolescents and pregnant women with disabilities, women who — đoạn 1901

experience poor partner/family support or intimate partner violence, and women in humanitarian settings have special needs requiring special support.

### Nguồn gốc: Pregnant adolescents and pregnant women with disabilities, women who — đoạn 1902

outline.

To enable participants to identify and manage the main problems that may prevent a healthy pregnancy outcome.

### Nguồn gốc: Specific objectives — đoạn 1915

On completion of this session, participants will be able to:

### Nguồn gốc: Specific objectives — đoạn 1916

1. describe and apply the clinical decision-making steps and benefits;

### Nguồn gốc: Specific objectives — đoạn 1917

2. use the PCPNC manual;

### Nguồn gốc: Specific objectives — đoạn 1918

3. use the PCPNC Quick check to rapidly assess each pregnant woman and

### Nguồn gốc: Specific objectives — đoạn 1919

quickly identify any danger signs; and

### Nguồn gốc: Specific objectives — đoạn 1920

4. use the RAM charts to determine the emergency treatments to provide to

the pregnant woman with danger signs.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Definition of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1923

• Clinical decision-making (CDM) is a purposeful and organized thinking

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Definition of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1924

process that a care provider follows to assess a client, make a diagnosis, plan and begin appropriate care, and evaluate the effectiveness of that care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Definition of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1925

• It links assessment, care and evaluation of the care through a series of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Definition of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1926

logical steps.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Definition of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1927

• Through clinical decision-making, the ANC provider identifies

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Definition of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1928

antenatal problems and makes a decision on the best action based on the information gathered during assessment of the pregnancy and well-being of the pregnant woman.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Steps in routine ANC — đoạn 1929

• Remind participants of the ANC steps discussed in Session 3.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Steps in routine ANC — đoạn 1930

• Highlight Step A: Clinical assessment which requires gathering a lot of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Steps in routine ANC — đoạn 1931

information on the patient’s health and pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Steps in routine ANC — đoạn 1932

• These are analysed and used in deciding on the client’s care plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Clinical decision-making steps — đoạn 1933

decision-making steps. For each step, ask participants to share their implementation experiences in their respective settings (10 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Client history, physical exam, laboratory investigations, and any — đoạn 1934

information previous records

### Nguồn gốc: Identify problem(s) or make a diagnosis — đoạn 1935

information

### Nguồn gốc: Develop a — đoạn 1936

• An individualized plan to meet all the needs of the woman (her

### Nguồn gốc: Develop a — đoạn 1937

care plan problems, risks, emotional needs, cultural beliefs, socioeconomic status).

### Nguồn gốc: Develop a — đoạn 1938

• Some women may require special care plans or urgent referral to

### Nguồn gốc: Develop a — đoạn 1939

another facility.

### Nguồn gốc: Develop a — đoạn 1940

• Every plan must be developed with input from the woman

### Nguồn gốc: Implement — đoạn 1941

• Give treatments and provide preventive measures; perform

### Nguồn gốc: Implement — đoạn 1942

care plan additional laboratory investigations if needed; counsel on nutrition and self-care; and provide counsel on specific needs or problems.

### Nguồn gốc: Implement — đoạn 1943

• Record findings and actions taken.

### Nguồn gốc: Implement — đoạn 1944

• Give return appointment; refer if necessary.

### Nguồn gốc: Implement — đoạn 1945

• On next visit, evaluate improvements or change in condition

### Nguồn gốc: Evaluate the — đoạn 1946

care plan through history, examination or repeat lab tests.

### Nguồn gốc: Evaluate the — đoạn 1947

• Was care plan implemented as planned? Was it eﬀective?

### Nguồn gốc: Evaluate the — đoạn 1948

• Does it need change or modification?

### Nguồn gốc: Evaluate the — đoạn 1949

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Evaluate the — đoạn 1950

To further deepen the understanding and use of the clinical decision-making steps, ask participants to identify the problem-solving steps undertaken by Fatima’s mother in the case story below.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Instructions for class activity 1 — đoạn 1951

In the following text under the heading “Fatima’s abdominal pain”, circle the sentence(s) that show each of the steps outlined below. Label them with the number of the step that they illustrate.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Instructions for class activity 1 — đoạn 1952

• Step 1 Interviews and examines

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Instructions for class activity 1 — đoạn 1953

• Step 2 Determines what the problem is

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Instructions for class activity 1 — đoạn 1954

• Step 3 Decides what to do

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Instructions for class activity 1 — đoạn 1955

• Step 4 Carries out her plan

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Instructions for class activity 1 — đoạn 1956

• Step 5 Follows up to check if her actions were effective

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback on Fatima’s story — đoạn 1957

• When all participants have completed the exercise, present slide 9 to

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback on Fatima’s story — đoạn 1958

describe the five key steps in the decision-making cycle that Fatima’s mother used.

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback on Fatima’s story — đoạn 1959

• Map out the five key steps in the story using the chart below as a guide.

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback on Fatima’s story — đoạn 1960

• Discuss these answers to ensure that participants have understood the

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback on Fatima’s story — đoạn 1961

principal steps.

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback on Fatima’s story — đoạn 1962

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Newly married twenty-year-old Fatima comes back to see her — đoạn 1963

mother in her house. She is holding her lower abdomen and wincing. Her mother is very concerned and rushes to her and asks, “What is wrong Fatima?”

### Nguồn gốc: Newly married twenty-year-old Fatima comes back to see her — đoạn 1964

She answers, “My menses have not come for two months and I am worried. Since this morning, I have noticed some slight cramping on and oﬀ” Is the pain severe and are you bleeding? Her mother anxiously asks. No, she answers. Mama, I am hungry too, she adds. I have not eaten since waking up this morning, because I am nauseous all the time and vomited this morning.

### Nguồn gốc: Newly married twenty-year-old Fatima comes back to see her — đoạn 1965

Come and lie down my daughter, she smiled and clapped her hands. Thanks be to God… you are pregnant, she announces. I will make you something nice to eat now.

### Nguồn gốc: Newly married twenty-year-old Fatima comes back to see her — đoạn 1966

Mother helps Fatima to the mat and settles her down. She quickly brings her a pot of delicious local porridge and encourages her to drink it slowly. Later, she brings some soup and Agidi, which Fatima also enjoys.

### Nguồn gốc: Newly married twenty-year-old Fatima comes back to see her — đoạn 1967

Are you feeling better now? Mother asks Fatima two hours later. Fatima reports back that she is better and that the pain is gone. Mother explains to her that she thinks she is pregnant and that many pregnant women experience nausea and vomiting in the early months. She teaches her about foods she should eat and tells her to rest when she goes back to her home.

### Nguồn gốc: Newly married twenty-year-old Fatima comes back to see her — đoạn 1968

If the pain should come back, and especially if there is bleeding, Fatima should inform Salifu her husband, to come back and call her so she can accompany Fatima to the clinic, she advises, as she sees her oﬀ.

### Nguồn gốc: Newly married twenty-year-old Fatima comes back to see her — đoạn 1969

Mother did not hear from Fatima or her husband for one week , so she paid them a visit in their home the next Saturday. To her relief, Fatima was well and had been following her dietary advice.

### Nguồn gốc: Newly married twenty-year-old Fatima comes back to see her — đoạn 1970

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Rationale for following CDM steps in ANC — đoạn 1971

• Explain how the care of pregnant women provides a unique opportunity to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Rationale for following CDM steps in ANC — đoạn 1972

use CDM steps repeatedly.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Rationale for following CDM steps in ANC — đoạn 1973

• Many clinical decisions are required from the first to the last contact as the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Rationale for following CDM steps in ANC — đoạn 1974

clinical situation changes and/or different needs arise.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: The five major steps of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1975

The five steps of the clinical decision-making process form a continuous cycle with each step feeding into the next step. During care for a pregnant woman, the cycle is repeated at each visit. Using clinical decision-making steps helps the ANC provider to:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: The five major steps of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1976

1. collect information in an organized way, therebysaving time and resources;

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: The five major steps of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1977

2. interpret information so that problems or needs are identified correctly, and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: The five major steps of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1978

none are overlooked;

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: The five major steps of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1979

3. give evidence-based care, avoiding unnecessary, inappropriate or excessive

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: The five major steps of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1980

treatments; and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: The five major steps of clinical decision-making — đoạn 1981

4. evaluate the effectiveness of the care provided.

### Nguồn gốc: Using the PCPNC manual to strengthen clinical decision- making during — đoạn 1982

care for the pregnant woman Slides 12 and 13: Using the PCPNC manual for decision-making •

### Nguồn gốc: Using the PCPNC manual to strengthen clinical decision- making during — đoạn 1983

•

### Nguồn gốc: Using the PCPNC manual to strengthen clinical decision- making during — đoạn 1984

Using slides 12 and 13, explain that the PCPNC manual helps ANC providers and other care providers to use clinical decision-making skills to identify and manage pregnancy complications.

### Nguồn gốc: It facilitates the collection of relevant information, the classification of — đoạn 1985

problems, and the identification of evidence-based management.

### Nguồn gốc: It facilitates the collection of relevant information, the classification of — đoạn 1986

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Quick check and rapid assessment and management (RAM) — đoạn 1987

(10 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Quick check and RAM — đoạn 1988

• Antenatal care clinics in many low-resource settings are visited by many

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Quick check and RAM — đoạn 1989

clients daily and are often busy with long waiting times.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Quick check and RAM — đoạn 1990

• Although most pregnant women visiting these clinics are generally healthy,

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Quick check and RAM — đoạn 1991

some may present with life threatening complications requiring prompt and quick attention.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Quick check and RAM — đoạn 1992

• These sick women must be identified and provided with life-saving care as

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Quick check and RAM — đoạn 1993

soon as they arrive at the clinic. to their settings? Referring to slide 14, explain Quick check and RAM.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 1994

• Refer participants to the “Introduction” section of the PCPNC manual –

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 1995

“Structure and presentation”. Select participants to read out this page whilst emphasizing the key points.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 1996

• Next, refer participants to flow charts B2–B7 in the PCPNC guide.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 1997

Quick check is on page B2. RAM is on pages B3–B7. Give all participants 10 minutes to browse through these flow charts.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 1998

• Quick check page – B2. Ask some participants to each read out the first

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 1999

paragraph, and the respective columns ASK, CHECK RECORD, LOOK, LISTEN, FEEL, SIGNS CLASSIFY and TREAT.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2000

• Ask them how long it would take them to do this “Quick check” assessment

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2001

for a woman who just arrived?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2002

• Emphasize the four possible classifications: EMERGENCY FOR WOMAN,

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2003

LABOUR, EMERGENCY FOR BABY and ROUTINE CARE.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2004

• Ask participants to identify which classification means the woman needs

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2005

RAM, and which one means that she can wait for her turn in the queue?

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2006

Case studies: Assessment and management of a pregnant woman with danger symptoms and signs (20 minutes) • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2007

In this case study exercise, you will task participants to use the RAM charts to classify a pregnant client’s complications and determine the type of care needed. Divide the class into groups and assign a facilitator to each group. Present slide 16 and ask participants to read and follow the instructions for the case study using the PCPNC charts.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2008

Provide each group with a case study for classification, along with the activity worksheet. Six case studies have been provided. You may provide additional case studies if needed.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2009

Slide 16: Group work case study – Instructions to the class

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2010

• Appoint a group member as chairperson to facilitate discussions and

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2011

another to report the outcome of the group activity to the class.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2012

• Read and analyse the case studies individually and as a group. When

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2013

the others in your group have finished reading it, discuss the questions. Identify and use the relevant PCPNC charts to guide management of the case. Agree on and document answers for each question on the worksheet provided (refer to participant manual).

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2014

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2015

• • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2016

The time for the group activity is 20 minutes. Each group will be given 10 minutes to present their answers. To end this session, lead a summary discussion to highlight the main lessons from these case studies. The answers to the questions in these case studies are presented below in italics.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity: Using the PCPNC manual — đoạn 2017

Where there are significant differences, refer participants to the PCPNC guide. If the national or local protocol differs, discuss what the correct treatment and advice would be.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 1: Ngozi — đoạn 2018

Ngozi is a 26-year-old nullipara. Her husband carried her into the health centre at 10pm. She is eight months pregnant and complaining of a severe headache, blurred vision and upper abdominal discomfort. She had passed out/fainted briefly on her way to the clinic.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 1: Ngozi — đoạn 2019

Recognizing these as danger signs and symptoms, you did a rapid assessment and found that her blood pressure was 180/120 mmHg. A dipstick test of her urine recorded 4+ proteinuria. She had not experienced any fit. The foetal heart rate is 150/min and regular.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 1: Ngozi — đoạn 2020

Question: Which flow chart(s) in the PCPNC guide is/are relevant for this case study? Answer: B2, B6, B13

### Nguồn gốc: Question: What next steps would you take in her care? — đoạn 2021

Answer: Transfer the woman to a treatment room, call for help, reassure woman and her husband, insert an IV line, give magnesium sulphate, give antihypertensive, refer her to a hospital. (Chart B13)

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Akosua — đoạn 2022

Madam Akosua is pregnant with her fourth baby. She is eight months pregnant and does not attend ANC clinic regularly. You noticed that she looked very worried and uncomfortable when she arrived at the clinic this morning. When you ask her what the problem is, she says that she has been bleeding on and off for two days now. The bleeding has suddenly gotten worse since morning, and she is very worried about her baby. She tells you that she has had to change her pads every two hours and is now using cloth pads instead. She is feeling rather weak and a little dizzy. The baby has not moved very much today.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Akosua — đoạn 2023

Question: Which flow chart(s) in the PCPNC guide is/are relevant for this case study? Answer: B2, B3, B4, B9, B 13, B17

### Nguồn gốc: Question: What next steps would you take in her care? — đoạn 2024

Answer: Transfer the woman to a treatment room for further comprehensive assessment, call for help, reassure woman, perform rapid assessment (ask about quantity of blood loss and presence of abdominal pain; check for pallor; measure blood pressure, pulse, foetal heart rate, abdominal tenderness, presence of contractions). If her blood pressure is low (systolic BP<90) and pulse rate is high (>110), take blood for Hb test, blood grouping and cross-matching; insert an IV line and start IV infusion with crystalloids; and refer her to a hospital.

### Nguồn gốc: Question: What next steps would you take in her care? — đoạn 2025

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 3: Moeshi — đoạn 2026

You are a care provider at the antenatal clinic in Mankanta (a slum area). As you walked into the clinic this morning to start work, you noticed a rather shy-looking teenager sitting in the corner of the antenatal waiting area away from other attendants. She looks rather weak and uncomfortable.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 3: Moeshi — đoạn 2027

You call her immediately into the consulting cubicle after explaining to other clients that we must give priority care to those who are unwell and need more urgent medical attention. She tells you that her name is Moeshi; she is 16 years old and she thinks she is about two and a half months pregnant. Her parents are very angry with her and will not speak to her. She has been vomiting since yesterday and has fever, headache and bodily pains. You check her temperature and it is 39 ˚C. Her eyes look sunken and her tongue furred and dry.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 3: Moeshi — đoạn 2028

Question: Which flow chart(s) in the PCPNC guide is/are relevant for this case study? Answer: B4, B6 (dangerous fever), B9, B16

### Nguồn gốc: Question: What next steps would you take in her care? — đoạn 2029

Answer: Transfer the woman to a treatment room for further comprehensive assessment (check for fast breathing and stiff neck); call for help; reassure woman; do a rapid test for malaria; set up an IV line and give IV fluids slowly; start parenteral antimalarials (for example, quinine); and refer her to a hospital.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 4: Madam Assi — đoạn 2030

Madam Assi is a 24-year-old client who is eight months pregnant. She is reporting for her fifth antenatal checkup. Today, she is complaining of fever, frequent and painful urination, and flank pain which started three days ago. Although she can feel her baby move, she thinks the movements are less than previously felt. You check her temperature and note it is 38 ˚C. Her blood pressure is 120/70 mmHg and her pulse is 100/min. Urine test is positive for protein (+).

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 4: Madam Assi — đoạn 2031

Question: Which flow chart(s) in the PCPNC guide is/are relevant for this case study? Answer: B2, B6, B7, B15, C8

### Nguồn gốc: Question: What next steps would you take in her care? — đoạn 2032

Answer: Transfer the woman to a treatment room for further comprehensive assessment (check foetal heart rate), reassure woman, start appropriate antibiotics and refer her to a hospital.

### Nguồn gốc: Question: What next steps would you take in her care? — đoạn 2033

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 5: Naithwati — đoạn 2034

Naithwati is a 21-year-old client who is coming to the clinic for the very first time. At the registration counter, you notice that she looks rather breathless and tired, so you immediately invite her into the consulting cubicle. She reports that she is seven months pregnant but was unable to come to the clinic earlier because she had no money. She complains of tiring easily and feeling breathless even when she exerts herself slightly. She has also had dizzy spells. She says that she can feel foetal movements.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 5: Naithwati — đoạn 2035

When you perform a quick check, you notice severe conjunctival pallor. Her temperature is normal; her BP is 130/60 mmHg, and her pulse 110/min. Question: Which flow chart(s) in the PCPNC guide is/are relevant for this case study?

### Nguồn gốc: Question: What next steps would you take in her care? — đoạn 2036

Answer: Transfer the woman to a treatment room for further comprehensive assessment (check foetal heart rate and haemoglobin level); reassure woman; start her on double dose iron and folate; and refer her to a hospital.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 6: Madam Ceci — đoạn 2037

Madam Ceci is a 29-year-old G2 P1. She is three months pregnant and reporting for her first antenatal clinic visit. She is well but is complaining of abnormal offensive vaginal discharge and vulval itchiness. Her partner is also complaining of urethral discharge. She has no fever, no lower abdominal or flank pain. Her temperature is normal.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 6: Madam Ceci — đoạn 2038

Her uterus can be felt just above the pubis (912 weeks). When you inspect her vulva, you notice curd-like slightly offensive vaginal discharge. Question: Which flow chart(s) in the PCPNC guide is/are relevant for this case study? Answer: B2, C9, F5, G2

### Nguồn gốc: Question: What next steps would you take in her care? — đoạn 2039

Answer: Keep her in the waiting area; she does not require RAM. Provide routine ANC. In addition, assess her for well-being of the pregnancy and for other STIs; treat her with appropriate antibiotics and antifungals; and counsel her on safer sex.

### Nguồn gốc: Question: What next steps would you take in her care? — đoạn 2040

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2041

Slides 17 and 18: Key messages To summarize and end Session 4, ask participants to read out the key messages or present slides 17 and 18. •

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2042

Clinical decision-making (CDM) comprises five major steps: o Gather information o Interpret information o Develop a care plan o Implement the care plan o Evaluate the care plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2043

•

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2044

Quick check should be used to quickly identify women with danger signs on arrival at the facility to minimize delays in providing them with emergency care.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2045

•

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2046

Women with urgent problems should be transferred to the emergency room or area, and a skilled care provider should perform rapid assessment and

### Nguồn gốc: management (RAM) of the urgent problems. — đoạn 2047

•

### Nguồn gốc: Comprehensive assessment, through history taking, physical — đoạn 2048

examination, laboratory testing and other special investigations, forms the basis for planning and providing care to a woman during her pregnancy. Allow clarification questions from participants (5 minutes).

### Nguồn gốc: Comprehensive assessment, through history taking, physical — đoạn 2049

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Prepare for the Session: Gather all training materials in advance — đoạn 2056

### Nguồn gốc: objectives — đoạn 2061

Slide 1: Title slide;

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 2: Session outline — đoạn 2062

Present the course title and welcome participants to the class. Briefly describe the session outline.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Aim and objectives — đoạn 2063

Task participants to read out loud the aim and objectives of the session (5 minutes). This session focuses on the following: (a) the advice and treatments to be given to a pregnant woman who presents with common discomforts and concerns of pregnancy; and (b) the management of specific common conditions and complications (observed signs and volunteered problems) during antenatal care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Aim and objectives — đoạn 2064

Note: Some select conditions (malaria, anaemia and HIV) in pregnancy are addressed in separate course sessions.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Aim and objectives — đoạn 2065

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2066

• Emphasize that most pregnant women attending antenatal clinics are

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2067

generally healthy and have no abnormal findings on assessment.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2068

• However, a significant proportion may present with concerns and complaints

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2069

that may be due to the changes of pregnancy or have signs and symptoms suggestive of ill-health and disease complications.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2070

• The visit provides the care provider with an opportunity to screen her

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2071

client, identify if the client has a problem or complication and provide e vidence-based interventions and treatments to protect her life and that of her unborn child.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2072

• Session 5 focuses on Box B: “Respond to observed signs and volunteered

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2073

problems”.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Brainstorming discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2074

• Ask participants to list some of the physiological changes that pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Brainstorming discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2075

brings to organ systems in a woman’s body and describe related discomforts and concerns.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Brainstorming discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2076

• List the organ systems on slide 5 to guide the discussion and answers.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Brainstorming discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2077

Record the answers given on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Brainstorming discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2078

• Complete the discussion by referring participants to and reviewing the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Brainstorming discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2079

list of common discomforts and concerns in the chart in Session 5 of the participant manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Importance of managing the common discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2080

• Using the information listed on the slide, highlight and discuss the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Importance of managing the common discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2081

importance of proper management of common complaints of pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Importance of managing the common discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2082

• Emphasize the importance of being knowledgeable about existing

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Importance of managing the common discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2083

local practices and treatments that pregnant women use to address some discomforts so the that harmful practices can be discouraged.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Principles for diagnosing and managing common discomforts of — đoạn 2084

pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Present the principles for diagnosing and managing common discomforts. In — đoạn 2085

order to be able to help women manage their discomforts/concerns, the ANC provider must:

### Nguồn gốc: Present the principles for diagnosing and managing common discomforts. In — đoạn 2086

• distinguish these symptoms from those of life-threatening pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Present the principles for diagnosing and managing common discomforts. In — đoạn 2087

complications; and

### Nguồn gốc: Present the principles for diagnosing and managing common discomforts. In — đoạn 2088

• provide information to the pregnant woman on the underlying cause(s) of

### Nguồn gốc: Present the principles for diagnosing and managing common discomforts. In — đoạn 2089

the discomfort and proffer evidence-based measures to relieve them.

### Nguồn gốc: Present the principles for diagnosing and managing common discomforts. In — đoạn 2090

• Note: Gaps in knowledge lead to widespread use of many useless or harmful

### Nguồn gốc: Present the principles for diagnosing and managing common discomforts. In — đoạn 2091

treatments for some common complaints.

### Nguồn gốc: Present the principles for diagnosing and managing common discomforts. In — đoạn 2092

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Managing the common discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2093

• Present slide 8, then

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Managing the common discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2094

• Introduce trainees to the detailed guide on the identification and

### Nguồn gốc: management of common discomforts and concerns in Appendix 5 of the — đoạn 2095

participant manual.

### Nguồn gốc: management of common discomforts and concerns in Appendix 5 of the — đoạn 2096

• The guide describes ways to distinguish common discomforts from danger

### Nguồn gốc: management of common discomforts and concerns in Appendix 5 of the — đoạn 2097

signs. Trainees should be encouraged to refer to this guide when managing their clients.

### Nguồn gốc: management of common discomforts and concerns in Appendix 5 of the — đoạn 2098

• During the next class group activity on this topic, facilitators should help

### Nguồn gốc: management of common discomforts and concerns in Appendix 5 of the — đoạn 2099

participants use the guide by comparing existing management practices to those recommended in the guide.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 1: Managing common discomforts of pregnancy — đoạn 2100

This group activity is designed to help students distinguish and manage the common discomforts of pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2101

• Review the “Instructions for group work” with the class and make sure that

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2102

students understand what is expected of them.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2103

• Divide the class into groups of approximately five. Assign each group one or

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2104

two organs and body systems as listed in the participant manual. Ask each group to list five discomforts related to their assigned systems. Provide each group with several copies of the “worksheet.”

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2105

• Allow 20 minutes for the groups to complete the activity.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2106

• Supervise the activity by spending some time with each group or assigning

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2107

other facilitators to each of them.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2108

• Allow 10 minutes for each group to report the outcomes of the group

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2109

activity.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2110

• Use the information in Appendix 5 to fill any knowledge gaps.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2111

Summarize the feedback session by emphasizing once again the principles for managing common discomforts and concerns of pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Group work – managing common discomforts and concerns — đoạn 2112

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2113

Slides 10 and 11: Common antenatal disease conditions and complications

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2114

• Introduce the topic by asking clients to brainstorm and answer the questions

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2115

presented on slide 10.

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2116

• Write down the answers given on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2117

• Present slide 11 and compare the answers provided by participants to those

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2118

listed.

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2119

• Discuss any differences and reach consensus on the common disease

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2120

conditions and complications seen by participants in their practice.

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2121

• Highlight conditions such as substance abuse and gender-related violence

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2122

which may be under-reported because they may not be screened for, or have stigma attached to them.

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2123

• Note that whilst some pregnant women with these conditions/

### Nguồn gốc: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2124

complications may present as emergencies with danger symptoms and signs that are detected during “Quick check” (for example, severe pre-eclampsia), others too may be found during routine screening, such as syphilis and foetal demise.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2125

• Emphasize the importance of prompt recognition and management of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2126

obstetric complications by highlighting their unpredictability and the speed with which they can lead to death or severe disability.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2127

• Remind trainees that the “Quick check” must be conducted on all women

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications — đoạn 2128

visiting an antenatal clinic.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2129

pregnancy complications

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2130

• Present all reference documents.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2131

• PCPNC: Refer clients to the following flow charts in the PCPNC guide:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2132

B2, B3, B9, C2 and C3 – to highlight the chart areas for recommended treatments and care: “Treat” “Treatment”, “Advise”, “Treat and Advise”

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2133

• Select participants to read out loud these sections and ask them to note any

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2134

differences in content. Highlight the linkages between flow charts using two examples: circulatory shock (B3 and B9) and anaemia (C4 and F3).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2135

• Introduce participants to other recommended treatment guidelines and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2136

protocols (MCPC, RCP and national protocols) if these are also available for the course. Briefly describe the layout of these documents to participants and how to use them.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2137

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2138

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2139

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2140

Stress the importance of applying the clinical decision-making steps in the management of clients by always linking information gathered (ASK, CHECK RECORD, LOOK, LISTEN, FEEL) to interpret findings (DIAGNOSIS /CLASSIFY) and to the recommended TREATMENT and ADVISE components of the flow chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Using evidence-based protocols and guidelines to manage — đoạn 2141

Clarify any issues raised and discuss any gaps in participants’ knowledge of how to use these charts. The upcoming group work activity on case studies will further enhance participants’ understanding and use of these charts in managing common antenatal disease conditions and complications.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2142

CASE STUDIES: Managing observed and volunteered problems and complications (30 minutes) This group work activity is designed to help participants manage pregnant clients presenting with pregnancy complications using the PCPNC guide and information.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2143

Slides 14 and 15: Group work activity 2 – managing observed and volunteered problems and complications

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2144

• Present slide 14 and review the “Instructions for group work” with the class

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2145

and make sure that trainees understand what is expected of them.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2146

• Divide the class into groups of approximately five and provide each group

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2147

with copies of the case studies and worksheets (or refer them to the relevant page in the participant manual).

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2148

• Assign specific case studies to the groups (using slide 15).

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2149

• Allow 20 minutes for the groups to complete the activity.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2150

• Supervise the activity by spending some time with each group or assigning

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2151

each group a facilitator.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2152

• Allow 10 minutes for each group to provide feedback on the outcome of the

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2153

group activity.

### Nguồn gốc: Group work activity 2 — đoạn 2154

Feedback and discussion: Refer to the answer key for each case study below while discussing the feedback provided by each group. Clarify any questions that may have arisen during the group activity and address any gaps and/ or inconsistencies in trainees’ knowledge.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 1: Antepartum haemorrhage (bleeding in late pregnancy) — đoạn 2156

Madam Akosua is pregnant with her fourth baby. She is eight months pregnant. She does not attend ANC regularly. You noticed that she looked very worried and uncomfortable when she arrived at the clinic this morning. When you ask her what the problem is, she responds that she has been bleeding on and off for two days now. The bleeding has suddenly gotten worse since morning, and she is very worried about her baby.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 1: Antepartum haemorrhage (bleeding in late pregnancy) — đoạn 2157

She tells you that she is forced to change her pads every two hours because they are soaked, and she must now use cloth pads instead. She is feeling rather weak and a little dizzy. The baby has not moved very much today. You make the diagnosis that Akosua has antepartum haemorrhage (bleeding in late pregnancy).

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 1: Antepartum haemorrhage (bleeding in late pregnancy) — đoạn 2158

Question: Using the relevant PCPNC charts and/or other reference guidelines, describe in detail the treatment and other care requirements you will offer Akosua.

### Nguồn gốc: Answers — đoạn 2159

PCPNC flow charts: B2, B4, B9, B3, B17

### Nguồn gốc: Treatments and other care — đoạn 2160

•

### Nguồn gốc: Do not do a vaginal examination — đoạn 2161

• Measure blood pressure and pulse; if in shock (BP< 90mmHg or pulse >110/min): o

### Nguồn gốc: Insert an IV line — đoạn 2162

o Give fluids rapidly if heavy bleeding or shock: infuse 1 litre in 15–20 mins; next, infuse one

### Nguồn gốc: Insert an IV line — đoạn 2163

litre in 30 mins o Position her on her left side with legs higher than chest o Monitor every 15 minutes for: BP, pulse, shortness of breath, urine output o

### Nguồn gốc: Record treatments given and time — đoạn 2164

o

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Severe pre-eclampsia — đoạn 2165

Ngozi is a 26-year-old nullipara. Her husband carried her into the health centre at 10pm. She is eight months pregnant and complaining of a severe headache, blurred vision and upper abdominal discomfort. Recognizing these as danger signs and symptoms, you did a rapid assessment and found that her blood pressure was 180/120 mmHg. A dipstick test of her urine recorded 4+ proteinuria. She had not had any fit.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Severe pre-eclampsia — đoạn 2166

The foetal heart rate is 150/min and regular. Your impression is that Ngozi has severe pre-eclampsia. Question: Using the relevant PCPNC charts and/or other reference guidelines, describe in detail the treatments and other care requirements you will offer Ngozi.

### Nguồn gốc: Answers — đoạn 2167

Flow charts: B2, B7, B9, B13, B14, B17

### Nguồn gốc: Treatments and other care — đoạn 2168

• (Transfer woman to the treatment room and call for help) •

### Nguồn gốc: Check/measure her blood pressure — đoạn 2169

• Insert an IV line and give fluids slowly – 1 litre in 6–8 hours •

### Nguồn gốc: Give magnesium sulphate (dosage and regime in chart) — đoạn 2170

• Give appropriate antihypertensive drugs (hydralazine 5mg IV slowly) and repeat dose at 30 minutes intervals until diastolic BP is at 90 mmHg. Do not give more than 20 mg in total. • Monitor her every 15 minutes for: BP, respiratory rate, pulse, knee jerk reflex, urine output •

### Nguồn gốc: Record treatments given and time — đoạn 2171

•

### Nguồn gốc: Refer her urgently to hospital (give referral note) — đoạn 2172

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 3: Urinary tract infection — đoạn 2173

Madam Assi is a 24-year-old client who is eight months pregnant. She is reporting for her fifth antenatal checkup. She looks ill today and is complaining of fever, vomiting and frequent and painful urination and flank pain which started three days ago. Although she can feel her baby move, she thinks the movements are less than previously felt.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 3: Urinary tract infection — đoạn 2174

You check her temperature and note that it is 39 ˚C. Her blood pressure is 120/70 mmHg and her pulse 100/min. Urine test was positive for protein (+). You conclude that Assi has urinary tract infection. Question: Using the relevant PCPNC charts and/or other reference guidelines, describe in detail the treatments and other care requirements you will offer Madam Assi.

### Nguồn gốc: Answers — đoạn 2175

Flow charts: B2, B6, B7, B15, B17

### Nguồn gốc: Other reference materials: _________________ — đoạn 2176

Treatments and other care – in treatment area: • Insert an IV line and give fluids at moderate rate: 1 litre in 2–3 hours. • Give appropriate IV/IM antibiotics (ampicillin, gentamicin) before referral; continue for 48 hours after woman is fever free. • Monitor her for: BP, temperature, respiratory rate, pulse, urine output.

### Nguồn gốc: Other reference materials: _________________ — đoạn 2177

• Record treatments given and time. •

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 4: Genital tract infection — đoạn 2178

Madam Ceci is a 29-year-old G2 P1. She is three months pregnant and reporting for her first antenatal contact. She is well but is complaining of abnormal offensive vaginal discharge and vulval itchiness. She reports that her partner is also complaining of urethral discharge. She has no fever, lower abdominal or flank pain. Her temperature is normal. Her uterus can be felt just above the pubis (12 weeks). When you inspect her vulva, you notice a curd-like slightly offensive vaginal discharge. Your impression is genital tract infection (possible gonorrhoea/chlamydia/ trichomonas and candidiasis) Question: Using the relevant PCPNC charts and/or other reference guidelines, describe in detail the treatments and other care requirements you will offer Ceci.

### Nguồn gốc: Other references: _________________ — đoạn 2179

Treatments and other care – in treatment area: • Give appropriate oral antibiotics to the woman (two antibiotics: ceftriaxone and erythromycin) •

### Nguồn gốc: Give appropriate oral antibiotics to her partner (ciprofloxacin, doxycycline) — đoạn 2180

•

### Nguồn gốc: Give antifungal drugs (clotrimazole pessary) — đoạn 2181

•

### Nguồn gốc: Advise on correct and consistent condom use — đoạn 2182

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 5: Intimate partner abuse — đoạn 2183

Naithwathi is a 28-year-old client who is coming to the clinic for the very first time. She reports that she is seven months pregnant but was unable to come to the clinic earlier because she had no money and her husband is unwilling to give her any. You notice that she looks sad and has a bruise on her face. After gently prompting, she tells that she has been beaten by her partner. This was not the first time. She has been married to him for three years and has one other child. Her husband is blaming her for getting pregnant too quickly even though he refused to let her use contraceptives. Apart from bodily pains, she is otherwise well and can feel her baby move. When you examine her, you notice that she has another bruise on her back that was healing. Her temperature, BP and pulse are all normal.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 5: Intimate partner abuse — đoạn 2184

Her SFH corresponds to her gestation, and FHR is present and normal. Your diagnosis is intimate partner abuse. Question: Using the relevant PCPNC charts and/or other reference guidelines, describe the treatments and other care requirements you will offer Naithwathi.

### Nguồn gốc: Treat and advise — đoạn 2185

• Support the woman (provide privacy; listen to her; help her assess her situation with respect to her own

### Nguồn gốc: Treat and advise — đoạn 2186

safety and the safety of her children; explore options; identify local sources of support; and refer)

### Nguồn gốc: Treat and advise — đoạn 2187

• Document abuse

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 6: Preterm rupture of membranes — đoạn 2188

You are a care provider at the antenatal clinic in Mankanta (a slum area). As you walked into the clinic this morning to start work, you noticed a rather shy looking teenager sitting in the corner of the waiting area, away from other attendants. She looks rather uncomfortable. You called her immediately into the consulting cubicle after obtaining permission from other waiting clients.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 6: Preterm rupture of membranes — đoạn 2189

Her name is Moeshi and she is an 18-year-old student. She is about seven and a half months pregnant. She has had drainage of copious clear fluid from her vagina for the last three days. This morning, however, the amount of fluid discharge seems to have increased. She also has very slight lower abdominal pain. She can nonetheless still feel her baby move. You check her temperature, and it is normal. Vaginal inspection confirms that she is draining amniotic fluid from her cervix. She does not have uterine contractions on palpation, and FHR is normal. You form an impression of preterm rupture of membranes.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 6: Preterm rupture of membranes — đoạn 2190

Question: Using the relevant PCPNC charts and/or other reference guidelines, describe the treatments and other care requirements you will offer Ngozi.

### Nguồn gốc: Answers — đoạn 2191

Flow charts: C7, B15, B17

### Nguồn gốc: Treat and advise — đoạn 2192

•

### Nguồn gốc: Give appropriate oral antibiotics (ampicillin or erythromycin) — đoạn 2193

•

### Nguồn gốc: Refer her urgently to hospital — đoạn 2194

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

session.

### Nguồn gốc: Common discomforts and concerns of pregnancy must be distinguished — đoạn 2201

from danger signs and other symptoms. The care provider should provide information to the pregnant woman on the causes of the discomforts and counsel her on how to relieve them. The correct management of discomforts increases the client’s confidence in the care provider.

### Nguồn gốc: About 15% of pregnant women will develop problems and — đoạn 2202

complications during their pregnancy. Pregnant women reporting to the antenatal clinic must be screened for problems at all visits and any identified problems managed promptly.

### Nguồn gốc: The PCPNC charts and/or other reference guidelines such as — đoạn 2203

national protocols and the MCPC and RCP manuals can guide antenatal care providers to use their clinical decision-making skills in identifying and classifying common pregnancy complications, and in providing appropriate, evidence-based treatment and advice.

### Nguồn gốc: The PCPNC charts and/or other reference guidelines such as — đoạn 2204

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 2: Session outline — đoạn 2219

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Aim: To describe the recommended evidence-based preventive measures that — đoạn 2220

should be provided during antenatal care.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 2221

On completion of this session, participants will be able to communicate effectively on the following:

### Nguồn gốc: 1. The preventive care measures for common infections and nutritional — đoạn 2222

problems during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: 2. The schedule for giving tetanus toxoid immunization, iron/folate — đoạn 2223

supplementation, deworming, and intermittent preventive treatment for malaria during pregnancy. Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Preventive care measures for healthy pregnancy outcomes — đoạn 2224

(25 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Plenary discussion questions — đoạn 2225

• Ask participants to mention the preventive care measures they provide to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Plenary discussion questions — đoạn 2226

pregnant women in their respective settings and write down their answers on the flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Plenary discussion questions — đoạn 2227

• Early identification and management of antenatal problems is obviously

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Plenary discussion questions — đoạn 2228

important, as outlined in sessions 3 and 4.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Plenary discussion questions — đoạn 2229

• Providing preventive measures to all pregnant women is equally important,

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Plenary discussion questions — đoạn 2230

particularly in Africa, where infections such as malaria, neonatal tetanus, parasitic infestations (such as amoebiasis, hookworm and schistosomiasis), HIV, viral hepatitis and tuberculosis are significant. Nutritional problems, including anaemia, are also prevalent in this environment.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2231

• Present slide 5 to highlight the role of preventive care in the provision of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2232

routine ANC to clients.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2233

• Pregnant women must be assessed and provided these preventive measures

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2234

as part of routine ANC visits, as shown in Figure 6.1.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2235

• They must also be counselled on adherence to medicines and self-care to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2236

avoid risk conditions and maintain good health throughout their pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: The package of evidence-based preventive measures recommended for — đoạn 2237

pregnant women in the sub-Saharan region includes:

### Nguồn gốc: The package of evidence-based preventive measures recommended for — đoạn 2238

• provision of tetanus toxoid immunization

### Nguồn gốc: The package of evidence-based preventive measures recommended for — đoạn 2239

• iron and folate supplementation

### Nguồn gốc: The package of evidence-based preventive measures recommended for — đoạn 2240

• deworming

### Nguồn gốc: The package of evidence-based preventive measures recommended for — đoạn 2241

• intermittent preventive treatment for malaria

### Nguồn gốc: The package of evidence-based preventive measures recommended for — đoạn 2242

• sleeping under an insecticide-treated bed net.

### Nguồn gốc: The package of evidence-based preventive measures recommended for — đoạn 2243

• prevention of mother-to-child transmission of HIV, viral hepatitis, and

### Nguồn gốc: The package of evidence-based preventive measures recommended for — đoạn 2244

syphilis (further discussed in session 11). Review the participants’ list of interventions on the flip chart and discuss any discrepancies.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Recommended preventive measures (2) — đoạn 2245

• Highlight the fact that all pregnant women must be assessed for and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Recommended preventive measures (2) — đoạn 2246

provided with preventive care interventions.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Recommended preventive measures (2) — đoạn 2247

• Emphasize that counselling on the interventions and medicines as well

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Recommended preventive measures (2) — đoạn 2248

as self-care forms an essential component of providing preventive care measures.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Recommended preventive measures (2) — đoạn 2249

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Assessing clients for and providing preventive measures (25 minutes) — đoạn 2250

Figure 6.2 (below) specifies how to assess the client for these preventive interventions and which actions to take. Emphasize that assessment involves questioning the client directly and reviewing their maternal health records. to assess the woman for preventive measures and when to give them.
