Figure 6.2 Preventive care measures for all pregnant women

### Nguồn gốc: Risk — đoạn 2255

condition

### Nguồn gốc: Nutritional — đoạn 2256

anaemia

### Nguồn gốc: Check the written — đoạn 2257

records of th e w o m a n ’ s tetanus (TT/ Td) immunization status.

### Nguồn gốc: Has she had a series — đoạn 2258

of five TT immunizations? If yes, was the last booster less than 10 years ago?

### Nguồn gốc: If a pregnant woman has not been previously — đoạn 2259

vaccinated or if her immunization status is unknown, give two doses of tetanus toxoidcontaining vaccine: the first dose as early as possible during pregnancy, and the second dose at least 4 weeks later3.

### Nguồn gốc: Check the woman’s — đoạn 2260

supply of the prescribed dose of iron/folate.

### Nguồn gốc: Check the woman’s — đoạn 2261

Start iron/folate supplements at first visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Give her 3 months’ supply of daily iron/folate — đoạn 2262

(1 tablet = 60 mg iron + 400 m c g folic acid)1.

See details in tables below.

visits.

anaemia.

prevent

Counsel on how to take and side‐eﬀects.

### Nguồn gốc: Intestinal — đoạn 2268

worms

### Nguồn gốc: Check — đoạn 2269

when the woman last received a dose of mebendazole. (If within previous 6 months, wait until 6 months have passed.)

### Nguồn gốc: Give mebendazole (500 mg) once during — đoạn 2270

second or third trimester of pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Confirm EDC and — đoạn 2271

gestational age.

### Nguồn gốc: IPT should start in the second trimester, and — đoạn 2272

doses should be given at least one month apart, with the objective of ensuring that at least three doses are received. Dose = 3 tablets (1 tablet = 500 mg sulfadoxine + 25 mg pyrimethamine).

### Nguồn gốc: Check when last — đoạn 2273

dose was taken.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsel woman and her family about sleeping — đoạn 2274

under LLIN.

### Nguồn gốc: Encourage sleeping under an insecticide‐ — đoạn 2275

treated bed net.

### Nguồn gốc: Assess if commenced Oral — đoạn 2276

pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) PCPNC G1–G8 on

### Nguồn gốc: ARVs — đoạn 2277

and containing tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) National PMTCT adherence to ARVs. should be oﬀered as an additional prevention guidelines Ask the woman about choice for pregnant women at substantial risk safe sex and condom of HIV infection as part of combination use.

### Nguồn gốc: ARVs — đoạn 2278

prevention approaches. 27

### Nguồn gốc: ARVs — đoạn 2279

Counsel on condom use and provide condoms.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for facilitators — đoạn 2352

Giving individual feedback will require the participation of all facilitators. As participants finish this exercise, they should raise a hand to get a facilitator’s attention or come to you. When they do, the facilitator should sit with each participant to review their work.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for facilitators — đoạn 2356

Sit somewhat away from others who are still working (if possible) and keep your voice low so as not to disturb others. First, check that the participant has written correct answers in the first chart – the information can be found in Figure 6.2 or PCPNC charts F2, F3, F4. If the participant has an incorrect answer, ask about it. Either she did not refer to Figure 6.2, or perhaps she is describing a standard that is different in her clinic. Make sure that she has a correct understanding of good practice.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for facilitators — đoạn 2357

Next, review the second chart. Ask the participant about some of the answers to show your interest in whether providing these preventive measures is a challenge in her clinic and how she plans to overcome these challenges. If you see that some participants have finished the exercise but have not discussed it with any facilitator, encourage them to do so.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for facilitators — đoạn 2358

### Nguồn gốc: Probable solutions — đoạn 2420

Conclude the session by presenting the key messages below.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2421

• In sub-Saharan Africa, environmental factors lead to diseases such as

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2422

malaria, nutritional anaemia, neonatal tetanus and parasitic infestations (such as amoebiasis, hookworm, and schistosomiasis) which are major contributors to poor pregnancy outcomes.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2423

• Special attention must be paid to the prevention of mother-to-child

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2424

transmission of HIV, hepatitis B and syphilis.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2425

• The

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2426

recommended preventive care medicines to be given during antenatal care include tetanus toxoid immunization, iron/ folate supplementation, deworming with mebendazole, and intermittent preventive treatment for malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2427

• During each antenatal visit, pregnant women must be counselled on

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (5 minutes) — đoạn 2428

adherence to preventive measures such as taking daily iron and folic acid tablets, sleeping under an insecticide-treated bed net, and returning for subsequent ANC contacts so that they can receive all the recommended preventive measures.

### Nguồn gốc: Classroom quiz: ANC (sessions 1–6) – answers — đoạn 2429

The following quiz can be conducted as a classroom question-answer activity. Simply read each question to the class and allow a show of hands for the response. If an incorrect response is given, provide the correct one. Avoid accepting answers from the same trainee(s). Encourage all trainees to participate, as the quiz provides you with an opportunity to assess their level of knowledge and understanding so far (for example, the quiz covers the content of sessions 1 through 5).

### Nguồn gốc: Classroom quiz: ANC (sessions 1–6) – answers — đoạn 2430

Depending on the progress made in this course, time constraints may prove a challenge. Suggested alternatives are: (i) reduce the number of selected questions asked; (ii) use some questions when summarizing the session as suggested in the previous session; and (3) hand out the quiz to the trainees as a take-home assignment, to be returned the next day for marking.

### Nguồn gốc: Classroom quiz: ANC (sessions 1–6) – answers — đoạn 2431

Present and discuss the correct answers as listed below.

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Give five reasons why periodic visits to a health care provider are necessary during pr — đoạn 2432

Ans.

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Give five reasons why periodic visits to a health care provider are necessary during pr — đoạn 2433

• Early detection and treatment of complications.

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Give five reasons why periodic visits to a health care provider are necessary during pr — đoạn 2434

• Establishment of a supportive relationship between the woman and the health care provider.

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Give five reasons why periodic visits to a health care provider are necessary during pr — đoạn 2435

• Development of a birth and emergency plan with the woman.

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Give five reasons why periodic visits to a health care provider are necessary during pr — đoạn 2436

• Provision of preventive measures.

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Give five reasons why periodic visits to a health care provider are necessary during pr — đoạn 2437

• Provision of advice and counselling.

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Give five reasons why periodic visits to a health care provider are necessary during pr — đoạn 2438

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 2. What is the recommended schedule of antenatal contacts (that is, how many routine visit — đoạn 2439

intervals)? Ans. Eight contacts at 12, 20, 26, 30, 34, 36, 38 and 40 weeks

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with th — đoạn 2440

maintain privacy and confidentiality. Ans. Any four of the following:

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with th — đoạn 2441

• ensure a private place for examination and counselling;

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with th — đoạn 2442

• ensure that discussions, especially about sensitive subjects, cannot be overheard;

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with th — đoạn 2443

• ensure that the woman has given her consent before discussing matters with her partner or family;

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with th — đoạn 2444

• never discuss confidential information about clients with other providers, or outside of the health facility;

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with th — đoạn 2445

• organize the examination area so that, during examinations, the woman is protected (with a curtain, screen, or

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with th — đoạn 2446

wall) from the view of other people;

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with th — đoạn 2447

• ensure all records are confidential and kept locked away; and

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with th — đoạn 2448

• limit access to logbooks and registers to responsible health care providers only.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. What are the daily and occasional administrative activities that should be conducted to — đoạn 2449

proper running of antenatal clinics?

### Nguồn gốc: 4. What are the daily and occasional administrative activities that should be conducted to — đoạn 2450

Ans.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. What are the daily and occasional administrative activities that should be conducted to — đoạn 2451

• Records of equipment, supplies, drugs, and vaccines should be updated.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. What are the daily and occasional administrative activities that should be conducted to — đoạn 2452

• Essential equipment should be checked daily to ensure proper functioning.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. What are the daily and occasional administrative activities that should be conducted to — đoạn 2453

• Staff lists and schedules should be established.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. What are the daily and occasional administrative activities that should be conducted to — đoạn 2454

• Periodic reports on births, deaths, and other indicators should be completed according to regulations.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Why is it essential for health care providers to follow universal precautions when prov — đoạn 2455

Ans. To protect the woman, her baby, themselves and the community from infection with bacteria and viruses, including HIV.

### Nguồn gốc: 6. What should be done first when a woman presents at an antenatal clinic? — đoạn 2456

Ans. A Quick check should be performed to identify any emergency signs and, if an emergency sign is identified, rapid assessment and management (RAM) should follow.

• syphilis

### Nguồn gốc: 7. Name five individual risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2464

### Nguồn gốc: 7. Name five individual risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2466

### Nguồn gốc: 8. Name five community risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2474

• great distance from a woman’s home to a health facility where the required care is available

### Nguồn gốc: 8. Name five community risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2475

• lack of transportation between home and a health facility

### Nguồn gốc: 8. Name five community risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2476

• low socioeconomic status

### Nguồn gốc: 8. Name five community risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2477

• low educational status

### Nguồn gốc: 8. Name five community risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2478

• prevailing sociocultural and religious beliefs that are harmful during pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: 8. Name five community risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2479

• violence against women.

### Nguồn gốc: 9. Name five health services risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2480

Ans. Any five of the following:

### Nguồn gốc: 9. Name five health services risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2481

• antenatal clinics that do not have the basic supplies, equipment and drugs for antenatal care;

### Nguồn gốc: 9. Name five health services risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2482

• staff that are not trained to provide routine and emergency care during the antenatal period;

### Nguồn gốc: 9. Name five health services risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2483

• absence of a functional referral system for the management of complications;

### Nguồn gốc: 9. Name five health services risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2484

• inability of health care providers to manage women with special needs;

### Nguồn gốc: 9. Name five health services risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2485

• negative attitudes of health care providers toward pregnant adolescents;

### Nguồn gốc: 9. Name five health services risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2486

• limited competencies of health care providers to manage women who experience violence; and

### Nguồn gốc: 9. Name five health services risk factors during pregnancy? — đoạn 2487

• poor links between the health facility and the community and traditional providers.

### Nguồn gốc: 10. When giving emotional support to a woman with special needs, how can the health care p — đoạn 2488

comfortable environment for the woman? Ans.

### Nguồn gốc: 10. When giving emotional support to a woman with special needs, how can the health care p — đoạn 2489

• By being aware of her own attitude;

### Nguồn gốc: 10. When giving emotional support to a woman with special needs, how can the health care p — đoạn 2490

• by being open and approachable;

### Nguồn gốc: 10. When giving emotional support to a woman with special needs, how can the health care p — đoạn 2491

• by using a gentle, reassuring tone of voice.

### Nguồn gốc: 11. What does the health care provider need to do to help the pregnant adolescent consider — đoạn 2492

decisions? Ans.

### Nguồn gốc: 11. What does the health care provider need to do to help the pregnant adolescent consider — đoạn 2493

• Help the adolescent girl understand the importance of planning to have her baby in a hospital or health centre.

### Nguồn gốc: 11. What does the health care provider need to do to help the pregnant adolescent consider — đoạn 2494

• Help her to understand the importance, to herself and to her baby, of STI or HIV/AIDS prevention, including the

### Nguồn gốc: 11. What does the health care provider need to do to help the pregnant adolescent consider — đoạn 2495

use of condoms in all sexual relations.

### Nguồn gốc: 11. What does the health care provider need to do to help the pregnant adolescent consider — đoạn 2496

• Help her to understand the importance of birth spacing for her own health and that of her baby, including the

### Nguồn gốc: 11. What does the health care provider need to do to help the pregnant adolescent consider — đoạn 2497

recommendation of at least 2–3 years between pregnancies.

### Nguồn gốc: 11. What does the health care provider need to do to help the pregnant adolescent consider — đoạn 2498

• Support her in making the best decisions for herself and for her baby.

### Nguồn gốc: Ans. FALSE — đoạn 2499

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 15. Clinical decision-making occurs before development of the care plan and does not need — đoạn 2500

that patient or client. True or false?

### Nguồn gốc: 16. What observations (LOOK, LISTEN, FEEL) does the health care provider need to make when — đoạn 2501

check on an antenatal client? Ans. The person doing the Quick check needs to observe whether the woman is being wheeled or carried into the clinic or whether any of the following are present: vaginal bleeding, convulsions, looks very ill, unconscious, in severe pain, in labour, delivery imminent.

### Nguồn gốc: 17. What emergency signs are included in rapid assessment and management (RAM) of a pregna — đoạn 2502

Ans.

### Nguồn gốc: 17. What emergency signs are included in rapid assessment and management (RAM) of a pregna — đoạn 2503

• Airway and breathing: very difficult breathing or central cyanosis

### Nguồn gốc: 17. What emergency signs are included in rapid assessment and management (RAM) of a pregna — đoạn 2504

• Circulation and shock: cold and moist skin, or weak and fast pulse

### Nguồn gốc: 17. What emergency signs are included in rapid assessment and management (RAM) of a pregna — đoạn 2505

• Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy: uterus not above the umbilicus

### Nguồn gốc: 17. What emergency signs are included in rapid assessment and management (RAM) of a pregna — đoạn 2506

• Vaginal bleeding in late pregnancy: uterus above the umbilicus

### Nguồn gốc: 17. What emergency signs are included in rapid assessment and management (RAM) of a pregna — đoạn 2507

• Convulsions or unconsciousness: convulsing (now or recently), or unconscious

### Nguồn gốc: 17. What emergency signs are included in rapid assessment and management (RAM) of a pregna — đoạn 2508

• Severe abdominal pain: not normal labour

### Nguồn gốc: 17. What emergency signs are included in rapid assessment and management (RAM) of a pregna — đoạn 2509

• Dangerous fever: temperature >38 ˚C and any of the following: very fast breathing, stiff neck, lethargy, very

### Nguồn gốc: 17. What emergency signs are included in rapid assessment and management (RAM) of a pregna — đoạn 2510

weak/not able to stand.

### Nguồn gốc: 18. At which antenatal visit should the woman be checked for anaemia? — đoạn 2511

Ans. At every visit, check for pallor.

### Nguồn gốc: 19. When checking for anaemia, what does the health care provider need to ask the pregnant — đoạn 2512

Ans. If the woman tires easily and if she is breathless (short of breath) during routine chores or work.

### Nguồn gốc: 20. At which antenatal contact should the woman’s haemoglobin be measured? — đoạn 2513

Ans. Check HB at contact 1, 3 and 6 if the woman has no complaints.

### Nguồn gốc: 20. At which antenatal contact should the woman’s haemoglobin be measured? — đoạn 2514

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

• Flip chart with paper and markers

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2516

• Training course materials: facilitator guide, participant manual

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2517

• Pregnancy, childbirth postpartum and newborn care (PCPNC) guide

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2518

• Learning guide for antenatal counselling

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2519

• Laptop

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2520

• Overhead projector

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2521

• One table and three chairs

### Nguồn gốc: Preparation — đoạn 2522

• Gather training materials in advance.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparation — đoạn 2523

• Review the module in the participant and facilitator manuals.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparation — đoạn 2524

• Identify suitable participants to perform the role plays and brief them so that they can prepare in advance.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 2: Session outline — đoạn 2525

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 2: Session outline — đoạn 2526

outlines.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Aim and objectives — đoạn 2527

session (5 Minutes).

### Nguồn gốc: AIM: To help participants understand the importance of good communication — đoạn 2528

during antenatal care while improving their skills in counselling pregnant women and support persons.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 2529

On completion of this session, participants will be able to: (a) demonstrate five good communication practices during ANC; (b) outline the steps involved in the counselling process; (c) discuss the advantages of group counselling, couple counselling and individual counselling; and (d) apply counselling principles and skills in antenatal care.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 2530

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Women want a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 2531

• Ask participants what they understand by a positive pregnancy experience;

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Women want a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 2532

write down their answers on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Women want a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 2533

• Present slide 4.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Women want a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 2534

• Ask participants to share some of their positive and negative experiences

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Women want a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 2535

during their interactions with antenatal clients.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Women want a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 2536

• Ask about the effect of these experiences on ANC uptake and quality.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Women want a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 2537

Slides 5, 6 and 7: What do women say about their ANC experience? • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Women want a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 2538

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Women want a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 2539

Many factors affect the uptake and quality of ANC for women. Present these slides to share some positive and negative comments received from antenatal clients on their pregnancy care experiences.

### Nguồn gốc: These comments were obtained during qualitative surveys — đoạn 2540

conducted by WHO for the purpose of developing guidelines to ensure a positive pregnancy experience.

### Nguồn gốc: Use — đoạn 2541

these together with the shared experience of participants to emphasize the importance of good client-provider interaction and communication during antenatal care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Key steps in routine ANC — đoạn 2542

• • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Key steps in routine ANC — đoạn 2543

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Key steps in routine ANC — đoạn 2544

To introduce the topic, ask participants what counselling in antenatal care is. Write down their answers on the flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: ANC counselling is an interactive process between the health care — đoạn 2545

provider and her client during which information is exchanged and support is provided to enable the woman and her family to make decisions, develop a plan, and take actions to ensure the health of herself and her baby. Using Slide 9, highlight the importance of ANC counselling in routine care provision.

### Nguồn gốc: ANC counselling is an interactive process between the health care — đoạn 2546

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: ANC counselling is an interactive process between the health care — đoạn 2547

Slide 10: Good counselling is critical for a positive pregnancy experience

### Nguồn gốc: ANC counselling is an interactive process between the health care — đoạn 2548

• A positive pregnancy experience hinges on good antenatal counselling.

### Nguồn gốc: ANC counselling is an interactive process between the health care — đoạn 2549

• Use this slide to describe and emphasize the client-provider interactions and

### Nguồn gốc: ANC counselling is an interactive process between the health care — đoạn 2550

communications that support positive pregnancy outcomes for the client.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Guiding principles for counselling — đoạn 2551

• Present slide 11 to list the guiding principles of counselling.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Guiding principles for counselling — đoạn 2552

Slides 12, 13, 14 and 15: Guiding principles for counselling

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Guiding principles for counselling — đoạn 2553

• Refer participants to the participant manual and/or present slides 12–15.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Guiding principles for counselling — đoạn 2554

• Ask participants to take turns reading the paragraphs describing each of the principles in detail.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Guiding principles for counselling — đoạn 2555

• Explain each principle by giving relevant examples. You may use the “examples” provided below if necessary.

### Nguồn gốc: Self-reflection — đoạn 2556

• Being a staunch Christian, the ANC care provider always insists that her

### Nguồn gốc: Self-reflection — đoạn 2557

Muslim clients also say the “Our Father” prayer prior to start of the clinic (or vice versa). This upsets them.

### Nguồn gốc: Respect and empathy — đoạn 2558

• The local cultural practice requires that all pregnant women wear large

### Nguồn gốc: Respect and empathy — đoạn 2559

beaded amulets around their hip to protect themselves and their babies from pregnancy complications.

### Nguồn gốc: Respect and empathy — đoạn 2560

• During group counselling on danger signs, explain the importance of danger

### Nguồn gốc: Respect and empathy — đoạn 2561

signs in detecting pregnancy complications early, and that pregnant women should adhere to your advice whilst also wearing the charms. They do not have to remove them before coming to the clinic because of their fear of your disapproval.

### Nguồn gốc: Respect and empathy — đoạn 2562

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Confidentiality — đoạn 2563

• Emphasize the need for clinic settings that promote visual and auditory

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Confidentiality — đoạn 2564

privacy (for example, consulting cubicles with closed doors or curtains; not conducting consultations with other clients in the room).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Confidentiality — đoạn 2565

• Use HIV as an example of a sensitive topic to highlight challenges that could

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Confidentiality — đoạn 2566

occur if a care provider shared a client’s positive test results with her best friend who unknowingly happens to be the client’s sister-in-law.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Confidentiality — đoạn 2567

• Discuss how ANC clients’ records are kept to ensure confidentiality.

### Nguồn gốc: Encouraging interaction — đoạn 2568

• Demonstrate verbal and nonverbal ways of promoting two-way interactions:

### Nguồn gốc: Encouraging interaction — đoạn 2569

• “Tell me about yourself and how this problem started.”

### Nguồn gốc: Encouraging interaction — đoạn 2570

• “Did you say that your husband refuses to give you money to come to this

### Nguồn gốc: Encouraging interaction — đoạn 2571

clinic?”

### Nguồn gốc: Encouraging interaction — đoạn 2572

• Holding the client’s hand if culturally acceptable.

### Nguồn gốc: Building on current knowledge or skills — đoạn 2573

• Explaining to an anaemic client who already knows that iron and folate must

### Nguồn gốc: Building on current knowledge or skills — đoạn 2574

be taken during pregnancy why she must now take a double dose of her iron medication.

### Nguồn gốc: Shared problem solving — đoạn 2575

• A pregnant woman facing spousal abuse: discuss all available options with

### Nguồn gốc: Shared problem solving — đoạn 2576

her – move to stay with her mother; report her spouse to the police; stay at home with her husband but take measures to protect herself from injuries during physical assaults/attacks.

### Nguồn gốc: Shared problem solving — đoạn 2577

• State that you will support her, whatever her decision: she decides for the

### Nguồn gốc: Shared problem solving — đoạn 2578

sake of her young children that she will continue to stay at home with her spouse and use protective measures.

### Nguồn gốc: Tailoring to specific needs — đoạn 2579

• Your client is an unmarried teenager, whose boyfriend abandoned her when

### Nguồn gốc: Tailoring to specific needs — đoạn 2580

she became pregnant. She wants to give up her baby for adoption and asks for your advice and help in this regard.

### Nguồn gốc: Tailoring to specific needs — đoạn 2581

• You readily consent and provide her with all the information and support she

### Nguồn gốc: Tailoring to specific needs — đoạn 2582

needs.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Key steps in the counselling process — đoạn 2583

(a) Assess the situation. (b) Define the problem, needs or information gaps. (c) Generate alternative solutions. (d) Prioritize the solutions. (e) Develop a plan. (f) Review and evaluate. Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Key steps in the counselling process — đoạn 2584

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Key steps in the counselling process — đoạn 2585

As you go through each step, ask a participant to read it out loud and explain the paragraphs in the participant manual that describe each step more fully. Correct any discrepancies (20 minutes). Note that the guiding principles outlined above support these six steps in the counselling process.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Key steps in the counselling process — đoạn 2586

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Counselling skills — đoạn 2587

• Present and discuss the counselling skills listed in the slide.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Counselling skills — đoạn 2588

• Emphasize that these skills are based on the guiding principles and the six

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Counselling skills — đoạn 2589

steps in the counselling process described above.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Counselling skills — đoạn 2590

• As you go through each counselling skill, ask participants in turn to read out

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Counselling skills — đoạn 2591

loud and explain to the rest of the class the paragraphs in the participant manual that describe the skills in detail.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Counselling skills — đoạn 2592

• Correct any misunderstanding.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: The counselling context — đoạn 2593

• Present slides 18 and 19 and clarify any issues that arise.

### Nguồn gốc: Class activities 1 and 2 — đoạn 2594

During these class activities, assist participants to learn the counselling skills through: 1. 2.

### Nguồn gốc: Class activities 1 and 2 — đoạn 2595

individual work (activity 1); and observing a demonstration of the correct ways of performing these skills (activity 2).

### Nguồn gốc: Class activities 1 and 2 — đoạn 2596

Prepare for this activity as described below.

### Nguồn gốc: Class activities 1 and 2 — đoạn 2597

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Applying counselling principles and skills (20 minutes) — đoạn 2598

• Refer participants to the relevant section in their manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Applying counselling principles and skills (20 minutes) — đoạn 2599

• Explain the purpose of the exercise.

### Nguồn gốc: Applying counselling principles and skills (20 minutes) — đoạn 2600

• Read out and explain the instructions.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 2601

• Ask participants to work individually on this exercise. They should read the

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 2602

question and write their answers in the space provided.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 2603

• When they have finished, the facilitator will lead a feedback discussion so

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 2604

that participants can share their answers and comments.

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2605

• When all participants have completed the exercise, ask for volunteers to read their answers to each

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2606

question. Then ask if anyone has another possible answer.

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2607

• If an answer does not demonstrate the counselling skill as it should, ask the other participants for

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2608

another answer.

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2609

• Refer to the answer sheet provided to give feedback and to conduct discussions to identify the best

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2610

answers.

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2611

Question 1: Write a response to each statement below that shows respect and empathy. (a) “I’ve been crying over something practically every day. It’s crazy.” Answer: You’re crying almost every day and you feel like that’s crazy ! (b) “Men in my family do not come to the health centre with their wives.” Answer: In your family, it is not expected that your husband will come to the clinic with you.

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2612

Question 2: Write a better response than the one below to encourage interaction. (a) “You don’t have to tell me about your mother-in-law. Believe me, I’ve heard it all before.” Answer: Tell me more about your mother-in-law and how you are feeling. Question 3: How should the health workers described below demonstrate active listening?

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2613

(a) Nurse Lila is leaning back in her chair and looking out the window while the woman is talking. Answer: She should sit forward in her chair, look at the woman while she talks, and nod or smile

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2614

(as appropriate) at what she is saying. (b) Sister Shepherd is sitting behind a desk, and the door to the noisy hallway is open. Answer: She should sit in a chair on the same side of the desk as the woman. She should shut

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2615

the door to make it quieter and more private. Question 4: Write better questions than those below to find out about a woman who has anaemia. (a) “Your haemoglobin has gone down. Have you been skipping your iron pills?” Answer: Are you able to take your iron pills each day, or are you having any difficulties with them?

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2616

(b) “I presume you are eating well?” Answer: Can you describe to me what you have been eating?

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2617

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2618

Question 5: Rewrite the following statements to better provide this information to a pregnant woman: (a) “Malaria infection can lead to severe anaemia, severe placental parasitation and even foetal death!” Answer: If you get malaria while you are pregnant, it is dangerous. You are likely to become

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2619

sicker than you would otherwise be, and it could even kill your baby. (b) “You must bring your husband for HIV testing and counselling, as well as consultation about

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2620

making plans for institutional birth and other contingencies.” Answer: It would be very helpful if you could bring your husband with you on your next contact

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2621

– if possible. I would like to talk to him about HIV testing and counselling and if he agrees, he

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2622

can be tested also. We could also all talk together about plans for the baby’s birth and what you could do in case of an emergency.

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2623

Topic: Counselling a client on pregnancy care by a skilled attendant (30 minutes) Preparations for this activity:

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2624

• Prepare for this role play activity when preparing for the session by:

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2625

• identifying and orientating role play actors as follows:

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2626

• choose a facilitator to play the role of the health provider and another two volunteers (participants) to

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2627

play the role of Jeanine the patient and her mother; and

### Nguồn gốc: Activity 1: Applying counselling principles and skills – answer sheet — đoạn 2628

• orientate the actors about their roles and allow them to practice these roles until perfect.

### Nguồn gốc: Actor roles — đoạn 2629

Health care provider: The provider is an experienced community midwife at a primary health care centre who has good communication skills. Jeanine: Jeanine is a 28-year-old woman; she has four living children and is now four months pregnant. One of her babies died shortly after birth, and her sister died in childbirth last year.

### Nguồn gốc: Actor roles — đoạn 2630

Jeanine’s mother: Jeanine’s mother is 52 years old. She has eight living children; she had two stillbirths and one child died at the age of 1 month. One of her daughters died in childbirth last year. Participants will act as the audience and observers. They should take notes on how the health provider applied counselling principles and skills.

### Nguồn gốc: Case scenario and background information — đoạn 2631

• Jeanine, a 28-year-old woman, has come to the health centre with her mother. She has four living children and is

### Nguồn gốc: Case scenario and background information — đoạn 2632

now four months pregnant. One of her babies died shortly after birth. Her mother and grandmother have helped her to deliver all her babies at home.

### Nguồn gốc: Case scenario and background information — đoạn 2633

• Jeanine’s mother is 52 years old. She has eight living children; she has had two stillbirths, with one other child

### Nguồn gốc: Case scenario and background information — đoạn 2634

dying at the age of 1 month.

### Nguồn gốc: Case scenario and background information — đoạn 2635

• Jeanine has been to the health centre once before, when she brought her 5-year-old son who had pneumonia

### Nguồn gốc: Case scenario and background information — đoạn 2636

last year. The two women are interested in learning more about the care available at the health centre because a relative delivered her baby there six months ago. Jeanine is nervous about her current pregnancy because her sister died in childbirth at home last year.

### Nguồn gốc: Case scenario and background information — đoạn 2637

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Focus of the role play — đoạn 2638

The focus of the role play is the interaction between the midwife, Jeanine, and her mother as they discuss Jeanine’s desires as an expectant mother – keeping in mind her rights as a human being. The midwife should demonstrate the following: • • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Focus of the role play — đoạn 2639

•

### Nguồn gốc: Focus of the role play — đoạn 2640

be friendly and reassuring; discuss safe motherhood principles and a woman’s right to expect safe, respectful health care; assess Jeanine’s knowledge about the role of the midwife and the services available for women; describe the role of the midwife to the women; briefly explain what services are available and how Jeanine can be involved in the decisions about her care; discuss Jeanine’s human rights as a childbearing woman, generally (such as her right to be free from ill treatment and to be assured privacy and confidentiality) and specifically (such as her right to have a companion during doctor-patient contacts throughout her pregnancy and during childbirth); and encourage the women to ask questions, and address the questions they ask.

### Nguồn gốc: Focus of the role play — đoạn 2641

Jeanine and her mother should ask questions and express their concerns until the midwife has provided them with enough information about the role of the midwife, their rights, and the care available at the health centre.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Demonstration of counselling skills — đoạn 2642

• Present the instructions for this demonstration role play.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Demonstration of counselling skills — đoạn 2643

• Instruct participants to closely observe the role play and to document their

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Demonstration of counselling skills — đoạn 2644

observations as per the guidance provided under “Focus of the role play”.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Demonstration of counselling skills — đoạn 2645

• They will discuss their observations in the feedback session at end of the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Demonstration of counselling skills — đoạn 2646

role play.

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2647

At the end of the role play, the facilitator should use the questions below to facilitate discussions:

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2648

• How did the midwife prepare her room for the counselling activity?

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2649

• How did the midwife approach Jeanine and her mother?

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2650

• Did the midwife give Jeanine and her mother enough information about the role of the midwife? About the

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2651

health centre? About her right to safe motherhood? About her right to a birth companion?

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2652

• How did Jeanine and her mother respond to the midwife?

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2653

• What did the midwife do to demonstrate emotional support and reassurance?

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2654

• Were the midwife’s explanations and reassurance effective? Why or why not?

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2655

Allow participants to comment on these questions extensively whilst pointing out the good counselling skills and practices.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Counselling approaches — đoạn 2656

• Introduce the topic by asking participants to describe the various ways/

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Counselling approaches — đoạn 2657

approaches they use to provide counselling in their settings. What factors influence their choices for the different approaches they use?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Counselling approaches — đoạn 2658

• Record their answers on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Counselling approaches — đoạn 2659

• Present slide 22 to describe the three counselling approaches used in

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Counselling approaches — đoạn 2660

antenatal care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Counselling approaches — đoạn 2661

• Compare these with participants’ answers and fill any gaps.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Counselling approaches — đoạn 2662

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Counselling approaches — đoạn 2663

• • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Counselling approaches — đoạn 2664

Slides 23–26 explain the advantages and disadvantages of each of the three counselling approaches (group, couple and individual). First, ask participants to list the advantages and disadvantages of each of the approaches; write down their answers on the flipchart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Counselling approaches — đoạn 2665

Then, present slides 23–26 to describe the advantages and disadvantages of each counselling approach. Compare these with participants’ answers and fill any gaps. For each slide, highlight the following important considerations:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2666

• During this interaction, the health provider counsels and delivers health

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2667

messages to several people at the same time.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2668

• This can be employed in busy clinics for non-sensitive messages; and in

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2669

settings where group ANC is being conducted.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2670

• This does not eliminate the need for individual counselling for sensitive and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2671

private issues.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2672

Slides 24 and 25: Couple counselling – advantages and disadvantages

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2673

• Couple counselling should be voluntary for both partners and should not be

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2674

mandatory or coerced.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2675

• The antenatal clinic set-up must be couple- or male-friendly, with adequate

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2676

seating provided for the pregnant woman and her partner.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2677

• During couple counselling, while joint decision-making is encouraged, both

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2678

partners must feel free to make independent decisions.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2679

• An agreement should be reached with the couple at the start of the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Group counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2680

counselling activity about sharing confidential information.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Individual counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2681

• The individual counselling approach is best suited to discuss sensitive

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Individual counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2682

matters, especially those related to sexuality.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Individual counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2683

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Individual counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2684

Conclude this subject by emphasizing that the approaches complement each other. The choice of which approach to use in addressing a health topic is influenced by clinic situations such as workload and time, the sensitive nature of the topic, and the complexity and relevance of the message.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Individual counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2685

Give this example: a group counselling approach may be used to introduce PMTCT and explain what HIV is and its modes of infection. Couple counselling on the same topic can be employed prior to testing of the pregnant woman and her partner whilst individual counselling can be used during post-test counselling.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Individual counselling – advantages and disadvantages — đoạn 2686

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 27: Individualizing ANC messages — đoạn 2687

Introduce the subject by explaining the following:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 27: Individualizing ANC messages — đoạn 2688

• The antenatal period is a nine-month long period during which the pregnant

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 27: Individualizing ANC messages — đoạn 2689

woman experiences many changes with different health needs and concerns along the way.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 27: Individualizing ANC messages — đoạn 2690

• Different pregnant women have different needs during their pregnancies.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 27: Individualizing ANC messages — đoạn 2691

• It is therefore important to individualize and prioritize health messages for

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 27: Individualizing ANC messages — đoạn 2692

each individual client. This means selecting topics to discuss at different times in relation to the client’s gestational age and clinical situation.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 27: Individualizing ANC messages — đoạn 2693

• Prioritization is also important because of the limited time available at each

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 27: Individualizing ANC messages — đoạn 2694

contact for client-provider interaction.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 27: Individualizing ANC messages — đoạn 2695

• Present slide 27.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2696

• Ask participants to list the health messages that are best delivered during

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2697

the first trimester, and the second and third trimesters.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2698

• Refer them to table 7.1 in the participant manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2699

• Record their answers on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2700

• Present slide 28 and compare their answers to those on the first trimester

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2701

list.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2702

Slide 29: Recommended counselling topics for the second and third trimesters

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2703

• Present the information on slide 29 and compare it with those provided by

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2704

participants. Fill in any gaps.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2705

• Refer participants to the PCPNC guide, specifically charts M1 and M2, which

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2706

contain information and counselling messages in formats that can also be shared with the woman to take home.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2707

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2708

The purpose of this group activity is for participants to practice their skills in conducting individual or couple counselling with the aid of learning guides and checklists.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2709

• Divide the class into groups of three and assign each group a facilitator.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2710

• Present the instructions for the group role play activity on slides 30 and 31 below.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2711

• Provide each group with a role play scenario and background information on their respective role plays.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2712

Each participant must play the role of either client, midwife or observer.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2713

• Refer participants to the learning guides/checklist in the participant manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2714

A brief plenary discussion will be held at end of the activity. Slides 30 and 31: Class activity – role play

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2715

• Using slides 30 and 31, read out the instructions and clarify any issues.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Recommended counselling topics for the first trimester — đoạn 2716

• When playing the role of client, participants must refer to the “Client

### Nguồn gốc: background information”. — đoạn 2717

• When acting as midwife, participants must refer to the “Counsellor

### Nguồn gốc: background information” and use the ANC learning guide. — đoạn 2718

• When acting as an observer, participants must use the ANC learning guide as

### Nguồn gốc: background information” and use the ANC learning guide. — đoạn 2719

a checklist to record their observations for discussion with the group during the feedback session.

### Nguồn gốc: Role play 1: scenario – Mrs A — đoạn 2720

Mrs. A is a 23-year-old lady reporting to the ANC clinic for her first visit; she is accompanied by her mother-in-law. She is four months pregnant. She lives with her husband and her mother-in-law in the village. Her mother-in-law has been advising her not to eat too much meat and not to take those “bad medicines” supplied at the clinic as they make delivery difficult and harm the babies.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2721

• You are 23 years old.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2722

• This is your first pregnancy and your first visit to the antenatal clinic.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2723

• The midwife has completed her assessment of you and has given you some iron tablets.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2724

• You have never taken iron tablets and are worried that they might harm your baby.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2725

• You tell the midwife that your mother-in-law will not allow you to take the iron tablets.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2726

• You also tell the midwife that your mother-in-law will not allow you to eat red meat during your pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2727

• You have completed your assessment of Mrs A and no problems were detected.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2728

• Her haemoglobin is 12.5g/dl.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2729

• You have given her a three-month supply of iron/folate tablets.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2730

You now need to counsel Mrs A about nutrition and her compliance in taking the iron/folate tablets.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2731

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Role play 1: scenario – Mrs B — đoạn 2732

Mrs B is a regular ANC attendant. She is six months pregnant and is at the clinic for her third visit. She looks well. All her examination findings are normal. Her only concern is that she does not want to have any more children. She thinks that five children are enough.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2733

• You are 30 years old and have five children.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2734

• This is your sixth pregnancy and you do not want to become pregnant again.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2735

• You are in the third trimester of pregnancy and want to talk to the midwife about not becoming pregnant again.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2736

• You have not used a family planning method in the past but have heard other women talk about the methods

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2737

they use.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2738

• You have not yet talked to your husband about not wanting any more children.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2739

• Mrs B is well known to you and is a regular attendant. She is now six months pregnant.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2740

• Mrs B has confided in you that she does not want to become pregnant again.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2741

• She is in the third trimester of pregnancy and is well.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2742

• You need to provide Mrs B with advice and counselling about family planning.

### Nguồn gốc: Role play 1: scenario – Mrs C — đoạn 2743

Mrs C is a new attendant reporting for the first time to the antenatal clinic. You notice that she had been quite interactive during the group counselling session on danger signs and asked a lot of questions. She mentioned that a doctor had told her to deliver her baby in a hospital. She has entered the cubicle for her routine check-up. She had no new health complaints. All her examination findings were normal.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2744

• You are 25 years old. This is your third pregnancy and your first clinic visit for this pregnancy. Your first and

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2745

second babies were born at home and the births were attended by your mother-in-law.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2746

• You bled a lot following the birth of your second baby and by the time your neighbours got you to the hospital,

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2747

you were very ill and had to be given two units of blood transfusions.

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2748

• The attending doctors and nurses told you how lucky you were then to still be alive. They said you should deliver

### Nguồn gốc: Client information — đoạn 2749

in the hospital in your next pregnancy. You are concerned since your village is far from the hospital. A client has informed you that the midwife can be of help in addressing this concern.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2750

• Mrs C is 25 years old. This is her third pregnancy and her first clinic visit for this pregnancy She has never had an

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2751

antenatal check-up. She is five months pregnant.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2752

• Her previous deliveries were all at home and the births were attended by her mother-in-law. She survived PPH

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2753

after the birth of her second child and has been advised to deliver her babies in a hospital.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2754

• She lives in a village about 15 mm from the hospital. You are to counsel and help Mrs. C prepare a birth and

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2755

emergency plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2756

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2757

Figure 7.3: Learning guide/checklist for counsellors and observers

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor information — đoạn 2758

Use the guides below to remind you of the counselling skills. Use the “Remarks” column to record observations or problem areas about the interaction with the client.

### Nguồn gốc: Use two-way communication — đoạn 2759

• Provide the woman ample opportunity to talk about and

### Nguồn gốc: Use two-way communication — đoạn 2760

clarify issues and problems.

### Nguồn gốc: Use two-way communication — đoạn 2761

• Provide the required information to the woman and encourage

### Nguồn gốc: Use two-way communication — đoạn 2762

her to talk about the information provided.

### Nguồn gốc: Build rapport — đoạn 2763

• Apply the guiding principles of counselling to establish a

### Nguồn gốc: Build rapport — đoạn 2764

trusting and caring environment for the woman.

### Nguồn gốc: Build rapport — đoạn 2765

• Put the woman at ease to talk about her situation and to

### Nguồn gốc: Build rapport — đoạn 2766

discuss sensitive topics.

### Nguồn gốc: Demonstrate active listening — đoạn 2767

• Close windows and doors to reduce distractions.

### Nguồn gốc: Demonstrate active listening — đoạn 2768

• Make sure that the woman is seated comfortably.

### Nguồn gốc: Demonstrate active listening — đoạn 2769

• Look at the woman as she talks with you.

### Nguồn gốc: Demonstrate active listening — đoạn 2770

• Use a warm, friendly tone of voice.

### Nguồn gốc: Demonstrate active listening — đoạn 2771

• Use gestures and body language such as nodding and smiling.

### Nguồn gốc: Demonstrate active listening — đoạn 2772

• Use verbal aﬃrmations such as “yes” and “I see”.

### Nguồn gốc: Demonstrate active listening — đoạn 2773

• Ask questions about what the woman has said.

### Nguồn gốc: Demonstrate active listening — đoạn 2774

• Paraphrase what the woman has said, when appropriate.

### Nguồn gốc: Ask questions — đoạn 2775

• Use open-ended questions.

### Nguồn gốc: Ask questions — đoạn 2776

• Ask questions in a non-judgmental way.

### Nguồn gốc: Provide information — đoạn 2777

• Provide clear and understandable information that relates to the

### Nguồn gốc: Provide information — đoạn 2778

woman’s needs.

### Nguồn gốc: Provide information — đoạn 2779

• Encourage the woman to ask questions and clarify anything she

### Nguồn gốc: Provide information — đoạn 2780

has not understood.

### Nguồn gốc: Provide information — đoạn 2781

• Ask the woman to repeat the information in her own words, if

### Nguồn gốc: Provide information — đoạn 2782

necessary.

### Nguồn gốc: Facilitate — đoạn 2783

• Help the woman to find appropriate solutions by asking her to list

### Nguồn gốc: Facilitate — đoạn 2784

possible solutions and consider the advantages and disadvantages of each.

### Nguồn gốc: Facilitate — đoạn 2785

• Keep summarizing the discussion to help keep the discussion

### Nguồn gốc: Facilitate — đoạn 2786

on track and move the woman toward making a decision.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Review and evaluate. — đoạn 2787

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 2788

When each participant has had a chance to play all three roles and group feedback discussions have been completed, bring participants together in a brief plenary discussion to conclude the activity. Use the following questions to facilitate discussions: (a) (b)

### Nguồn gốc: What were some of the skill challenges they encountered? — đoạn 2789

(c) How will they overcome challenges at their respective ANC clinics when conducting similar counselling for their clients? Allow participants to comment on these questions extensively; then summarize lessons learned to conclude the activity. Remind participants that they will have the opportunity to further practice their counselling skills in other training sessions that address specific complications, such as Malaria, anaemia and HIV/

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 32: Key messages — đoạn 2790

End session 7 by presenting these key messages:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 32: Key messages — đoạn 2791

• Antenatal counselling is an interactive process between the health care

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 32: Key messages — đoạn 2792

provider and a pregnant woman and her support persons. It is an important component of ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 32: Key messages — đoạn 2793

• Communication involves the exchange of information and is most

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 32: Key messages — đoạn 2794

productive and effective when it is a two-way process in which each pers on can discuss and clarify issues and problems.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 2795

1.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 2796

Key steps in the counselling process include: (a) assessing the situation; (b) defining the problem, needs or information gaps; (c) generating alternative solutions; (d) prioritizing the solutions; (e) developing a plan; and (f) reviewing and evaluating.

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Individual, couple and group counselling during ANC contacts each have their advantages — đoạn 2797

counselling saves time for the provider. Couple counselling may help couples cope better with their pregnancy, plan together for antenatal care and birth, and strengthen their communications relating to that pregnancy. In individual counselling, the care provider can identify the client’s individual needs and target counselling messages to address those needs. The client is also more likely to discuss sensitive issues.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. With good communication skills, the care provider can establish good rapport, counsel e — đoạn 2798

quality care to the client.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. With good communication skills, the care provider can establish good rapport, counsel e — đoạn 2799

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 3. With good communication skills, the care provider can establish good rapport, counsel e — đoạn 2800

Session 8:

• Flip chart with paper and markers

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2802

• Training course materials: facilitator guide, participant manual

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2803

• Pregnancy, childbirth, postpartum and newborn care (PCPNC) guide

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2804

• Learning guide for ANC counselling

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2805

• Laptop

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2806

• Overhead projector

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2807

• One table and three chairs

### Nguồn gốc: Preparation — đoạn 2808

• Gather training materials in advance.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Aim and objectives — đoạn 2809

(5 Minutes). Aim: To enable participants to support pregnant women and families to prepare birth and emergency plans.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 2810

On completion of this session, participants will be able to:

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 2811

1. explain to the pregnant woman the importance of preparing a birth and

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 2812

emergency plan; and

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 2813

2. assist the pregnant woman and her family to prepare their own birth and

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 2814

emergency plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 2815

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Barriers to skilled attendance at birth — đoạn 2816

• Ask participants “what percentage of women in their communities/

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Barriers to skilled attendance at birth — đoạn 2817

countries deliver with skilled birth attendants or in health facilities”

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Barriers to skilled attendance at birth — đoạn 2818

• Ask “why is it important for pregnant women to deliver with skilled birth

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Barriers to skilled attendance at birth — đoạn 2819

attendants and in health facilities?”.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Barriers to skilled attendance at birth — đoạn 2820

• Note their answers.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Barriers to skilled attendance at birth — đoạn 2821

• Approximately 16–33% of maternal deaths could be averted if pregnant

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Barriers to skilled attendance at birth — đoạn 2822

women received care from skilled attendants.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Barriers to skilled attendance at birth — đoạn 2823

• ANC serves as the entry point into the continuum of maternal care services.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Barriers to skilled attendance at birth — đoạn 2824

• It therefore provides an opportunity for helping women prepare for and use

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Barriers to skilled attendance at birth — đoạn 2825

subsequent maternity care services through careful planning, education and increasing their level of satisfaction with services.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2826

• Developing a birth and emergency plan is a key component of routine ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2827

• Present slide 6 (figure 8.1) to show its importance within the scheme of care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2828

• This session focuses on how to develop this plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2829

• Ask participants if the development of a birth and emergency plan is part of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2830

the ANC they currently provide.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Key steps in routine antenatal care — đoạn 2831

• Listen to their answers, and then continue.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Defining birth preparedness and complication readiness (BP/CR) — đoạn 2832

• Present slide 7 and task a participant to read the definitions on the slide for

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Defining birth preparedness and complication readiness (BP/CR) — đoạn 2833

birth preparedness and complication readiness.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Defining birth preparedness and complication readiness (BP/CR) — đoạn 2834

• Clarify any questions participants may have on these definitions.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Defining birth preparedness and complication readiness (BP/CR) — đoạn 2835

The birth preparedness and complication readiness plan (also known as “birth and emergency plan”) is the document that lets the care provider and support persons of the pregnant woman know her wishes and preferences for how things should be done to manage her when labour or complications occur.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Why is a birth and emergency plan important? — đoạn 2836

following facts:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Why is a birth and emergency plan important? — đoạn 2837

• It is an important global strategy to increase universal access to skilled

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Why is a birth and emergency plan important? — đoạn 2838

attendance at birth.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Why is a birth and emergency plan important? — đoạn 2839

• It is a key component of routine ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Why is a birth and emergency plan important? — đoạn 2840

• It increases the likelihood that needed resources are available and that the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Why is a birth and emergency plan important? — đoạn 2841

woman and her baby will receive appropriate and timely care at the time of birth or complications.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Why is a birth and emergency plan important? — đoạn 2842

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Developing a birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2843

• Ask participants to answer the following two questions:

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Who should be involved in its development?” — đoạn 2844

• Present slide 9 and explain that the plan is developed during ANC

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Who should be involved in its development?” — đoạn 2845

counselling and must be initiated from the first visit.

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Who should be involved in its development?” — đoạn 2846

• It is individualized to reflect the pregnant woman’s situation and resources.

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Who should be involved in its development?” — đoạn 2847

• Also highlight the importance of including family members/support persons.

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Who should be involved in its development?” — đoạn 2848

• Task a participant each to read out the stated reasons for including family

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Who should be involved in its development?” — đoạn 2849

members in the BP/CR planning as outlined in the participant manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Help families develop a birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2850

(20 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Helping families prepare the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2851

• Explain that the care provider must first understand the client and her

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Helping families prepare the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2852

family/support persons’ attitudes and situations to be able to assist them to come up with the best BP/CR plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Helping families prepare the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2853

• She must ask a number of questions to get relevant information.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Helping families prepare the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2854

• he then provides advice and additional information as needed to help the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Helping families prepare the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2855

families and client make their decisions about what they would do.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing the birth plan — đoạn 2856

• Ask participants to list some questions they would ask clients to better

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing the birth plan — đoạn 2857

understand their attitudes and situations regarding delivery at a health facility.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing the birth plan — đoạn 2858

• Note their answers on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing the birth plan — đoạn 2859

• Present slide 11 and compare the questions listed to those provided by

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing the birth plan — đoạn 2860

participants. Fill in any gaps.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing the birth plan — đoạn 2861

• Refer participants to the page on preparing the birth plan in their manual

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing the birth plan — đoạn 2862

(Birth preparedness).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing the birth plan — đoạn 2863

• Task a participant each to read one question and its related advice.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing the birth plan — đoạn 2864

• Discuss and clarify any concerns that arise.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Developing the emergency plan — đoạn 2865

• Complications are not always predictable and can occur at any time.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Developing the emergency plan — đoạn 2866

• Use the information on slide 12 to list and explain the relevant questions to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Developing the emergency plan — đoạn 2867

ask to determine and plan a client’s emergency preparedness.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Developing the emergency plan — đoạn 2868

• Emphasize the importance of reminding clients that complications can

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Developing the emergency plan — đoạn 2869

occur on several occasions during the nine-month-long pregnancy period. They must therefore have extra funding and plans for such occasions.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Developing the emergency plan — đoạn 2870

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Danger signs during pregnancy — đoạn 2871

• For a client and her family to implement a birth and emergency plan, they

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Danger signs during pregnancy — đoạn 2872

would first have to recognize these situations. An essential component of preparing a plan is to educated clients about the signs and symptoms of labour and danger signs.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Danger signs during pregnancy — đoạn 2873

• First, ask participants to list all danger sign/symptoms they know.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Danger signs during pregnancy — đoạn 2874

• Write these on a flip chart. Then ask them to sort these signs into two groups:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Danger signs during pregnancy — đoạn 2875

“Go to hospital immediately” or “Go to hospital as soon as possible”.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Danger signs during pregnancy — đoạn 2876

• Present slide 13 and discuss the answers.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Danger signs during pregnancy — đoạn 2877

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Danger signs during pregnancy — đoạn 2878

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Danger signs during pregnancy — đoạn 2879

Understanding the sociocultural environments from which clients come makes it easier for care providers to include additional relevant questions and advice in their BP/CR planning. For example, in a culture where men are not allowed into labour wards but must make decisions for cesarean section, the health care provider may advise the client to have an additional support person including the husband.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Danger signs during pregnancy — đoạn 2880

Table 8.3 in the participant manual provides some possible advice and solutions to address common challenges that clients and their families experience in the development of BP/CR plans.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2881

• When a plan has been reached with the client and family, it is important that

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2882

this is documented and referred to at each antenatal visit, for review and revision.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2883

• The record also serves as a reminder to the client and family of what to do

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2884

when the anticipated situation arises.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2885

• Refer participants to the sample form in their manual. Ask if they have such

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2886

forms in their settings and how they differ from this sample. • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2887

Note that the best-laid plans do not always go according to plan. Remind clients to always check if their agreed plan is implementable, for example, village transport is still available and functional. Having alternate plans may be necessary for some key issues such as: what to do if the first choice of transport fails, or what to do at home if the baby is delivered before the clinic is reached, etc.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2888

These scenarios must all be considered during review and further development of the plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2889

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2890

The objective of this activity is to practice developing a birth and emergency plan, taking into account local and cultural beliefs and practices. Present the instructions and provide the role play scenarios to the groups (or refer them to the relevant page in their manual).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Document and review the birth and emergency plan — đoạn 2891

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024
