### Nguồn gốc: Role play scenario — đoạn 2907

• An ANC provider (midwife) is undertaking counselling on birth preparedness and complication readiness

### Nguồn gốc: Role play scenario — đoạn 2908

with a client and support person at the clinic.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2909

You are an experienced midwife at the antenatal clinic. You will require the following: 1. a sample birth and emergency plan form 2. a counselling guide for BPCR ( see PCPNC M2) or use the guide in the box below:

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2910

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2911

Figure 8.5: Counselling guide for developing a birth and emergency plan (for use by counsellor and observers) In order to help a woman and her family develop their individualized birth and emergency plan, ask questions first; then listen to their answers to understand their attitudes and situation. Provide advice based on their situation and the answers they give, and document the agreed plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2912

When planning for facility birth – consider the following:

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2913

• Where will you go to deliver?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2914

• How will you get there?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2915

• How will you pay for transport to get there?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2916

• How much will it cost to deliver at the facility and how will you pay for this?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2917

• Can you start saving for these costs now?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2918

• Who will go with you and support you during labour and delivery?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2919

• Who will help take care of your home and other children while you are away?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2920

Gather and bring along the following items to the facility:

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2921

• home-based maternal record

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2922

• supplies for delivery (provide the list)

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2923

• clean clothes for you and the baby.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2924

• food and water for you and the support person.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2925

To plan for an emergency, consider:

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2926

• What are the danger signs?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2927

• Where should you go if a danger sign occurs?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2928

• How will you get there?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2929

• Will you have to pay for transport to get there and how much will it cost?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2930

• What costs will you have to pay at the health centre and how will you pay for these costs?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2931

• Can you start saving for these possible costs now?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2932

• Who will go with you to the health centre?

### Nguồn gốc: Counsellor background information — đoạn 2933

• Who will help to care for your home and other children while you are away?

### Nguồn gốc: Client and support persons: background information — đoạn 2934

(Select participants to play client and support roles in each of the scenarios)

### Nguồn gốc: Client 1: Madam A — đoạn 2935

She is 17 years old, with her first pregnancy. This is her first ANC visit. She is single and unemployed and lives in a slum area in same town as the clinic.

### Nguồn gốc: Client 2: Mrs B — đoạn 2936

She is 35 years old and a farmer. She has six children. She lives with her mother-in-law and her husband about 25 km away. This is her seventh pregnancy. She delivered all her babies at home and says she prefers home births.

### Nguồn gốc: Client 3: Mrs C — đoạn 2937

She is 35 years old and a teacher. She is pregnant with her third child. Her previous deliveries were all in this hospital. However, she was unhappy with the way she was treated when she requested to be allowed to have a squatting position for delivery at her last delivery.

### Nguồn gốc: Client 3: Mrs C — đoạn 2938

Support person: her husband. Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

have been completed, bring participants together in a brief plenary discussion to conclude the activity.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. How will they overcome challenges at their respective ANC clinics when conducting simil — đoạn 2942

counselling for their clients?

• Allow participants to comment on these questions extensively.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. How will they overcome challenges at their respective ANC clinics when conducting simil — đoạn 2944

• Then summarize lessons learned to conclude the activity.

### Nguồn gốc: Establishing links with the community to support birth and emergency — đoạn 2945

plans

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Establishing links with the community — đoạn 2946

• Ask participants to list groups and organizations within their communities

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Establishing links with the community — đoạn 2947

that work with and support women, especially during pregnancy. How have they engaged these groups?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Establishing links with the community — đoạn 2948

• Emphasize that establishing links with the community is essential to support

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Establishing links with the community — đoạn 2949

birth and emergency planning.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Establishing links with the community — đoạn 2950

• Using slide 18, give examples of how communities can support women’s BP/

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Establishing links with the community — đoạn 2951

CR plans.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Establishing links with the community — đoạn 2952

• Challenge ANC care providers to explore other innovative ways to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Establishing links with the community — đoạn 2953

encourage communities to play their role in the BP/CR strategy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Key messages — đoạn 2954

• Developing a birth and emergency plan is a key component of ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Key messages — đoạn 2955

• Having a birth plan can reduce confusion at the time of birth or

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Key messages — đoạn 2956

emergency and increase the chance that the woman and her baby will receive appropriate and timely care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Key messages — đoạn 2957

• In preparing for birth, it may be important to include the woman’s

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Key messages — đoạn 2958

family members, her partner, or other decision-makers in her life. Helping the family prepare their own birth and emergency plan involves ongoing

### Nguồn gốc: discussion with the pregnant woman and her family to help them decide — đoạn 2959

where to give birth, organize the resources they need for the birth, and decide what they will do in an emergency situation.

### Nguồn gốc: discussion with the pregnant woman and her family to help them decide — đoạn 2960

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Session 9: Malaria prevention — đoạn 2961

and control in pregnancy Before teaching this session, you should familiarize yourself with the national policy/protocols for prevention and control of malaria in pregnancy. Check that the guidelines for malaria in pregnancy prevention and control in this session are consistent with the national guidelines, and if not, revise the text and slides as needed.

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2966

Organization recommendations on antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience, April 2017

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2967

• National malaria guidelines and protocols (up to date)

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 2968

• National antenatal care guidelines (up to date)

• Laptop

• Gather training materials in advance

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Aim and objectives — đoạn 2976

(5 minutes). Aim: To help participants understand the importance of malaria in pregnancy and develop skills for malaria prevention and case management in pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: At the end of the session, participants will be able to: — đoạn 2977

1. describe the burden and consequences of malaria in the pregnant woman

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: At the end of the session, participants will be able to: — đoạn 2978

and her baby;

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: At the end of the session, participants will be able to: — đoạn 2979

2. counsel pregnant women on ways to prevent malaria in pregnancy;

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: At the end of the session, participants will be able to: — đoạn 2980

3. identify and correctly manage and/or refer cases of malaria in pregnancy;

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: At the end of the session, participants will be able to: — đoạn 2981

and

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: At the end of the session, participants will be able to: — đoạn 2982

4. describe the severe consequences of malaria in pregnant women when

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: At the end of the session, participants will be able to: — đoạn 2983

associated with HIV, sickle cell disease or nutritional anaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: Burden of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 2984

Introduce this topic by asking how many participants have ever managed a client with malaria in pregnancy; and how many clients with this condition (MiP) they see in a month in their facilities. Note their answers.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 4 and 5: Burden of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 2985

• Malaria infection during pregnancy is an enormous public health problem,

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 4 and 5: Burden of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 2986

with substantial risks for the mother, her foetus and the neonate.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 4 and 5: Burden of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 2987

• Women are four times as likely to get sick from malaria if they are pregnant

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 4 and 5: Burden of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 2988

and twice as likely to die from the disease.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 4 and 5: Burden of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 2989

• Malaria and pregnancy are mutually aggravating conditions. The physiological

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 4 and 5: Burden of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 2990

changes of pregnancy and the pathological changes due to malaria have a synergistic effect on the course of each other, thus making life difficult for the mother, the child and the treating physician.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 4 and 5: Burden of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 2991

• In Africa, 30 million women living in malaria-endemic areas become pregnant

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 4 and 5: Burden of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 2992

each year. For these women, malaria is a threat both to themselves and to their babies, with up to 200 000 newborn deaths.

### Nguồn gốc: Malaria and its transmission (15 minutes) — đoạn 2993

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–8: Malaria transmission — đoạn 2994

• Present slides 6–8 and explain the following facts (if not well understood).

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–8: Malaria transmission — đoạn 2995

• Malaria is an infection caused by a protozoan of the Plasmodium

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–8: Malaria transmission — đoạn 2996

family. The most common (responsible for over 90% of infections) and most dangerous in sub-Saharan Africa is the P. falciparum.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–8: Malaria transmission — đoạn 2997

• The parasite is transmitted through the bite of an infected female

### Nguồn gốc: Anopheles mosquito. When the infected female mosquito bites its victim, — đoạn 2998

it injects the Plasmodium parasites into the victim’s blood vessels. These parasites enter red blood cells (RBCs) and the liver and multiply. When they infect and later burst out of the red blood cells, they damage these cells. New parasites released go on to attack newer red blood cells in a vicious cycle or are picked up by another mosquito, to be transported to another victim.

### Nguồn gốc: Anopheles mosquito. When the infected female mosquito bites its victim, — đoạn 2999

• During pregnancy, the placenta becomes an additional infection site for the

### Nguồn gốc: Anopheles mosquito. When the infected female mosquito bites its victim, — đoạn 3000

Plasmodium parasite, leading to consequences for the pregnancy too.

### Nguồn gốc: Present the transmission cycle using slide 8 — đoạn 3001

• Depending on the intensity of disease transmission, areas are described

### Nguồn gốc: Present the transmission cycle using slide 8 — đoạn 3002

as being endemic or non-endemic for malaria . Many areas in subSaharan Africa are endemic for malaria. In stable endemic areas, malaria transmission occurs all year round, with individuals continually exposed to the parasite.

### Nguồn gốc: Present the transmission cycle using slide 8 — đoạn 3003

• Repeated exposure develops immunity, which results in less severe

### Nguồn gốc: Present the transmission cycle using slide 8 — đoạn 3004

clinical malaria, particularly in adults. Only the very young (children under 5 years of age), pregnant women and visitors to the area are at risk. However, in non-endemic areas, people have lower immunities and epidemics may occur with devastating consequences for all.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Mechanisms leading to severe malaria and its complications — đoạn 3005

(optional)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Mechanisms leading to severe malaria and its complications — đoạn 3006

• The continuing breakdown of red blood cells leads to anaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Mechanisms leading to severe malaria and its complications — đoạn 3007

• You may present slide 9 to explain further the pathological changes that

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Mechanisms leading to severe malaria and its complications — đoạn 3008

occur with the infection of RBCs by the Plasmodium parasite and how these lead to the complications that ensue.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Why are pregnant women more susceptible to malaria? — đoạn 3009

• Present slide 10. Ask participants to read the listed reasons why pregnant

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Why are pregnant women more susceptible to malaria? — đoạn 3010

women are more susceptible to malaria infection.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Why are pregnant women more susceptible to malaria? — đoạn 3011

• Explain these points further and highlight the fact that malaria infection in

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Why are pregnant women more susceptible to malaria? — đoạn 3012

pregnancy results in more frequent and severe clinical illness in the mother because of her increased vulnerability.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Why are pregnant women more susceptible to malaria? — đoạn 3013

• Note that this vulnerability is particularly greatest during first

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Why are pregnant women more susceptible to malaria? — đoạn 3014

pregnancies.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Why are pregnant women more susceptible to malaria? — đoạn 3015

• Women with subsequent pregnancies can develop and mount improved

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Why are pregnant women more susceptible to malaria? — đoạn 3016

immune responses against malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Why are pregnant women more susceptible to malaria? — đoạn 3017

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3018

• Present slide 11.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3019

• During pregnancy, malaria infection often leads to debilitating anaemia

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3020

and severe complications such as cerebral malaria and even death. The pregnancy may end in miscarriage and/or the foetus may suffer growth retardation or stillbirth.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3021

• Complications in the newborn include prematurity, low birth weight and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3022

congenital malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3023

• Explain that these complications are due to the higher rates of parasitaemia

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3024

(parasite loads) due to weakened resistance, additional sites for parasite development in the placenta, and the disruption of placenta function.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3025

• For women with pre-existing conditions such as HIV, sickle cell anaemia or

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3026

pre-existing malarial anaemia leads to even graver outcomes.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3027

• Present slide 12 and refer participants to Table 9.2 in the participant manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3028

• Compare the list on the slide to the one provided by participants on the flip

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3029

chart and fill in any gaps.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3030

Strategies to reduce the burden of malaria in pregnancy (60 minutes) • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3031

Preventing and controlling malaria in pregnancy is a major global priority. All health care providers caring for pregnant women in sub-Saharan Africa must be proficient in the prevention and management of malaria in pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3032

Slide 13: Strategies to reduce the burden of malaria in pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3033

• Present slide 13.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3034

• Refer to Figure 9.3 in the participant manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3035

• A three-pronged approach is currently recommended to reduce the burden

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3036

of malaria infection among pregnant women:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3037

1. use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN);

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3038

2. intermittent preventive treatment in pregnancy (IPTp); and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Adverse consequences of malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3039

3. case management of malaria illness.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3040

• Sleeping under an insecticide-treated bed net is probably the most effective

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3041

method for preventing mosquito bites at night when the pregnant woman is asleep.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3042

• ITNs prevent mosquito bites by repelling mosquitoes or killing them if they

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3043

land on the net. All pregnant women should be counselled to obtain and sleep under an ITN.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3044

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3045

Slides 15–18: Intermittent preventive treatment in pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3046

• Present slide 15.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3047

• The IPTp strategy assumes that every pregnant woman living in areas of high

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3048

malaria transmission has malaria parasites in her blood or placenta, whether she has symptoms of malaria or not.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3049

• Pregnant women with malaria infection but no symptoms may still suffer

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Use of insecticide-treated nets (ITN) — đoạn 3050

health consequences such as anaemia, low birth weight and foetal death.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: IPTp-SP dosing — đoạn 3051

• Emphasize that IPTp-SP and SP can be safely administered from the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: IPTp-SP dosing — đoạn 3052

beginning of the second trimester until delivery.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: IPTp-SP dosing — đoạn 3053

• Refer to Table 9.3 in the participant manual and give five minutes for

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: IPTp-SP dosing — đoạn 3054

participants to read.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: IPTp-SP dosing — đoạn 3055

• Explain that pregnant women should receive their first dose of IPTp-SP as

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: IPTp-SP dosing — đoạn 3056

early as possible at the beginning of the second trimester, defined as 13 weeks gestation.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: IPTp-SP dosing — đoạn 3057

• The period between 13 and 20 weeks is critical for irreversible

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: IPTp-SP dosing — đoạn 3058

negative consequences of MiP, when parasite densities are highest and major benefits can be achieved from malaria prevention.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: IPTp-SP dosing — đoạn 3059

• Pregnant women should receive an additional dose of IPTp-SP at each

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: IPTp-SP dosing — đoạn 3060

contact with a health care worker trained to deliver IPTp-SP until the time of delivery, ensuring that doses of IPTp-SP are administered at least one month apart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Precautions for SP administration — đoạn 3061

• Although studies have reported no major adverse reactions to SP in mother

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Precautions for SP administration — đoạn 3062

or baby, potential complications are still possible.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Precautions for SP administration — đoạn 3063

• It is important to screen all mothers prior to administering each SP dose.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Precautions for SP administration — đoạn 3064

• If LMP is unknown, ensure that the client is in the second or third trimester

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Precautions for SP administration — đoạn 3065

by enquiring if quickening has occurred.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Precautions for SP administration — đoạn 3066

• Screen the pregnant woman for history of allergy to sulphur. Ask the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Precautions for SP administration — đoạn 3067

pregnant woman if she has been recently treated with SP or another sulphur-containing medicine (within the past month). If so, wait until one month has passed before giving SP.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Precautions for SP administration — đoạn 3068

• Check for G6PD defect, unexplained recurrent jaundice, and severe liver

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Precautions for SP administration — đoạn 3069

disease. If present, do not give IPTp-SP.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Other protective measures — đoạn 3070

• When a woman cannot take SP, it is even more important for her to avoid

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Other protective measures — đoạn 3071

mosquito bites that could transmit malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Other protective measures — đoạn 3072

• Advise her on her increased risk from malaria and on other measures to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Other protective measures — đoạn 3073

prevent mosquito bites.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Other protective measures — đoạn 3074

• Discuss the additional measures to prevent malaria infection listed on the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Other protective measures — đoạn 3075

slide.

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3076

•

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3077

•

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3078

Early diagnosis and treatment of malaria reduces disease and prevents deaths. It also contributes to reducing malaria transmission. Despite preventive measures, some pregnant women will still become infected with malaria and present with clinical symptoms.

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3079

These women must be promptly and adequately treated to prevent the development of severe malaria with life-threatening consequences, including maternal and/or foetal death.

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3080

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3081

Slides 20 and 21: Malaria case management and diagnosis

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3082

• Malaria is identified by its signs and symptoms and through laboratory

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3083

investigation. In areas where malaria is endemic, many people know the symptoms of malaria and may diagnose the problem and even attempt to treat themselves before reporting to the clinic.

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3084

• Using clinical decision-making skills, care providers can diagnose malaria

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3085

by gathering information through history, examination and confirmatory laboratory testing where possible.

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3086

Malaria is classified into clinical categories:

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3087

• uncomplicated and complicated (severe) malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3088

• Uncomplicated malaria is defined as symptomatic malaria without signs

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3089

of vital organ involvement. The signs and symptoms of uncomplicated malaria are non-specific. Present slide 21 and task a participant to read out the clinical signs and symptoms of uncomplicated malaria (Table 9.4).

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3090

• Severe (or complicated) malaria is defined as symptomatic malaria with signs

### Nguồn gốc: Case management of malaria illness in pregnancy — đoạn 3091

of vital organ dysfunction, either clinical or laboratory.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Complicated or severe malaria — đoạn 3092

• Present slide 22 and task another participant to read out the signs and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Complicated or severe malaria — đoạn 3093

symptoms of complicated/severe malaria. (table 9.4)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Complicated or severe malaria — đoạn 3094

• Ask participants to highlight the differences between the two categories.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Complicated or severe malaria — đoạn 3095

Point out that the main differences are that the signs of severe malaria are those indicating vital organ involvement, whereas the signs of uncomplicated malaria are non-specific.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Complicated or severe malaria — đoạn 3096

• Emphasize that when a midwife sees a woman who has malaria, he/she

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Complicated or severe malaria — đoạn 3097

must distinguish between uncomplicated malaria and severe malaria, to ensure that the woman receives the correct treatment.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Laboratory investigation — đoạn 3098

• Ask participants which laboratory test they perform to confirm diagnosis of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Laboratory investigation — đoạn 3099

malaria. Do they always perform the test prior to treatment?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Laboratory investigation — đoạn 3100

• Present slide 23.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Laboratory investigation — đoạn 3101

• Emphasize that all cases of suspected malaria should be confirmed using

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Laboratory investigation — đoạn 3102

parasite-based diagnostic testing.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Laboratory investigation — đoạn 3103

• However, the absence of laboratory tests should not prevent the clinical

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Laboratory investigation — đoạn 3104

diagnosis and treatment of malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 24: Excluding other causes of fever during pregnancy — đoạn 3105

• Ask participants to list other common conditions that they see which may

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 24: Excluding other causes of fever during pregnancy — đoạn 3106

be mistaken for malaria and considered as differential diagnosis. Write down their answers on the flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 24: Excluding other causes of fever during pregnancy — đoạn 3107

• Present slide 24 and compare the answers to those provided on the flip

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 24: Excluding other causes of fever during pregnancy — đoạn 3108

chart. Fill in any gaps.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 24: Excluding other causes of fever during pregnancy — đoạn 3109

• Ask participants to explain how they will distinguish these conditions from

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 24: Excluding other causes of fever during pregnancy — đoạn 3110

malaria. Refer them to the PCPNC guide (see PCPNC Chart C8).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 24: Excluding other causes of fever during pregnancy — đoạn 3111

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Treating uncomplicated malaria during pregnancy — đoạn 3112

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Treating uncomplicated malaria during pregnancy — đoạn 3113

•

### Nguồn gốc: Treating uncomplicated malaria during pregnancy — đoạn 3114

After the health care provider has diagnosed malaria and determined whether it is uncomplicated or severe, he/she must provide care promptly. If the diagnosis is uncomplicated malaria, the objective of the treatment is to cure the infection as rapidly as possible. This prevents progression to severe malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Treating uncomplicated malaria during pregnancy — đoạn 3115

Prompt cure of the infection also achieves the public health goal of reducing transmission of malaria infection. Refer participants to Table 9.5 in their manual (see below), which deals with steps to treat uncomplicated malaria. instructions) in this table. Allow participants to ask questions and clarify any challenges.

### Nguồn gốc: Treating uncomplicated malaria during pregnancy — đoạn 3116

Table 9.5: Management of uncomplicated malaria during pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Give medicines — đoạn 3117

to treat malaria

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 3118

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions — đoạn 3119

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Give an — đoạn 3120

antipyretic

### Nguồn gốc: Give an — đoạn 3121

•

### Nguồn gốc: Advise the — đoạn 3122

pregnant woman

### Nguồn gốc: Refer to national guidelines for the recommended medicines and — đoạn 3123

dosing regimens for treatment of malaria in pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Usually, any client diagnosed with malaria during any trimester of — đoạn 3124

pregnancy can be treated with quinine. Artemisinin-based combination therapy, such as artesunateamodiaquine or artesunate-lumefantrine, is recommended for use only in the second or third trimesters of pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Give the pregnant woman the recommended medicine and — đoạn 3125

number of doses for a complete treatment. Ask her to take the first dose while you are watching. Such as paracetamol to lower fever: 1 gram (2 tablets) every 6 hours, if needed. Teach the woman how and when to take the antimalarial medicines. Advise the woman to: − follow the instructions to take all the antimalarial medicines; − take paracetamol to lower the body temperature, if needed; − continue taking iron/folate tablets and consume iron-rich foods to prevent or treat malarial anaemia; and − sleep under an ITN and use other measures to prevent mosquito bites.

### Nguồn gốc: Give the pregnant woman the recommended medicine and — đoạn 3126

Answer any questions that she may have.

### Nguồn gốc: Ask her to come — đoạn 3127

for a follow-up visit

### Nguồn gốc: Ask her to come — đoạn 3128

•

### Nguồn gốc: Ensure that the woman knows the danger signs during — đoạn 3129

pregnancy. Advise her to return to the facility for a follow-up visit after two days, or sooner if she observes any danger signs or her condition worsens.

### Nguồn gốc: At the follow-up — đoạn 3130

visit, check that her symptoms have improved

### Nguồn gốc: At the follow-up — đoạn 3131

•

### Nguồn gốc: At the follow-up — đoạn 3132

If her symptoms have improved, tell her when to return for her next ANC visit. If her symptoms have not improved or have worsened, or if she has any symptoms that suggest severe malaria, stabilize and refer the woman to hospital immediately.

### Nguồn gốc: At the follow-up — đoạn 3133

•

### Nguồn gốc: One month after • — đoạn 3134

completion of the malaria treatment, give or resume IPTp

### Nguồn gốc: One month after • — đoạn 3135

Give the first dose of IPTp with SP at the next visit if she has not previously received it. Follow the IPTp schedule to give the next dose if IPTp was started previously.

### Nguồn gốc: One month after • — đoạn 3136

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Management of severe malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3137

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Management of severe malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3138

•

### Nguồn gốc: Management of severe malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3139

•

### Nguồn gốc: Management of severe malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3140

•

### Nguồn gốc: Management of severe malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3141

The objective of antimalarial treatment in severe malaria is to prevent maternal death. The risk of death from severe malaria is greatest in the first 24 hours. It is therefore recommended that the affected patient be stabilized and referred immediately to a hospital.

### Nguồn gốc: Management of severe malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3142

o Ask participants if they refer all such patients to hospital. Note their answers.

### Nguồn gốc: Management of severe malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3143

Explain that the treatment of a pregnant woman with malaria in a hospital is important as it will include checking for and managing life-threatening complications relating to organ dysfunction such as severe anaemia, hypoglycaemia, and kidney and pulmonary malfunction.

### Nguồn gốc: Management of severe malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3144

Appendix 6 provides information on medicines and procedures for the treatment of severe malaria in a hospital. Parenteral antimalarials are required for the treatment of severe malaria. Give participants five minutes to review Appendix 6 in their manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Management of severe malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 3145

However, the woman should be managed according to national treatment protocols in place. If possible, prereferral treatment should include the first dose of quinine and paracetamol if she can swallow tablets. Remember that a woman in the first trimester of pregnancy should not be given artemether. A care provider must also accompany the sick woman to the hospital.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 25: Treating malaria during pregnancy — đoạn 3146

• Present slide 25 to summarize the management and treatment guidelines

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 25: Treating malaria during pregnancy — đoạn 3147

for uncomplicated and severe malaria highlighting the differences.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 25: Treating malaria during pregnancy — đoạn 3148

treatment (30 minutes)

• Present slide 26 and inform the class that in this individual class exercise,

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Case studies and class activity instructions — đoạn 3150

they will use information provided in case studies to diagnose malaria and specify the appropriate treatment for the case.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Case studies and class activity instructions — đoạn 3151

• Present instructions for the activity and ensure that all participants

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Case studies and class activity instructions — đoạn 3152

understand the assignment.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Case studies and class activity instructions — đoạn 3153

• Refer them to case studies 1 and 2 on pages …. of their manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Case studies and class activity instructions — đoạn 3154

• Optional – present slides 27, 28, 29 and 30 (read out the case studies and related questions).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 26: Case studies and class activity instructions — đoạn 3155

Allow the class to work on the assignment for 30 minutes.

present the correct answers using the answer sheets below. Allow the class to discuss the answers and clarify any discrepancies.

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 1: Madam Fati — đoạn 3159

Part A: Fati is 30 years old. She is approximately 20 weeks pregnant with her second baby. She has come to the antenatal clinic for her first ANC visit complaining of severe headache and fever. A “Quick check” was performed and with no emergency signs found. Fati and her family moved to the area six months ago. She has never suffered from malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Questions — đoạn 3160

1.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3161

The assessment should include all the recommended steps regarding the first antenatal visit, including:

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3162

• history (personal information, menstrual and contraceptive history, present pregnancy, obstetric history,

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3163

medical and medication history, daily habits and lifestyle);

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3164

• physical examination (including general well-being and BP, visual breast examination, abdominal and genital

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3165

examination); and

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3166

• testing (including haemoglobin, syphilis screening, HIV testing after VCT and consent, blood group and Rh if

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3167

available, urine for protein, urine for glucose if indicated). 2.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3168

What aspects of Fati’s physical examination will help you identify her problems and needs?

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3169

Fati’s general appearance, her blood pressure, temperature, respiration, pulse, pallor in eyelids (to check for anaemia), signs of dehydration (loose dry skin, sunken eyes) and abdominal examination (for fundal height, position and lie, and foetal heart sounds) will help identify her problems and needs.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3170

3.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3171

What screening procedures and laboratory tests will you include (if available) in your assessment of

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3172

Check: haemoglobin for anaemia; malaria test; urine for protein (if blood pressure is greater than 140/90 mmHg, to rule out pre-eclampsia). Part B: After assessing Fati, your main findings include the following: •

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3173

•

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3174

•

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3175

History: Fati states that she has been well during this her pregnancy and only developed fever and headache yesterday morning. She says that she does not have any other symptoms such as cough, painful urination, abdominal pain, or leaking of fluid. She has not had convulsions or loss of consciousness. She has not taken any medication. She does not have a bed net.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3176

Physical examination: Fati’s temperature is 38.7 oC; her blood pressure is 122/68 mmHg; her pulse rate is 80 beats per minute; and her respiration rate is 18 breaths per minute. Fati is not pale. Her fundal height is 23 cm (which is compatible with the dates of her last menstrual period) and foetal heart tones are 140 beats per minute.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3177

Laboratory testing: Her haemoglobin is 10.5 g/dl. The thick blood film test for malaria is positive.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3178

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Questions — đoạn 3179

4.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3180

She has uncomplicated malaria and anaemia, both of which need to be treated. Since she recently moved into the area, she needs education on malaria prevention, and a bed net. She has just begun her second trimester of pregnancy and needs ANC. 5.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3181

• • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3182

Treatment for malaria with the recommended antimalarial medicine and number of doses for a complete treatment. Also, paracetamol to lower the fever. Advice on how and when to take the antimalarial medicines and paracetamol and to return for follow-up after two days or sooner if she gets worse.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3183

Treatment for anaemia with iron and folic acid twice a day. ANC, including developing a birth and emergency plan with the woman, and after screening, tetanus immunization and deworming with mebendazole. Counselling on pertinent health issues, including malaria prevention, nutrition, self-care, safer sex, preventive medicines, quitting smoking and alcohol, and taking only medicines given by a health care provider.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3184

At her next ANC visit that occurs one month or more after malaria treatment has been completed, give IPTp-SP.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Victoria — đoạn 3185

•

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Victoria — đoạn 3186

•

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Victoria — đoạn 3187

•

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Victoria — đoạn 3188

Victoria is 24 years old. She reported at the antenatal clinic looking very ill and weak. During “Quick check”, she told the care provider at the antenatal clinic that she was three months pregnant and had been ill with fever, headaches, body aches, dark urine and vomiting for the past four days. She had taken some paracetamol tablets but saw no improvement. She has no cough, neck pains or painful urination. Her stools are normal, and she has no abdominal pain.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Victoria — đoạn 3189

Upon rapid assessment, you note the following: she has slight yellowish discoloration of the eyes; she is pale; and her temperature is 39 oC. Her tongue is dry, and her eyes are slightly sunken. Her blood pressure is 100/60 mmHg, her pulse 100/min, and volume weak. Her uterus is felt just above the pubis.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Victoria — đoạn 3190

You suspect she has severe malaria since her address indicates that she lives in the slum area of the city where malaria is a problem, and her signs indicate vital organ involvement. You perform a rapid diagnostic test for malaria, and it is positive.

### Nguồn gốc: Questions — đoạn 3191

1.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3192

Haemoglobin to determine if she is anaemic. HIV test if she has not tested in last three months and she consents.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3193

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Victoria (continued) — đoạn 3194

2.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3195

She has severe malaria, so she needs to be stabilized and referred promptly to a hospital. If she can swallow, she will be given a first dose of oral quinine and paracetamol. 3.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3196

IV line with dextrose 5% solution and quinine if she is vomiting. 4.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3197

What advice would you give to Victoria after treatment and recovery from malaria?

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3198

Will educate her on the causes of malaria and how to prevent it by sleeping under an insecticide-treated bed net and avoiding mosquito bites. Will also advise her to return for IPTp-SP after one month; take one iron and folic acid tablet daily, or if she is anaemic, twice daily; and to eat foods high in iron (describe what those foods are).

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3199

5.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3200

What measures would you take to ensure that Victoria recovers completely and remains healthy?

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3201

Have her come for regular ANC visits (including preventive medicines); continue refilling her iron and folic acid tablets; encourage her to eat a healthy diet as advised and to practice safer sex; and prepare a birth and emergency plan with her and her family.

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3202

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Answer — đoạn 3203

Preventing and managing malaria in pregnant women with special high-risk conditions (20 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 31: Malaria in pregnant women with special high-risk conditions — đoạn 3204

• Some women in the sub-region have additional health conditions that make

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 31: Malaria in pregnant women with special high-risk conditions — đoạn 3205

them even more susceptible to malaria infection during pregnancy. This further aggravates the situation with severer consequences for the mother and her baby.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 31: Malaria in pregnant women with special high-risk conditions — đoạn 3206

• Three such conditions include:

### Nguồn gốc: 1. HIV — đoạn 3207

2. sickle cell disease

### Nguồn gốc: 1. HIV — đoạn 3208

3. pre-existing nutritional anaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3209

• Ask participants why they think the conditions in slide 31 are special risks for

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3210

malaria in pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3211

• Listen to their answers and present slides 32 and 33 to explain this further.

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3212

Slides 34, 35 and 36: Malaria-related care for women with special risk conditions Closer vigilance is required during antenatal care for women with the above three high-risk conditions for malaria in pregnancy. •

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3213

• • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3214

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3215

•

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3216

When seen at all antenatal contacts, the provider must probe for symptoms suggestive of malaria and assess carefully for the presence of fever, anaemia and the well-being of the baby. Counsel the woman and her family about her increased risk for severe malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3217

Perform a blood film or rapid test for malaria parasites at the first visit and at all later visits if possible. Give her information on other protective measures against mosquito bites. Counsel her on the additional danger signs of malaria in pregnancy for women with her pre-existing condition (for example, signs of sickling crises and severe anaemia) Counsel her to report early to the health facility if she has any symptoms of malaria. She should avoid self-treatment at home.

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3218

Emphasize that if malaria develops, the care provider must quickly confirm the diagnosis – if possible, initiate treatments and refer her promptly to a hospital for further management. Note that in these women, malaria can rapidly progress from uncomplicated to severe with life-threatening effects within a few hours.

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3219

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3220

(30 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3221

Slide 37: Class activity 2 – counselling women with special risk conditions for malaria in pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3222

• The purpose of this role play is to demonstrate and observe and practice

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3223

counselling a client who has a special risk condition about preventing malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3224

• You may choose to conduct this role play as a “play” performed by three

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3225

participants for the entire class, as described below, or as a practice session in which everyone participates.

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3226

• If you choose the latter, organize participants into groups of three and ask

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3227

them to do the role play at the same time. If time allows, ask them to repeat it two or three times, playing different roles each time.

### Nguồn gốc: Reasons why women with these conditions remain at high risk — đoạn 3228

• Present slide 37 to give instructions to participants.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for demonstration and practice — đoạn 3229

• Select three participants to perform the following roles:

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for demonstration and practice — đoạn 3230

1. health care provider (midwife)

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for demonstration and practice — đoạn 3231

2. antenatal client with special need (sickle cell anaemia in pregnancy)

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Support person (for example, family member) — đoạn 3232

• Task the three participants taking part in the role play to read about their roles in the participant manual

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Support person (for example, family member) — đoạn 3233

and then take a few minutes to prepare. Also see charts below.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Support person (for example, family member) — đoạn 3234

• The rest of the participants will be observers. Observers should also prepare by reviewing the

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Support person (for example, family member) — đoạn 3235

Learning guide for antenatal counselling, the instructions on preventing malaria in women with special risk conditions, and other information about malaria prevention.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Support person (for example, family member) — đoạn 3236

• The role play should last up to 10 minutes.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Support person (for example, family member) — đoạn 3237

• After the demonstration, the class will discuss the counselling process and messages.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Support person (for example, family member) — đoạn 3238

Case scenario: read out the following scenario to introduce the role play The client is 17 years old, unmarried, and pregnant for the first time. She has come to the facility for the first ANC visit, presenting at six months’ gestation. The midwife took her history and did a physical examination.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Support person (for example, family member) — đoạn 3239

The client’s mother said that her daughter has sickle cell anaemia. She has had malaria a few times and sometimes had to go to the hospital because of it. She lives in the city with relatives during the school year but is living at home in this rural area with her mother now. From her history and examination, the midwife finds no other problems. However, her haemoglobin is 8.0g/dL. A malaria test is negative. The midwife will now counsel this woman (and her mother) about the danger of malaria for a person with sickle cell anaemia and how to prevent it.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Support person (for example, family member) — đoạn 3240

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Health care provider — đoạn 3241

You are an experienced midwife who has good counselling skills. Refer to Appendix 7 and the information provided on malaria prevention in this session to prepare yourself to counsel this young woman about preventing malaria. You may also want to review the Learning guide on antenatal counselling.

### Nguồn gốc: Client — đoạn 3242

You are 17 years old, unmarried, and pregnant for the first time. Your mother has brought you to the facility for the first ANC visit. You are about six months pregnant. You have sickle cell anaemia. You have had malaria a few times in your life and you were admitted into the hospital a couple of times.

### Nguồn gốc: Support person — đoạn 3243

You are the mother of the client. You care for your daughter, but you are obviously angry with her for becoming pregnant out of wedlock.

### Nguồn gốc: Observers — đoạn 3244

The observers should note the following during the counselling process. If the midwife has: • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Observers — đoạn 3245

assessed the woman’s situation; defined the problem and the information needs of the client; Generated appropriate messages about preventing malaria for the client in line with her special condition; checked that the client and support person understand what is being discussed; and helped the client plan how she would protect herself from malaria.

### Nguồn gốc: Observers — đoạn 3246

Feedback session for role play: At the end of the role play, ask for comments from the observers using the following points as a guide to facilitate the discussions. • • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Observers — đoạn 3247

How did the midwife prepare her room for the counselling activity? How did the midwife approach the client and her support person? Did the midwife give the client and her support person enough information about why she is at special risk for malaria? How did the midwife check for the client and support person’s understanding of their high risk for malaria?

### Nguồn gốc: Observers — đoạn 3248

What did the midwife do to demonstrate emotional support and reassurance? Did the midwife provide enough information on how the client can protect herself against malaria in pregnancy and did she help the client reach a decision on what to do?

### Nguồn gốc: Observers — đoạn 3249

Allow participants to comment on these questions extensively whilst pointing out the good counselling skills and practices.

### Nguồn gốc: Observers — đoạn 3250

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3251

Slides 38 and 39: Key messages

### Nguồn gốc: 1. WHO-recommended strategies for controlling malaria in pregnancy include: — đoạn 3252

(a) sleeping under an insecticide-treated bed net; (b) intermittent preventive treatment with SP (at each scheduled

### Nguồn gốc: ANC visit during second and third trimesters of pregnancy); and — đoạn 3253

(c) prompt case management of malaria illness.

### Nguồn gốc: 2. IPTp-SP should be administered under directly observed therapy (DOT), — đoạn 3254

starting as early as possible in the second trimester (13 to 16 weeks), with doses given at least one month apart until the time of delivery.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Recommended antimalarial medicines in pregnancy include quinine and — đoạn 3255

artemisinin-based combination therapies (ACTs).

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Pregnant women with special risk conditions such as HIV infection, — đoạn 3256

sickle cell anaemia and pre-existing anaemia due to other causes suffer severe consequences when they get malaria infection during pregnancy. If they develop malaria, refer the woman promptly to a hospital for treatment.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Pregnant women with special risk conditions such as HIV infection, — đoạn 3257

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Session 10: Prevention and management — đoạn 3258

of anaemia in pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3259

• Flip chart with paper and markers

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3260

• Training course materials: facilitator guide, participant manual

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3261

• Pregnancy, childbirth, postpartum and newborn care (PCPNC) guide

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3262

• Up-to-date national antenatal care guidelines in line with WHO recommendations on antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience (2016)

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3263

• WHO’s Managing complications in pregnancy and childbirth

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3264

• Relevant national nutrition guidelines

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3265

• Laptop

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3266

• Overhead projector

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3267

• A list of all available haematinics (blood tonics) and their composition (iron folate content)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Session outline — đoạn 3268

Welcome and introduce participants to the session by presenting slides 1 and 2

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Session outline — đoạn 3269

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Session outline — đoạn 3270

Anaemia is a condition in which there is a reduced number of red blood cells or the haemoglobin concentration within the red blood cells is lower than normal. on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Aim — đoạn 3271

• Present slide 3 or ask a participant to read the aim of the session.

### Nguồn gốc: AIM — đoạn 3272

To enable participants to understand the burden of the problem of anaemia in pregnancy, its common causes, and prevention strategies; and to develop skills for diagnosis and management of anaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: AIM — đoạn 3273

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Session objectives — đoạn 3274

• Present slide 4 or ask participants to read in turn the objectives of the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Session objectives — đoạn 3275

session.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: At the end of the session, participants will be able to: — đoạn 3276

• • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: At the end of the session, participants will be able to: — đoạn 3277

describe the burden of the problem of anaemia in pregnancy and its effects on the pregnant woman and her baby; define the common causes of anaemia in sub-Saharan Africa; describe strategies for preventing anaemia in pregnancy; Counsel a pregnant woman about prevention of anaemia in pregnancy; correctly manage and/or refer cases of anaemia in pregnancy; and explain why pregnant women with HIV or sickle cell disease face additional risks and complications from anaemia during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: The burden of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3278

•• Anaemia is a global public health problem affecting both developing and developed countries at all ages. During pregnancy, it is an important cause of morbidity and mortality. It is estimated that one out of 10 maternal deaths can be attributed to iron deficiency anaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: The burden of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3279

• Ask participants about the prevalence of anaemia in pregnancy in their

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: The burden of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3280

settings.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: The burden of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3281

• Present slide 5 (10 minutes) to describe the global and regional burdens of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: The burden of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3282

anaemia in pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: The burden of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3283

• According to recent estimates, about forty-two per cent (41.8%) of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: The burden of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3284

pregnant women in the world are anaemic.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: The burden of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3285

• In sub-Saharan Africa, the prevalence of anaemia in pregnancy is higher,

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: The burden of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3286

about 50%.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Effects of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3287

Highlight the following facts:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Effects of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3288

• Anaemia in pregnancy is a major contributor to poor maternal and newborn

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Effects of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3289

outcomes.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Effects of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3290

• It is associated with increased risks of low-birth-weight newborns,

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Effects of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3291

premature birth and perinatal mortality.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Effects of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3292

• For the mother, anaemia is associated with poor maternal well-being,

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Effects of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3293

increased susceptibility to infections, and increased risk of death from major obstetric complications. In fact, the risk for maternal death has been shown to decrease by 20% for every 1 g/dL increase in haemoglobin level.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 6: Effects of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3294

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: WHO definition of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3295

• According to the World Health Organization (WHO), anaemia is defined as

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: WHO definition of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3296

haemoglobin (Hb) levels <12.0 g/dL in non-pregnant women and <13.0 g/ dL in men.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: WHO definition of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3297

• During pregnancy, anaemia is diagnosed when a woman’s haemoglobin

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: WHO definition of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3298

concentration is found to be less than 11.0 g/dL during the first trimester or lower than 10.5 g/dL during the second trimester. o Moderate anaemia: Hb >7 g/dL and <11 g/dL o Severe anaemia: Hb <7 g/dL

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: WHO definition of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3299

• Ask participants why lower Hb levels are required for diagnosing anaemia

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: WHO definition of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3300

in pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: WHO definition of anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3301

• Explain the physiological basis if this is not known by the class.

### Nguồn gốc: Common causes of anaemia during pregnancy in sub-Saharan Africa — đoạn 3302

Slide 8: Common causes of anaemia in pregnancy in sub-Saharan Africa

### Nguồn gốc: Common causes of anaemia during pregnancy in sub-Saharan Africa — đoạn 3303

• Ask participants to list the common causes of anaemia in pregnancy in their

### Nguồn gốc: Common causes of anaemia during pregnancy in sub-Saharan Africa — đoạn 3304

settings. Note their answers on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Common causes of anaemia during pregnancy in sub-Saharan Africa — đoạn 3305

• Present slide 8 and compare the answers provided to those listed on the

### Nguồn gốc: Common causes of anaemia during pregnancy in sub-Saharan Africa — đoạn 3306

slide and fill in the knowledge gaps.

### Nguồn gốc: Common causes of anaemia during pregnancy in sub-Saharan Africa — đoạn 3307

• Explain how prevalent these conditions are in the sub-region and how they

### Nguồn gốc: Common causes of anaemia during pregnancy in sub-Saharan Africa — đoạn 3308

cause anaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: Common causes of anaemia during pregnancy in sub-Saharan Africa — đoạn 3309

• Remind participants that a pregnant woman may have more than one cause

### Nguồn gốc: Common causes of anaemia during pregnancy in sub-Saharan Africa — đoạn 3310

of anaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy: WHO-recommended strategies and — đoạn 3311

related ANC plan (30 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3312

• Ask participants to list and/or describe the strategies in their respective

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3313

settings to prevent anaemia in pregnancy. Record these on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3314

• Encourage a discussion on how well these strategies are being implemented,

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3315

including the challenges.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3316

• Present slide 9 to review the WHO strategies and determine if all these

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3317

strategies are known and implemented in participants’ settings.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3318

• Refer to the care plan for prevention of anaemia in pregnancy in the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3319

participant manual).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3320

• Task a participant each to read out a component of the plan and emphasize

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3321

the importance of each component.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9: Preventing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3322

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3323

• A variety of locally available foods exist that are rich in iron and folate. It

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3324

is important that care providers know about these foods so that they can advise their clients.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3325

• Several medicines (blood tonics) sold over the counter in most countries are

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3326

purported to be ideal for pregnant women.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3327

• Very often these medicines do not all contain the WHO-recommended

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3328

vvdosage levels for iron and folate to meet the needs of pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3329

• It is important for care providers to know about such medicines so they can

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3330

advise clients and prescribe the right ones. o Task participants to find out about the aforementioned foods and medications in a homework activity. o Present slide 11 and explain the instructions for this activity.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3331

• It is recommended that participants are given adequate time to research and obtain answers to the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3332

assignment. This will require at least 24 hours. The feedback discussion can be held one day later or during the skills practice discussions on day 5.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3333

• Ask participants to list the iron/folate-rich foods and provide evidence to support the selection of these

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3334

foods. Record their answers on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3335

• Ask them to also list the medicines/products that they reviewed and the iron and folic acid content of each

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3336

product. Record them on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3337

• For each product, ask participants whether the daily intake will meet the daily dose of iron and folic acid

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3338

needed for supplementation during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3339

• Ask whether the iron content is sufficient for the treatment of anaemia during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3340

• Caution that health workers need to be very careful about recommending or endorsing such products

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3341

unless they are sure of what they provide.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3342

• Usually, the iron and folic acid tablets prescribed at the health facility are better than these more

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3343

expensive products.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Class activity – homework — đoạn 3344

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Identifying and treating pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3345

(30 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Identifying pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3346

• At the antenatal clinic, all pregnant women should be screened for anaemia

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Identifying pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3347

at every antenatal visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Identifying pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3348

• Refer to PCPNC Chart C4 to classify the signs of anaemia. Have participants

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Identifying pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3349

take turns reading aloud until you have reviewed the entire chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Identifying pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3350

• Present slide 11 to emphasize these symptoms.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Identifying pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3351

• Note that during client assessment, it is also important to determine the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Identifying pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3352

underlying cause of anaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Identifying pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3353

• Ask participants to list questions they could ask to help identify the cause.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Identifying pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3354

• The diagnosis of anaemia and its causes and severity must always be

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Identifying pregnant women with anaemia — đoạn 3355

confirmed through laboratory tests. This is important for monitoring treatment response.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Treating the pregnant woman with anaemia — đoạn 3356

• Key factors that influence the care plan for the pregnant woman with

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Treating the pregnant woman with anaemia — đoạn 3357

anaemia include: the cause(s) of anaemia, its level of severity, and the woman’s gestational age at the time of diagnosis.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Treating the pregnant woman with anaemia — đoạn 3358

• Ask a participant each to read out the explanations given in their manual on

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Treating the pregnant woman with anaemia — đoạn 3359

how these key factors influence the treatment options and care plan.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Treating the pregnant woman with anaemia — đoạn 3360

• Give additional explanations if needed.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Managing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3361

• Refer participants to Figure 10.3 below (Flow chart on management of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Managing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3362

anaemia in pregnancy) – and present slide 12.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Managing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3363

• Using Figure 10.2, highlight how the severity of anaemia, potential causes

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Managing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3364

and gestational age have all influenced the care plan outlined.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Managing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3365

• Emphasize that any pregnant woman whose haemoglobin is less than 11 g/

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Managing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3366

dL has anaemia and should be treated with iron and folic acid tablets, one tablet two times a day. Review the chart in Figure 10.2 with participants to be sure that they understand how to read it and how it may be used.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Managing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3367

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Managing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3368

Figure 10.2 Flow chart for managing anaemia in pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Managing anaemia in pregnancy — đoạn 3369

Screen all pregnant women for anaemia. Check haemoglobin levels at booking and at all visits (if possible)

### Nguồn gốc: Haemoglobin is <7 g/dL (severe anaemia) — đoạn 3370

and/or woman is symptomatic for anaemia at any gestation

### Nguồn gốc: Refer urgently to a — đoạn 3371

hospital with blood transfusion facility for admission and appropriate care*

### Nguồn gốc: Refer for — đoạn 3372

delivery in hospital

### Nguồn gốc: Haemoglobin is >7 and <11 g/dL — đoạn 3373

(moderate anaemia)

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3374

>28 weeks

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3375

< 28 weeks

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3376

• Give one iron and folic acid tablet twice

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3377

daily (60 mg iron and 400 mcg folic acid per tablet) and/or

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3378

• Give IPTp-SP – first dose as early as

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3379

possible in the second trimester and at each scheduled ANC visit during the second and third trimesters

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3380

• Counsel on nutrition

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3381

• Deworm once after first trimester

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3382

• Monitor haemoglobin at all visits until

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3383

normal

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3384

• Monitor foetal growth closely

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3385

• Identify and treat other causes of anaemia

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3386

•

### Nguồn gốc: Gestation — đoạn 3387

See “Treat and Advise” column in Chart C4 and Chart F3 in the

### Nguồn gốc: PCPNC guide – refer to anaemia guidelines — đoạn 3388

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3389

• Effective treatment of anaemia involves regular follow-up of the patient to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3390

check on treatment response.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3391

• In many patients with iron deficiency anaemia, haemoglobin levels generally

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3392

respond quickly to treatment with oral iron therapy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3393

• However, in some patients, the effectiveness of the treatment can become

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3394

compromised by poor absorption of the iron, gastrointestinal discomforts (nausea, vomiting, constipation and abdominal discomfort) and nonadherence to treatment.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3395

• Expected Hb rise (if treatment is effective) is 1.0 g/dL two weeks after

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3396

initiation of treatment, and thereafter about 0.5 g//dL weekly.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3397

Describe and discuss steps that must be taken to monitor treatment response. All pregnant women with anaemia started on oral iron therapy should be seen and have their haemoglobin levels checked again two weeks after initiation of iron treatment. The expected Hb rise (if treatment is effective) is 1.0 g/dL.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3398

If haemoglobin rise is adequate and there are no challenges:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3399

• Review woman again at two to four weekly intervals (depending on severity of anaemia and gestational

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3400

age).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3401

• Check haemoglobin levels at all subsequent visits to determine if response to treatment is still satisfactory.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3402

If haemoglobin rise is unsatisfactory and//or there are challenges:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3403

• The care provider must find out why response to treatment is unsatisfactory. Ask questions such as:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3404

o Has the client procured the prescribed iron and folate medication? o Is she taking them twice a day as ordered? o Does she have any discomforts or concerns associated with taking her medication such us nausea, vomiting, bloating, constipation or diarrhoea?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3405

• If the client has challenges due to intolerance and GI upsets, the provider can suggest the following:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3406

o Take medication with meals and with vitamin C-rich foods such as oranges or mangoes o Change to other iron preparations such as iron fumarate and iron gluconate

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3407

• The client must be reviewed again in two weeks for response. If response is still unsatisfactory refer the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3408

woman to a hospital for other treatment options, such as intravenous iron.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3409

• Note: No pregnant woman should enter into labour with anaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3410

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3411

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3412

Emphasize the correct dosage for treatment of anaemia in pregnancy and the criticality of educating the client on the importance of taking iron as prescribed, on common side-effects, and how they can be managed. Emphasize that, in a hospital setting, trained medical doctors or experienced midwives may give parenteral iron and blood transfusion for treatment of anaemia. These clients must be monitored very closely during treatment.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 14: Monitoring response to treatment with iron and folate — đoạn 3413
