Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Managing anaemia in special risk groups: HIV infection, haemolytic — đoạn 3414

anaemia (30 minutes) (refer to slide 13)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Managing anaemia in special risk groups — đoạn 3415

• Pregnant women with HIV infection or haemolytic anaemia, such as sickle

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Managing anaemia in special risk groups — đoạn 3416

cell anaemia or G6PD, are special high-risk anaemia patients.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Managing anaemia in special risk groups — đoạn 3417

• Ask participants why these women have especially high risk for severe

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Managing anaemia in special risk groups — đoạn 3418

anaemia and its complications? Note and correct the answers given.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Managing anaemia in special risk groups — đoạn 3419

• Women presenting with these risk conditions and with anaemia should be

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Managing anaemia in special risk groups — đoạn 3420

managed at hospital level and by doctors with the relevant skills.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Managing anaemia in special risk groups — đoạn 3421

• Refer participants to Figure 10.3 in the participant manual. Task participants

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Managing anaemia in special risk groups — đoạn 3422

to read out sections of the chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Managing anaemia in special risk groups — đoạn 3423

• Discuss and explain the various care plans, emphasizing how they differ

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 15: Managing anaemia in special risk groups — đoạn 3424

from routine care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Class activity 2 – case studies — đoạn 3425

• The purpose of class activity 2 is to enable participants to use their clinical

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Class activity 2 – case studies — đoạn 3426

decision-making skills and the care planning guides provided in the session to manage cases of anaemia in pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Class activity 2 – case studies — đoạn 3427

• Present slide 16 to provide instructions for this activity. Note that this is an

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 16: Class activity 2 – case studies — đoạn 3428

individual exercise.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Case study – Madam Bie — đoạn 3429

• Refer participants to the case studies in the participant manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Case study – Madam Bie — đoạn 3430

• Present slides 17 and 18 or have participants read out the case studies.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Case study – Madam Bie — đoạn 3431

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 3432

• Task co-facilitators to supervise the class activity and individually review participants’ answers when they

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 3433

have completed the assignment.

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 3434

• When all participants have completed the assignment, the lead facilitator will give feedback to the entire

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback and discussion — đoạn 3435

group. Encourage participants to share their answers and compare them to the possible answers provided below in italics.

### Nguồn gốc: CASE STUDY 1: Jemila — đoạn 3436

Ms Jemila is 23 years old, She lives in the poor part of town and has had three children. Her last child is 15 months old. She is now three months pregnant and is reporting to you for her first ANC visit. She is feeling well and has no health complaints. Her uterine size corresponds to 12 gestation weeks. You examine her and she looks pale. Her Hb is 8.5 g/dL. Her sickling test is negative. Her urine and stools are negative. Blood test is negative for malaria parasites.

### Nguồn gốc: Question 1: What treatments will you give Jemila? — đoạn 3437

Ans. • •

### Nguồn gốc: Start her on iron and folate tablets twice per day — đoạn 3438

Ask her to return after two weeks’ gestation, at which time you will give her one dose of mebendazole and start her on IPTp-SP

### Nguồn gốc: Start her on iron and folate tablets twice per day — đoạn 3439

Question 2: What counselling messages related to anaemia will you give her? Ans. • •

### Nguồn gốc: Start her on iron and folate tablets twice per day — đoạn 3440

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Start her on iron and folate tablets twice per day — đoạn 3441

•

### Nguồn gốc: Start her on iron and folate tablets twice per day — đoạn 3442

Explain the causes of anaemia and the importance of taking iron and folate tablets to treat or prevent anaemia. Advise her to: take iron tablets with foods containing vitamin C, which improves the absorption of the iron; and take the iron tablets with food. Gastrointestinal side-effects that are likely to occur if iron is taken on an empty stomach include nausea, vomiting, constipation and diarrhoea.

### Nguồn gốc: Start her on iron and folate tablets twice per day — đoạn 3443

Advise her to: eat foods rich in iron, folate, vitamin C, B vitamins and protein-rich foods. Avoid foods that inhibit iron absorption, including tea, coffee, cola, bran, and other unhealthy “foods” such as clay. Provide examples of iron-rich foods: red meat (especially liver), poultry, fish, whole grains, dark green leafy vegetables, shellfish, dried fruits. Examples of folate-rich foods are beans, dark green leafy vegetables, spinach, citrus fruits, mangos, avocados.

### Nguồn gốc: Start her on iron and folate tablets twice per day — đoạn 3444

Explain how to prevent malaria: sleeping under an insecticide-treated bed net, avoiding mosquito bites, attending ANC visits to receive IPT.

### Nguồn gốc: Start her on iron and folate tablets twice per day — đoạn 3445

Question 3: When will you ask her to return to the clinic for follow-up? Ans. • •

### Nguồn gốc: Start her on iron and folate tablets twice per day — đoạn 3446

She should return in two weeks for follow-up: to check whether her haemoglobin level is improving, to receive mebendazole, and to start IPTp with SP. She can come sooner if she has any concerns.

### Nguồn gốc: Start her on iron and folate tablets twice per day — đoạn 3447

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Madam Bie — đoạn 3448

Madam Bie is eight months pregnant. This is her second antenatal visit to your clinic. She came for her first visit when she was six months pregnant. At her first visit two months ago, her haemoglobin level was 7.5 g/dL. You gave her a prescription for tabs ferrous sulphate to take one tablet (60 mg elemental iron) twice daily and tabs folic acid 400 mcg once daily. You also started her on the required dose of IPTp-SP. You asked her to report back for a check-up in two weeks, but she did not come back until now. At this second visit, her haemoglobin is 8.5 g/dL.

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Madam Bie — đoạn 3449

Question 1: Is the improvement in her haemoglobin level satisfactory? Explain your answer. Ans. •

### Nguồn gốc: Case study 2: Madam Bie — đoạn 3450

No, the improvement in her Hb level is NOT satisfactory. Her haemoglobin would have been normal around 11.5 g/dL if she was taking her treatment as planned.

### Nguồn gốc: Question 2: What questions will you ask her? — đoạn 3451

Ans. • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Question 2: What questions will you ask her? — đoạn 3452

Why she did not come for her follow-up check-up at two weeks

### Nguồn gốc: If she obtained and was still taking her medications — đoạn 3453

If she has had any challenges with taking her iron/folate medications

### Nguồn gốc: If she has signs and symptoms suggestive of worsening anaemia — đoạn 3454

If she has noticed any jaundice, bloody urine, worms in her stool or bleeding from vagina

### Nguồn gốc: If she has signs and symptoms suggestive of worsening anaemia — đoạn 3455

Question 3: What additional actions and treatments will you undertake now? Ans. •

### Nguồn gốc: If she has signs and symptoms suggestive of worsening anaemia — đoạn 3456

Counsel her on how to take the iron pills; counsel her on recommended iron-rich foods and malaria prevention; provide her with iron and folate tablets, two per day (morning and evening); and provide IPTp-SP and mebendazole.

### Nguồn gốc: Question 4: What messages will you address in counselling? — đoạn 3457

Ans. •

### Nguồn gốc: Question 4: What messages will you address in counselling? — đoạn 3458

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Question 4: What messages will you address in counselling? — đoạn 3459

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Question 4: What messages will you address in counselling? — đoạn 3460

She has anaemia and it is extremely important that she takes her iron pills to treat the anaemia and improve the haemoglobin level as much as possible before childbirth. The dose of iron tablets should be double – one with the morning meal and one with the evening meal, preferably taking the tablet with citrus to improve absorption.

### Nguồn gốc: Question 4: What messages will you address in counselling? — đoạn 3461

Eat foods rich in iron, folate, vitamin C, B vitamins and protein. Avoid foods that inhibit iron absorption, including tea, coffee, cola, bran, and other unhealthy “foods” such as clay. Examples of iron-rich foods are red meat (especially liver), poultry, fish, whole grains, dark green leafy vegetables, shellfish, dried fruits. Examples of folate-rich foods are beans, dark green leafy vegetables, spinach, citrus fruits, mangos, avocados.

### Nguồn gốc: Question 4: What messages will you address in counselling? — đoạn 3462

Because of her anaemia, she has an increased chance of complications. She should revise her birth plan to give birth at a hospital with blood transfusion facilities. She should identify blood donors.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsel her on danger signs in pregnancy and complication readiness — đoạn 3463

Question 5: When will you tell her to return to the health centre? Ans. •

### Nguồn gốc: Counsel her on danger signs in pregnancy and complication readiness — đoạn 3464

She should return to the clinic every week for monitoring until childbirth.

### Nguồn gốc: Counsel her on danger signs in pregnancy and complication readiness — đoạn 3465

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3466

Slides 19–21: Key messages

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3467

• Anaemia during pregnancy is associated with significant health

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3468

consequences for both the mother and her baby.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3469

• WHO strategies for controlling anaemia in pregnancy include: iron/folate

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3470

supplementation to all pregnant women; control of malaria in pregnancy by IPTp with SP and sleeping under an insecticide-treated bed net; control of hookworm infestation by deworming; nutrition counselling; and optimal birth spacing.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3471

• Whether they are symptomatic or not, all pregnant women should be

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3472

screened for anaemia at every antenatal visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3473

• Any pregnant woman whose haemoglobin is less than 11 g/dL has anaemia

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3474

and should be treated with iron and folic acid tablets, one tablet twice a day.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3475

• Important considerations for developing a care plan for clients with anaemia

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3476

in pregnancy include the severity of the anaemia, gestational age at the time of diagnosis, and the underlying cause(s) of the anaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3477

• The general principle is that “No pregnant woman should enter labour with

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3478

anaemia”.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3479

• The expected haemoglobin rise (if treatment is effective) is 1g/dL after two

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3480

weeks following initiation of iron therapy. Thereafter, Hb levels increase at rates of 0.5–1.0 g/dL.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3481

• Pregnant women with anaemia who have HIV infection or haemolytic

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3482

anaemia, such as sickle cell anaemia or G6PD, are special high-risk anaemia patients. They should be managed at hospital level and by doctors with the relevant skills.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 3483

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Session 11: Integrated approach to the — đoạn 3484

prevention of mother-to-child transmission of human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B, syphilis and tuberculosis

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3485

• Flip chart with paper and markers

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3486

• Training course materials: facilitator guide, participant manual

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3487

• Pregnancy, childbirth, postpartum and newborn care (PCPNC) guide

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3488

• National HIV prevention and control guidelines

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3489

• National strategy for the elimination/prevention of mother-to-child transmission (PMTCT or eMTCT)

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3490

• National STI prevention and control guidelines

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3491

• Guidelines for the prevention, care and treatment of chronic hepatitis B infections

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3492

• WHO guideline on syphilis screening and treatment for pregnant women

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3493

• WHO policy advice on dual elimination and triple elimination

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3494

• Laptop

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3495

• Overhead projector

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3496

• Table and chairs for role play

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3497

•

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3498

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3499

Before teaching this session, familiarize yourself with all the above-mentioned reference materials, especially with the national policy/protocols, including algorithms for testing and treatment in your country. Check that the training content is consistent with existing national protocols and, if not, revise the text and slides as needed.

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3500

If the national guidelines and protocols are not up to date, use the training materials and WHO guidelines indicated above for reference.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 2: Session outline — đoạn 3501

• Present slides 1 and 2 to welcome participants and introduce the session

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 2: Session outline — đoạn 3502

(2 minutes)

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 2: Session outline — đoạn 3503

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Aim: To enable participants to implement integrated counselling, testing and — đoạn 3504

treatment of HIV, hepatitis B, (HBV), syphilis and tuberculosis (TB) during antenatal care.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 3505

• Provide key information on HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB infections.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 3506

• Describe the key strategies for preventing mother-to-child transmission of

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 3507

HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 3508

• Provide counselling and testing for HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 3509

• Provide care and treatment for pregnant women with HIV, HBV, syphilis and

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 3510

TB.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: Overview of HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB — đoạn 3511

• Ask participants to reflect on the four diseases mentioned in the aims and

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3512

• Ask if they see pregnant women with these conditions in their settings.

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3513

• Ask participants if they know why these four infectious diseases have been

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3514

prioritized for intervention.

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3515

• Present slide 4 to highlight and explain the reasons listed.

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3516

• Emphasize that babies can also get these infections from their mothers

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3517

during the pregnancy, and/or soon after birth when they are breast feeding.

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3518

Slide 5: Preventing mother-to-child transmission of HIV, HBV, TB and syphilis

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3519

• Ask participants to describe ways through which we can prevent babies

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3520

from getting these infections from their mothers. Note their answers.

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3521

• Present the information on the slide and compare the strategies listed to the

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3522

answers given and fill in the gaps. Highlight the following points:

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3523

• Preventing MTCT of infectious diseases during pregnancy and providing

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3524

appropriate treatment for the infected pregnant woman are a key objective of ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3525

• WHO recommends providing an integrated care package that links ANC to

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3526

relevant clinical services for these infections, such as HIV testing services (HTS), TB screening, SRH, and other harm reduction programmes.

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3527

• The primary purpose of such integration is to make care more convenient

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3528

for pregnant women coming for ANC and presenting with these conditions.

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3529

• Integrating ANC with these services also creates the opportunity to promote

### Nguồn gốc: objectives. — đoạn 3530

testing uptake, diagnose coinfections and start care and treatment services for all co-existing conditions at the same time and in the same place.

### Nguồn gốc: Key information on HIV, hepatitis B, syphilis and tuberculosis infection — đoạn 3531

• • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Key information on HIV, hepatitis B, syphilis and tuberculosis infection — đoạn 3532

Begin by asking participants to share what they know about the three diseases, if they have any experience with a patient, a friend or family member affected. Ask about the causes, modes of transmission, signs and symptoms and prevalence/burden. Note their answers on a flip chart. Refer participants to their manual for more information.

### Nguồn gốc: Key information on HIV, hepatitis B, syphilis and tuberculosis infection — đoạn 3533

Highlight the key features of the four infections and correct any knowledge gaps during the presentation. Explain their similarities and the rationale for elimination. Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–7: HIV infection and burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3534

• In 2016, there were 25 500 000 HIV-infected people in sub-Saharan Africa,

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–7: HIV infection and burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3535

with 116 000 new HIV infections in children.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–7: HIV infection and burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3536

• Briefly discuss some of the misconceptions and challenges associated with

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–7: HIV infection and burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3537

the management of the disease. Note for example that:

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–7: HIV infection and burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3538

• HIV cannot be transmitted through hugging or mosquito bites.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–7: HIV infection and burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3539

• Most persons with infection are asymptomatic.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–7: HIV infection and burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3540

• Women in their reproductive age are at higher risk.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–7: HIV infection and burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3541

• The stigma associated with HIV infection is a barrier to diagnosis and care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–7: HIV infection and burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3542

• PMTCT remains a key control strategy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 6–7: HIV infection and burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3543

• Persons with HIV infection require lifelong treatment and support.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3544

• Present slides 8–11 to provide background information on TB in

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3545

pregnancy. You may also enrich the presentation with the additional information provided below.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3546

• TB is believed to be nearly as old as human history. The human host serves

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3547

as a natural reservoir for M. tuberculosis.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3548

• TB infection is caused by inhalation of viable bacilli, which may persist in an

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3549

inactive state (known as latent TB infection [LTBI]) or progress to active TB disease. Individuals with LTBI are asymptomatic and not contagious. Latent TB bacilli remain viable and may reactivate, causing active symptomatic TB disease, which can be transmitted via airborne spread.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3550

• Tuberculosis (TB) was declared a public health emergency by WHO in 2005

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3551

in the Africa Region.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3552

• Worldwide, the burden of tuberculosis (TB) disease in pregnant women is

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3553

substantial.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3554

• The exact global extent of the problem of maternal mortality caused by TB

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3555

is unknown – particularly in the context of HIV and stigma. But studies from sub-Saharan Africa and India report TB as the cause of 6–15% of all-cause mortality and 15–34% of indirect maternal mortality. (Khan M et al., AIDS 2001; 15:1857-63) Slides 10–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy (continued)

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3556

• Congenital TB is very rare. It occurs by trans-placental transmission through

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3557

umbilical veins to the foetal liver and lungs or by aspiration and swallowing of infected amniotic fluid in utero or intrapartum, causing primary infection of foetal lungs and gut.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3558

• Transmission of tuberculosis through breast milk does not occur. Horizontal

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3559

transmission in the postpartum period by droplets from an infected mother or undiagnosed family member is the more common route of transmission to the infant.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3560

• TB during pregnancy is associated with a two-fold increase of mother-tochild transmission of HIV. This, because TB infection of the placenta results

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3561

in increased placental HIV viral load.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8–11: Tuberculosis in pregnancy — đoạn 3562

• Antenatal care presents a unique opportunity for evaluation and

### Nguồn gốc: management of latent and active TB among individuals with risk of TB who — đoạn 3563

may not otherwise present for medical care. Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3564

• Present slide 12 on general information on syphilis:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3565

• It is a bacterial STI caused by Treponema pallidum, which causes substantial

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3566

morbidity and mortality.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3567

• In 2012, an estimated 5.6 million new cases of syphilis occurred among 15–

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3568

49-year-olds worldwide. There are an estimated 18 million prevalent cases of syphilis.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3569

• Ask participants if they have seen cases of syphilis. Let them describe the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3570

clinical presentations if they have.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy — đoạn 3571

• Refer participants to their manual for additional information if necessary.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3572

• Untreated primary and secondary syphilis infections in pregnancy result in

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3573

severe adverse pregnancy outcomes.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3574

• Latent (asymptomatic) syphilis infections in pregnancy also cause serious

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3575

adverse pregnancy outcomes in more than half of cases.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3576

• Mother-to-child transmission of syphilis (congenital syphilis) is usually

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3577

devastating to the foetus.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3578

• The foetus can be easily cured with treatment, and the risk of adverse

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3579

outcomes to the foetus is minimal if the mother receives adequate treatment during early pregnancy – ideally before the second trimester. Slide 14 – 15: Hepatitis B infection and its burden in pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3580

• Present the information on the slide and supplement it with the additional

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3581

information provided below if needed.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3582

• Viruses are the most common causes of hepatitis in the world, but

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3583

other infections, toxic substances (such as alcohol and certain drugs), and autoimmune diseases also cause hepatitis.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3584

• There are five known types of the family of viruses (Hepadnaviridae) with

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3585

affinity for the liver which affect humans (A, B, C, D, E).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3586

• Two types (B and C) can cause severe liver damage and lead to chronic

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3587

disease and liver cancer. Hepatitis B virus (HBV) is the commonest type of viral hepatitis in Africa.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3588

• Briefly describe the clinical presentations of the infection and occurrence of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3589

fulminant infections during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3590

• Symptomatic acute disease occurs in about 1% of perinatally infected

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3591

individuals, in 10% of children infected between 1 and 5 years of age, and in about 30% of individuals infected after the age of 5 years.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3592

• Highlight the risks of MTCT and the long-term health outcomes in the baby.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3593

• A vaccine is available to protect the newborn and medications exist to treat

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3594

women with chronic infection and high viral loads.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3595

Conclude the presentations on the various infections by summarizing their similarities and the impact of coinfections with these germs on the pregnant woman and her baby.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3596

• HIV, hepatitis B and syphilis can be sexually transmitted.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3597

• Very often, coinfections with these diseases occurs in the same individual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3598

• The three infections and TB can be screened for or identified using rapid diagnostic test kits at point of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3599

care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3600

• Treatments are also available to prevent mother-to-child transmission.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3601

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3602

Slides 16 and 17: Provider-initiated counselling and testing Present the information on the slides 16 and 17 to describe the PITC approach and its benefits.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3603

• The woman knows her status and can take appropriate measures.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3604

• She can protect her partner and family from infection in case of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3605

serodiscordance.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3606

• She gets support from her partner and her family.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3607

• She can encourage her partner to be tested.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3608

• She can get treatment as soon as possible in case of a positive result and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Syphilis and its burden in pregnancy (2) — đoạn 3609

prevent MTCT. Highlight its role in the continuum of maternal and child care and in reinforcing couple involvement.

### Nguồn gốc: Counselling and testing/screening for HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB — đoạn 3610

• • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Counselling and testing/screening for HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB — đoạn 3611

The introduction of provider-initiated testing and counselling (PITC) of HIV as a routine component of ANC has led to a massive decline in MTCT of HIV. Many countries have therefore prioritized PITC in ANC as a key component of their effort to eliminate mother-to-child transmission of HIV (eMTCT).

### Nguồn gốc: Counselling and testing/screening for HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB — đoạn 3612

HIV testing is also being effectively combined with screening for syphilis, HBV and TB, and this has had considerable public health benefits. The PITC approach is therefore strongly recommended for settings with a high prevalence of the four diseases.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Guiding principles of PITC — đoạn 3613

• Present the 5Cs (guiding principles) for PITC.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Guiding principles of PITC — đoạn 3614

• Refer participants to the relevant section in their manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Guiding principles of PITC — đoạn 3615

• Task a participant each to read out loud and explain the text describing each

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Guiding principles of PITC — đoạn 3616

principle. Clarify any points if necessary.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Guiding principles of PITC — đoạn 3617

• The five key guiding principles (5Cs) for PITC are described in the text box

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 18: Guiding principles of PITC — đoạn 3618

below.

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Consent: the woman/couple is informed of the process for testing and counselling and of — đoạn 3619

decline testing. In most settings, verbal consent for HIV testing is considered sufficient.

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Confidentiality: what the provider and the client discuss will not be disclosed to anyo — đoạn 3620

expressed consent of the person being tested. Confidentiality applies not only to the test results but also to any personal information, such as information concerning sexual behaviour and the use of illegal drugs.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Counselling: this includes pre- and post-test counselling. Pre-test information can be — đoạn 3621

counselling setting, but clients/couples can also ask questions in private individual or couple counselling. However, testing must always be followed by appropriate high-quality post-test counselling for all clients, based on the specific test results.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Correct test results: quality assurance mechanisms must exist to ensure that people rec — đoạn 3622

diagnosis. For HIV, all people who receive a positive diagnosis are retested to verify their diagnosis before initiation of HIV care or treatment.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Connection to care and treatment: linkages to prevention, treatment and care services s — đoạn 3623

this must include appropriate follow-up, including long-term prevention and treatment support. Without linkage to treatment and care, testing and learning about one’s positive status for the infections will have limited value. Pregnant women who test negative for infections also require linkages to prevention care services, if they continue to be at high risk for infection.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Connection to care and treatment: linkages to prevention, treatment and care services s — đoạn 3624

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Providing PITC for HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB — đoạn 3625

• Refer participants to the learning guide Table 11.1 in their manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Providing PITC for HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB — đoạn 3626

• Explain the purpose of the guide and task seven or eight participants to each

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Providing PITC for HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB — đoạn 3627

read out a section of the guide and explain their understanding of the steps described in the text.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Providing PITC for HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB — đoạn 3628

• Clarify any knowledge gaps or misunderstandings.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 19: Providing PITC for HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB — đoạn 3629

End discussions on the counselling approach by asking participants to share their experiences on counselling women for HIV, syphilis and hepatitis B testing. •

### Nguồn gốc: Do women more often consent or decline? — đoạn 3630

•

### Nguồn gốc: Did they use the steps described? — đoạn 3631

•

### Nguồn gốc: How have they overcome difficulties? — đoạn 3632

Recommended HIV, HBV, syphilis and tuberculosis testing and screening approaches for pregnant women and their newborns •

### Nguồn gốc: How have they overcome difficulties? — đoạn 3633

As part of preparations for this session, familiarize yourself with the national protocols for HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB testing.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3634

• Ask participants about the kits they use in their facilities to test for HIV, HBV

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3635

and syphilis.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3636

• Do they screen all clients for TB and how is this done? Note their answers.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3637

• Present slide 20 to highlight the general WHO recommendations for

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3638

selecting test kits.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3639

• Rapid diagnostic tests (RDTs) are so called as they produce a test result

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3640

quickly, usually in less than 30 minutes.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3641

• List the benefits of RDTs at points of care (POC).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3642

• Emphasize that participants must always adhere to national testing

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3643

protocols as these have often been carefully selected to meet national settings.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3644

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3645

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3646

and their newborns. You may also use the additional information provided below to enrich the presentations. Also refer to Figure 11.2 in the participant manual. Inform participants that they will have an opportunity to see and practice using these tests in Session 14 if they have no prior experience.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 20: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3647

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3648

HIV:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3649

• HIV RDTs detect HIV 1 and 2 antibodies and antigen (HIV-1 p24) in the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3650

blood. Most persons (about 90%) will develop enough HIV antibodies to be detected three to four weeks after infection. RDTs are just as accurate as a standard HIV test.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3651

• If the test is negative at ANC booking, it is advised that it is repeated at

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3652

36 weeks. If the initial test is positive, then further supplementary tests are done to confirm the diagnosis. A combination of RDTs, simple and enzyme immunoassays are recommended as second-and third-line assays to confirm an initial reactive test result before lifelong treatment is started.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3653

• Molecular testing techniques that detect the presence of HIV

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 21: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3654

viral nucleic acid, such as RNA or DNA, are recommended for early infant diagnosis (EID) in infants under 18 months of age. This is because passively transferred HIV antibodies from the mother to the baby can give false positive

### Nguồn gốc: results if antibody detecting RDTs are used. — đoạn 3655

HBV:

### Nguồn gốc: results if antibody detecting RDTs are used. — đoạn 3656

• A positive HBsAg (hepatitis B surface antigen) test result means that the

### Nguồn gốc: results if antibody detecting RDTs are used. — đoạn 3657

patient is infected and can spread the hepatitis B virus to others through their blood.

### Nguồn gốc: results if antibody detecting RDTs are used. — đoạn 3658

• Anti-HBs or HBsAb (hepatitis B surface antibody) – a “positive” or

### Nguồn gốc: results if antibody detecting RDTs are used. — đoạn 3659

“reactive” anti-HBs (or HBsAb) test result indicates that a person is protected against the hepatitis B virus.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3660

Syphilis:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3661

• Traditionally, a variety of screening/diagnostic tests were available for

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3662

detecting syphilis. They included VDRL, TPHA, RPR and TPPA. Each had limitations, and interpreting their results can be challenging for less experienced care providers.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3663

• Recently, POC rapid syphilis tests (RSTs) for treponemal antibodies have

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3664

been developed and approved for use.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3665

• RSTs provide results in 10–15 minutes and can be performed on-site in

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3666

any setting, since they do not require refrigerated storage or laboratory equipment.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3667

• RSTs use T. pallidum antigens to detect treponema-specific antibodies.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 22: Recommended testing/screening approaches — đoạn 3668

However, a positive test result does not distinguish between active and previously treated infections.

### Nguồn gốc: Tuberculosis — đoạn 3669

• Traditionally, TB diagnosis was confirmed by sputum microscopy and

### Nguồn gốc: Tuberculosis — đoạn 3670

culture. Results could take weeks to obtain. Negative culture results did not conclusively mean that the patient did not have TB, especially for HIV coinfected persons.

### Nguồn gốc: Tuberculosis — đoạn 3671

• Recent advances are changing the picture, however. Simpler and quicker

### Nguồn gốc: Tuberculosis — đoạn 3672

tests for TB are being developed.

### Nguồn gốc: Tuberculosis — đoạn 3673

• A test recently approved (2011) for use is the Xpert MTB/RIF assay. It is

### Nguồn gốc: Tuberculosis — đoạn 3674

a nucleic acid amplification test that uses disposable cartridges with a tabletop GeneXpert instrument.

### Nguồn gốc: Tuberculosis — đoạn 3675

• Sputum samples from patients with suspected TB are mixed with the reagent

### Nguồn gốc: Tuberculosis — đoạn 3676

provided for the assay, and a cartridge containing this mixture is placed into the GeneXpert machine. All processing from this point on is fully automated and test results are available in 2 hours. Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Tuberculosis — đoạn 3677

Inform participants that the purpose of this role play activity is to enable participants to learn and practice their skills in counselling a pregnant woman about HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3678

• Using slide 23, present the instructions for the role play activity, making sure

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3679

participants understand their roles and responsibilities.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3680

• Read out the three role play scenarios (slides 24–26).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3681

• Divide the class into three to six groups (of five to six individuals per group).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3682

Assign each group a role play scenario and a supervisor/co-facilitator.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3683

• Ask them to carefully read the background information for the respective

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3684

roles: client, counsellor, observer – as described in their manual, before they proceed to act/or observe.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3685

• Each group must ensure that members practice the role of a counsellor by

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3686

rotating the roles).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3687

• Observers will give feedback at the end of each role play practice.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 23: Class activity – role plays — đoạn 3688

Role plays:

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for co-facilitators — đoạn 3689

• As participants perform the role plays, group facilitators should listen and observe in order to assess the

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for co-facilitators — đoạn 3690

participants’ counselling skills as well as messages given to the client.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for co-facilitators — đoạn 3691

• They should permit feedback and comments from peer observers before giving their own feedback.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for co-facilitators — đoạn 3692

• They should also monitor time to ensure that all participants have practiced.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for co-facilitators — đoạn 3693

• For large groups (comprising more than six participants), they could subdivide the groups into three and

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for co-facilitators — đoạn 3694

allow simultaneous role play practice by the subgroups.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for co-facilitators — đoạn 3695

• Care must be taken to ensure that group activities do not interfere with each other.

### Nguồn gốc: Instructions for co-facilitators — đoạn 3696

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3697

Bring the class back together after the role play activity is over. Start the feedback session by asking how many role plays were scored 5 by their peers. You may raise the following questions to get the discussions going.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3698

• Did the counsellors provide the necessary information about the disease?

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3699

• Did the counsellors put the clients at ease to discuss the topic?

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3700

• Was confidentiality ensured?

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3701

• Was the information provided clear and understandable?

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3702

• Did the counsellors help the clients plan how to go about resolving any problems or meeting their needs

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3703

regarding the infection?

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3704

• Do participants feel comfortable about counselling clients about the four infections discussed?

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3705

Summarize the discussions and address any concerns to end the role play activity.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3706

Slide 27: Care, treatment and support for pregnant woman with HIV, HBV, syphilis and TB

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3707

• Linkage to treatment and support is a key ingredient of the PITC strategy.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3708

• Ask participants to list some of the challenges that persons with HIV, TB and

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3709

STIs face in their communities, and to describe how these affect a pregnant woman’s ability to access care.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3710

• Write down their answers on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3711

• Present slide 27 and task a participant to read out the information on the

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3712

slide.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3713

• Ask participants if they know of any community-based support groups for

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3714

HIV, TB and the other diseases.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3715

Slide 28: Care and treatment for HIV, HBV, syphilis or TB

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3716

• Emphasize that the number and scheduling of antenatal visits may need

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3717

to be modified to enable the care provider to meet the health needs of the client who has tested positive for these infectious diseases.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3718

• Care must also be client-centred. Women with HIV and HBV, for example,

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3719

require long-term treatment and follow-up.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3720

• ANC care providers must therefore involve other relevant experts and

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3721

coordinate care with these team members right from when the diagnosis is made. Note that many countries have protocols in place to manage these infections. It is best for participants to learn and use existing national protocols for case management. Where necessary, adapt slides 29–30 accordingly to reflect existing national protocols.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3722

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3723

Slide 29: Care and treatment for mothers and newborns – HIV Present the slides or ask a participant to read the information. Highlight the following for HIV infection in pregnancy:

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3724

• Antiretroviral therapy (ART) or highly active antiretroviral therapy (HAART)

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3725

using a combination of medicines is the most effective treatment.

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3726

• Care providers must monitor for adherence to these medicines and check

### Nguồn gốc: End of activity feedback session: (15 minutes) — đoạn 3727

the patient for side-effects such as anaemia or poor response to treatment. Immunosuppression with AIDS occurs if the woman’s CD4 cell count falls below 200, leaving her more susceptible to infections.

### Nguồn gốc: HBV: universal HBV birth dose recommended for all newborns — đoạn 3728

•

### Nguồn gốc: FTC: emtricitabine, EFV: efavirenz — đoạn 3729

irrespective of maternal HBV status.

### Nguồn gốc: FTC: emtricitabine, EFV: efavirenz — đoạn 3730

• •

### Nguồn gốc: FTC: emtricitabine, EFV: efavirenz — đoạn 3731

Remind participants that each country decides on its treatment protocols based on several factors, including cost considerations and prevalence of HIV. The information below on ARVs is provided for the facilitator. Do not present it to the participants (it is not in the participant manual). However, it may be a useful reference if you need to answer questions about ARV treatment options.

### Nguồn gốc: Some facts on antiretroviral drugs — đoạn 3732

• Recommended antiretroviral treatment regimens are based on worldwide evidence from ART programmes.

### Nguồn gốc: Some facts on antiretroviral drugs — đoạn 3733

• Factors considered include the following: effectiveness of the drug, pill burden, dosing in relation to food

### Nguồn gốc: Some facts on antiretroviral drugs — đoạn 3734

toxicity, dosing frequency, nutritional requirements, convenience and drug interaction profiles, resistance to ARV, availability and cost.

### Nguồn gốc: Some facts on antiretroviral drugs — đoạn 3735

• Triple drug therapy (treatment combining three drugs) is presently the recommended treatment regimen for

### Nguồn gốc: Some facts on antiretroviral drugs — đoạn 3736

all persons needing ARV therapy. Mono therapy or dual therapy (treatment with one or two drugs only) is contraindicated. Recommended triple therapy regimens consist of:

### Nguồn gốc: Some facts on antiretroviral drugs — đoạn 3737

• two nucleoside/nucleotide reverse transcriptase inhibitors (NRTIs) and one non-nucleoside reverse

### Nguồn gốc: Some facts on antiretroviral drugs — đoạn 3738

transcriptase inhibitor (NNRTI) or two NRTIs and one boosted protease inhibitor (PI)

### Nguồn gốc: Nucleoside revers — đoạn 3739

transcriptase inhibitors (NRTI)

### Nguồn gốc: Nucleotide reverse — đoạn 3740

transcriptase inhibitor (NRTI)

### Nguồn gốc: Non-nucleoside — đoạn 3741

reverse transcriptase inhibitors (NNRTI)

### Nguồn gốc: Ritonavir-boosted lopinavir — đoạn 3742

(LPV/r)

### Nguồn gốc: Ritonavir-boosted atazanavir — đoạn 3743

(ATV/r)

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3744

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3745

A first-line regimen is the first option for treatment of all patients who fit the treatment criteria. A second-line regimen is used when there is evidence of treatment failure with the first-line regimen, and this must be confirmed preferably by CD4 and viral load monitoring where available.

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3746

A third-line or salvage therapy is recommended for heavily treatment-experienced patients and those who have failed second-line treatment. Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3747

Slide 30: Care and treatment for mothers and newborns – syphilis

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3748

• Present slide 30 and provide the following additional information on syphilis.

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3749

• When benzathine or procaine penicillin cannot be used (for example due

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3750

to penicillin allergy) or are not available (due to stock-outs), the WHO STI guidelines suggest using, with caution, erythromycin 500 mg orally four times daily for 14 days; or ceftriaxone 1 g intramuscularly once daily for 10–14 days; or azithromycin 2 g once orally.

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3751

• Although erythromycin and azithromycin treat the pregnant woman, they

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3752

do not cross the placental barrier completely and, as a result, the foetus is not treated. It is therefore necessary to treat the newborn infant soon after delivery. Slide 31: Care and treatment for mothers and newborns – TB

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3753

• Present slide 31 and also highlight the following:

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3754

• TB disease can be treated by taking several drugs for 6–12 months.

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3755

• It is very important that people who have TB disease finish the medicines

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3756

and take the drugs exactly as prescribed.

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3757

• Mention the difficulty of multidrug-resistant TB, especially among HIV

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3758

coinfected patients.

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3759

• The mother must be monitored for signs of clinical deterioration at each

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3760

visit.

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3761

• Her baby must preferably be referred for paediatrician assessment after

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3762

birth and before BCG is administered.

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3763

• BCG vaccine should also not be used in children who are known to be HIV

### Nguồn gốc: Emtricitabine (FTC) — đoạn 3764

coinfected. End the presentations on treatment and care by asking participants to share any best practices they might have that increase access to care and treatment for pregnant women diagnosed with TB, HIV, HBV and syphilis.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 3765

Slides 32 and 33: Key messages To end Session 11, ask a few participants to state one key message or highlight of the session for them. Next, present slides 32 and 33 to summarize and conclude the session.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 3766

• • • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 3767

HIV, hepatitis B, syphilis and TB testing/screening and counselling constitute an important task for all health workers caring for pregnant women. It is an entry point to provide prevention and appropriate care and support to the pregnant woman and her baby.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 3768

Test-positive women should be provided appropriate medicine(s) as soon as possible during pregnancy, and be linked to the appropriate care centres for long-term follow-up. HIV-positive women should receive a single daily dose of TDF + 3TC (FTC) + EFV for life, and should be advised that early infant diagnosis for HIV is offered after birth.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 3769

Pregnant women should be tested for syphilis during the first trimester and those positive should be given a single dose of 2 400 000 IU of benzathine benzylpenicillin as soon as possible. Pregnant woman with hepatitis B should be given TDF or entecavir where indicated.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 3770

Universal hepatitis B pentavalent vaccines are recommended for all newborns at birth. Women with TB, especially those with HIV coinfection, should be referred for directly observed therapy using short-course (DOTS) combination therapy of four drugs for at least six months.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 3771

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages — đoạn 3772

Session 12:

### Nguồn gốc: Ultrasonography in antenatal care — đoạn 3773

As part of preparations for this session, ensure that the supplies and materials required for the session are available and ready for use.

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3774

• Flip chart with paper and markers

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3775

• Training course materials: facilitator guide, participant manual

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3776

• Laptop

### Nguồn gốc: Materials — đoạn 3777

• Overhead projector

### Nguồn gốc: Some acronyms used in sonography — đoạn 3778

AC abdominal circumference

### Nguồn gốc: AFI — đoạn 3779

amniotic fluid index

### Nguồn gốc: CRL — đoạn 3780

crown-rump length FL femur length HC head circumference

### Nguồn gốc: MSD mean sac diameter — đoạn 3781

SDP single deepest pocket measurement, largest vertical pocket of amniotic fluid

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 3: Session aim and objectives — đoạn 3782

Present or task a participant to read the aim and objectives of the session (5 minutes). Aim: To enable participants to understand the benefits and common indications of ultrasound for effective patient care during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3783

On completion of this session, participants will be able to:

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3784

1. explain what ultrasound and ultrasonography are;

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3785

2. understand the benefits and indications of ultrasonography in antenatal

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3786

care;

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3787

3. recognize the limitations of ultrasound findings in clinical decision-making

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3788

in caring for the pregnant woman;

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3789

4. identify the correct timings for ultrasonography scans for different

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3790

pregnancy conditions or indications; and

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3791

5. describe how to prepare a client for the obstetric ultrasound procedure and

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3792

its outcomes.

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives: — đoạn 3793

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Introduction — đoạn 3794

•

### Nguồn gốc: Introduction — đoạn 3795

•

### Nguồn gốc: Introduction — đoạn 3796

To begin, ask participants how many of them have witnessed ultrasonography or requested this investigation for management of the pregnant woman. Do they provide ultrasonography in their settings? Encouage them to list some of the challenges they have encountered in its use.

### Nguồn gốc: Introduction — đoạn 3797

Refer to this list during the training session to address participants’ concerns and challenges.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: What is ultrasonography? — đoạn 3798

• Present slide 4. Explain that all ultrasound machines use a tool called a

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: What is ultrasonography? — đoạn 3799

transducer that uses sound waves and echoes to create pictures of internal organs and the baby on a computer.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: What is ultrasonography? — đoạn 3800

• The most common kinds of ultrasound during pregnancy are:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 4: What is ultrasonography? — đoạn 3801

o transabdominal ultrasound: the transducer is placed and moved over the abdomen to create the pictures; o transvaginal ultrasound: the ultrasound is done through the vagina. A thin transducer shaped like a wand is passed into the vagina; and o doppler ultrasound: here the transducer converts the ultrasound waves into audible waves. This approach is used to listen to the baby’s heartbeat and to measure the blood flow in the umbilical cord and in some of the baby’s blood vessels.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3802

• Present slide 5.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3803

• Although the general perception is that ultrasound imaging has no

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3804

adverse effects on the mother or the foetus, evidence collated from laboratory studies has shown effects of potential clinical significance.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3805

• Explain that the ALARA principle is therefore advocated to minimize

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 5: Obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3806

exposure to the barest minimum necessary.

### Nguồn gốc: Additional information: other types of available ultrasound scan — đoạn 3807

(a) Three-dimensional (3D) ultrasound: a 3D ultrasound takes thousands of pictures at once. It makes a 3D image that is almost as clear as a photograph. Some providers use this kind of ultrasound to check if the baby’s organs are growing and developing normally. It can also check for abnormalities in a baby’s face.

### Nguồn gốc: Additional information: other types of available ultrasound scan — đoạn 3808

A 3D ultrasound can also be used to check for problems in the uterus. (b) Four-dimensional (4D) ultrasound: this is like a 3D ultrasound, but it also shows the baby’s movements in a video format.

### Nguồn gốc: Additional information: other types of available ultrasound scan — đoạn 3809

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Use of ultrasound in pregnancy — đoạn 3810

• Ask participants, “what are the benefits and uses of ultrasound in pregnancy

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Use of ultrasound in pregnancy — đoạn 3811

care?”

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Use of ultrasound in pregnancy — đoạn 3812

• Then ask participants to list the reasons for which ultrasound scans have

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Use of ultrasound in pregnancy — đoạn 3813

been requested in their workplaces.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Use of ultrasound in pregnancy — đoạn 3814

• Highlight the fact that ultrasonography has been used safely during

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Use of ultrasound in pregnancy — đoạn 3815

pregnancy for over 40 years.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Use of ultrasound in pregnancy — đoạn 3816

• It has become an invaluable third eye and is an essential and useful tool for

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Use of ultrasound in pregnancy — đoạn 3817

screening, diagnosis and surveillance of maternal and foetal health.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Use of ultrasound in pregnancy — đoạn 3818

• WHO recommends the use of ultrasound to monitor foetal development

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 7: Use of ultrasound in pregnancy — đoạn 3819

and well-being.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8 and 9: Routine vs indicated ultrasound — đoạn 3820

• During pregnancy, ultrasound scans may be done for all women routinely or

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8 and 9: Routine vs indicated ultrasound — đoạn 3821

for a specific reason (indicated).

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8 and 9: Routine vs indicated ultrasound — đoạn 3822

• Present slide 8.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8 and 9: Routine vs indicated ultrasound — đoạn 3823

• Routine scans are performed at specified gestation periods during the

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8 and 9: Routine vs indicated ultrasound — đoạn 3824

pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8 and 9: Routine vs indicated ultrasound — đoạn 3825

• WHO recommends one ultrasound scan before 24 weeks of gestation

### Nguồn gốc: Slides 8 and 9: Routine vs indicated ultrasound — đoạn 3826

(early ultrasound) to estimate gestational age, improve detection of foetal anomalies and multiple pregnancies, reduce induction of labour for post-term pregnancy, and improve a woman’s pregnancy experience. done easily.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 3827

• Present slide 9 and ask participants to list other reasons for which they would

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 3828

request an ultrasound scan during antenatal care.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 3829

• Refer them to Table 12.1 on “Common indications of ultrasound scanning

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 3830

during pregnancy” in the participant manual.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 3831

• Ask a participant to read out the information in the table.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 3832

• Encourage participants to identify the indications for ultrasound in each of

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 9 — đoạn 3833

the three trimesters.

### Nguồn gốc: Limitations of ultrasonography during pregnancy — đoạn 3834

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Limitations of ultrasonography during pregnancy — đoạn 3835

Despite being a tool of considerable significance in pregnancy surveillance, there are limitations on the use of ultrasound that must be appreciated by both the care provider and the antenatal client. Risks associated with the use of ultrasound in pregnancy (as with all other medical diagnostic tools) can be categorized into diagnostic errors and possible biological side-effects.

### Nguồn gốc: Limitations of ultrasonography during pregnancy — đoạn 3836

Fully understanding the limitations and benefits of ultrasonography is important to minimize errors in clinical decisions for patient care and reduce abuse of the procedure.

### Nguồn gốc: Limitations of ultrasonography during pregnancy — đoạn 3837

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Limitations of obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3838

• Present slide 10.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Limitations of obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3839

• Using the information on the slide, discuss the key considerations when

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Limitations of obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3840

requesting an ultrasound during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Limitations of obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3841

• Use examples to highlight the limiting factors below.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Limitations of obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3842

o Mild pre-eclampsia – no abnormalities will be noted in the baby o The older the pregnancy, the less accurate the calculated EDD using size measurements o The more obese the mother is, the poorer the image quality, which in turn negatively affects the detection rate of foetal anomalies.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3843

• Present the slide and explain that it is important for ANC care providers to

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3844

appreciate the limitations of ultrasounds when reviewing the reports they receive.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3845

• Moreover, they must always make care decisions for the client with due

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3846

consideration of the clinical presentations and observations also made.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3847

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3848

Slide 12–16: Common obstetric measurements and their limitations •

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3849

•

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3850

related calculations with regard to gestational age, expected date of delivery, and foetal weight and well-being. Highlight the margins of error listed for the various measurements and discuss how these might affect clinical decisions such as planned delivery by caesarean section or inductions.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3851

Slide 12–16 (continued): Common obstetric measurements and their limitations

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3852

• Ask participants to share any experiences that they may have had where

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Key considerations when reviewing ultrasound (US) reports — đoạn 3853

not recognizing the limitations of scan measurements had led to poor decision-making?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Preparing a client for obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3854

• Ask participants to describe how they prepare their clients for ultrasound.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Preparing a client for obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3855

• Note their responses and present slide 17.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Preparing a client for obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3856

• Emphasize the importance of the preparation steps listed in optimizing the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Preparing a client for obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3857

outcomes of the procedure.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Preparing a client for obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3858

• Ultrasound scans must only be requested for medical reasons.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Preparing a client for obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3859

• Clients must understand the benefits and risks of the scan – including the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Preparing a client for obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3860

possibility of misdiagnosis.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Preparing a client for obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3861

• In settings where scans are only available in private facilities, it is

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Preparing a client for obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3862

important to direct clients to certified sonographers to minimize the challenge of misdiagnosis and abuse.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 17: Preparing a client for obstetric ultrasound — đoạn 3863

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3864

Slides 18 and 19: Educating and counselling on ultrasound

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3865

• Ask participants to describe any experiences they have had breaking news

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3866

of scan reports to a client/ couple.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3867

• Explain that, generally, pregnant women and their partners put a very high

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3868

value on obstetric ultrasound examinations. They often believe that an ultrasound examination will provide clear-cut answers on the exact expected date of delivery and accurately tell whether something is wrong with their babies or not. They often do not always understand the limitations and potential disadvantages of pregnancy ultrasounds. Therefore, pre-scan counselling is necessary to dispel such myths.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3869

• Ultrasound findings, if not properly communicated, can contribute to

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3870

unnecessary anxiety for the expectant mother and her family. This is particularly important where findings indicate bad news such as foetal abnormality, demise or presence of pregnancy complications. The worry and anxiety caused by poorly communicated ultrasound examination findings can have long-lasting effects.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3871

• Present the information on slides 18 and 19. Highlight key steps for

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3872

breaking bad news to clients. Note that these steps follow basic counselling principles.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3873

Slide 20 – 21: Class activity – debate

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3874

• Present the topic for the debate activity and explain its purpose.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3875

• Review all instructions on the slide with participants so that everyone

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3876

understands how the debate will work and how to prepare.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3877

• Divide the class into the three groups.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3878

• Choose three people from the audience group to be the judges. The

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3879

facilitator will act as moderator.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3880

• Provide the debating teams with their reference materials (see text below).

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3881

• Encourage them to conduct additional searches to enrich their arguments.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3882

Allow each group 10 minutes to prepare for the debate whilst you set up the room. Also educate the three judges on their scoring system whilst the groups prepare.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3883

• Total time for the debate is 30 minutes.

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3884

• After the debate is finished and the winning side determined, summarize the

### Nguồn gốc: Educating/counselling clients on ultrasound findings — đoạn 3885

points made in this debate and record them on a flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Examples of arguments for and against ultrasound are provided below — đoạn 3886

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: A: It is safe — đoạn 3887

• Ultrasound is, arguably, the most commonly used diagnostic procedure in obstetrics.

### Nguồn gốc: A: It is safe — đoạn 3888

• Its advantages are: it is non-invasive, convenient, painless, and yields immediate and extensive results.

### Nguồn gốc: A: It is safe — đoạn 3889

• While the ultrasound scan is not 100% accurate, it is widely considered to be safe for both mother and unborn

### Nguồn gốc: A: It is safe — đoạn 3890

baby.

### Nguồn gốc: A: It is safe — đoạn 3891

• According to the American College of Obstetricians and Gynaecologists, there have been no documented

### Nguồn gốc: A: It is safe — đoạn 3892

reports of negative effects on the foetus from diagnostic ultrasound procedures.

### Nguồn gốc: A: It is safe — đoạn 3893

• A review of over 50 medical studies shows that ultrasounds do not pose any danger to mothers or foetuses. They

### Nguồn gốc: A: It is safe — đoạn 3894

do not cause birth defects, childhood developmental or intellectual problems, or cancer. “It is unlikely that the routine ultrasounds done during pregnancy – which last 15 minutes or less – could cause harm.”

### Nguồn gốc: A: It is safe — đoạn 3895

• Foetuses can hear ultrasound examinations. Ultrasound examinations during pregnancy expose the foetus to a

### Nguồn gốc: A: It is safe — đoạn 3896

sound as loud as that made by a subway train coming into a station, say US researchers. But doctors do not think the experience causes a baby any lasting harm. ... The sound was similar to the highest notes on a piano.

### Nguồn gốc: B: It improves diagnosis and care provision — đoạn 3897

• Ultrasound is used during pregnancy to check the baby’s development, the presence of a multiple pregnancy, and

### Nguồn gốc: B: It improves diagnosis and care provision — đoạn 3898

to help pick up any abnormalities. o It can screen for birth defects, like spina bifida or heart defects. o It can help with other prenatal tests, like chorionic villus sampling (also called CVS) or amniocentesis (also called amnio). CVS involves taking cells from the placenta for testing.

### Nguồn gốc: B: It improves diagnosis and care provision — đoạn 3899

The placenta is a tissue that provides nutrients for the baby. Amnio is a test where amniotic fluid and cells are taken from the sac around the baby. o It can check for pregnancy complications, such as ectopic pregnancy, molar pregnancy, and miscarriage.

### Nguồn gốc: B: It improves diagnosis and care provision — đoạn 3900

• Ultrasound is therefore an essential and useful tool for screening, diagnosis and surveillance of maternal and

### Nguồn gốc: B: It improves diagnosis and care provision — đoạn 3901

foetal health. C: It enhances the positive pregnancy experience for the couple and increases bonding

### Nguồn gốc: B: It improves diagnosis and care provision — đoạn 3902

• Ultrasound provides parents with the first glimpses of their developing baby and enhances bonding. Ultrasound

### Nguồn gốc: B: It improves diagnosis and care provision — đoạn 3903

screening enables expectant parents to experience the pregnancy as more real and gives partners, especially, the opportunity to experience and see the pregnancy. The expectant father meets and connects with the foetus.

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3904

• There is to date insufficient evidence about the absolute safety of pregnancy ultrasounds.

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3905

• Although the general perception is that ultrasound imaging has no adverse effects on the mother or the
