### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3906

foetus, evidence collated from laboratory studies has shown effects of potential clinical significance. Potential bioeffects of ultrasound can be either thermal or mechanical.

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3907

• More studies around safety are warranted [46, 48], especially since experimental studies on foetal mice have

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3908

shown adverse effects.

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3909

• Although previous systematic reviews regarding safety have been unable to find evidence of significant adverse

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3910

maternal, perinatal or childhood outcomes [6, 46], an association between exposure to ultrasound in pregnancy and non-right handedness has been found.

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3911

• Pregnant physiotherapists who provided ultrasound treatments for more than 20 hours per week were found to

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3912

have an increased risk for spontaneous abortions.

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3913

• There are some concerns that children who have been exposed to ultrasound while in their mothers’ uterus have

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3914

a greater chance of suffering from dyslexia and other speech and learning problems.

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3915

• The uncertainties regarding safety are evident in the recommendations of prudent use and adherence to the

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3916

ALARA (as low as reasonably achievable) principle.

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3917

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: A: Safety concerns — đoạn 3918

B: It is costly and frequently inaccessible for persons in low-resource settings

### Nguồn gốc: C: Misdiagnosis and interference with the positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 3919

• •

### Nguồn gốc: C: Misdiagnosis and interference with the positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 3920

• •

### Nguồn gốc: C: Misdiagnosis and interference with the positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 3921

•

### Nguồn gốc: C: Misdiagnosis and interference with the positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 3922

• •

### Nguồn gốc: C: Misdiagnosis and interference with the positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 3923

Ultrasound is an invaluable third eye, but it cannot see everything. Sometimes ultrasounds create unnecessary stress. There are situations where the use of ultrasound had caused more harm than good, as deviations picked up that were not clear, creating a lot of anxiety and stress for the pregnant woman and her family. Many examples exist of false positive findings that caused distress or even ruined the experience of pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: C: Misdiagnosis and interference with the positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 3924

Uncertain answers arising from difficult to interpret ultrasound findings were described as hard to deal with for expectant parents. Ultrasounds in the second and third trimesters are not perfect. A 2014 study from France found that, out of 526 pregnancy ultrasounds performed during the said periods, 8.8% of cases showed false positive results for foetal anomaly and 9.2% showed misclassification of potential anomalies.

### Nguồn gốc: C: Misdiagnosis and interference with the positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 3925

The worry and anxiety caused by the ultrasound examination can have long-lasting effects; the belief and anxiety about something being wrong with the child sometimes lasts throughout the child’s upbringing.

### Nguồn gốc: D: Effect on obstetric skills and practice — đoạn 3926

Reliance on ultrasound is now sometimes outweighing clinical experience. There are concerns that some clinical skills are lost because of the increased use of obstetric ultrasound. The liberal use/abuse of obstetric ultrasound is occurring for reasons including avoidance of blame by colleagues or expectant parents if major problems were not detected or anything subsequently went wrong.

### Nguồn gốc: Arguments against (continued) — đoạn 3927

•

### Nguồn gốc: Arguments against (continued) — đoạn 3928

Care providers sometimes find it particularly difficult to communicate complex information from ultrasound

### Nguồn gốc: findings to people with lower levels of education, or where there are language barriers an — đoạn 3929

via interpreters. This create stress for care providers and clients. For example, You know your baby has this, it has a 20% chance of being very severely disabled, a 30% chance of mild disability, and a 50% chance of being normal.

### Nguồn gốc: E: Non-medical use/abuse of scans and ethical challenges — đoạn 3930

• There is a growing phenomenon of non-medical or so-called ‘entertainment’ ultrasound which is raising concern

### Nguồn gốc: E: Non-medical use/abuse of scans and ethical challenges — đoạn 3931

among pregnancy care providers.

### Nguồn gốc: E: Non-medical use/abuse of scans and ethical challenges — đoạn 3932

• Non-medical use of obstetric ultrasound can be defined as situations where the purpose of the examination is

### Nguồn gốc: E: Non-medical use/abuse of scans and ethical challenges — đoạn 3933

solely to view the foetus, take its picture, or determine its sex without medical indication.

### Nguồn gốc: E: Non-medical use/abuse of scans and ethical challenges — đoạn 3934

• An ethical analysis of non-medical foetal ultrasound concludes that obstetric ultrasound practice is ethically

### Nguồn gốc: E: Non-medical use/abuse of scans and ethical challenges — đoạn 3935

justifiable only if the indication for its use is based on medical evidence. Furthermore, the practice of non-medical or ‘entertainment’ ultrasound is discouraged by governments and professional bodies.

### Nguồn gốc: E: Non-medical use/abuse of scans and ethical challenges — đoạn 3936

• In some settings, non-desired babies (sex, down syndrome) are aborted in preference for desired babies because

### Nguồn gốc: E: Non-medical use/abuse of scans and ethical challenges — đoạn 3937

of routine ultrasound.

### Nguồn gốc: E: Non-medical use/abuse of scans and ethical challenges — đoạn 3938

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Feedback after debate — đoạn 3939

After the debate is over and the winning side determined, summarize the points made in this debate by presenting slides 22–27.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 28: Conclusion to the debate — đoạn 3940

• Conclude the debate by presenting slide 28. • Clarify any concerns.

### Nguồn gốc: Side 29: Key messages — đoạn 3941

Conclude the session by presenting slide 29 on key messages.

### Nguồn gốc: Side 29: Key messages — đoạn 3942

• Ultrasonography is an important tool during ANC for early detection of

### Nguồn gốc: Side 29: Key messages — đoạn 3943

complications and monitoring the progress of pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: Side 29: Key messages — đoạn 3944

• WHO recommends one ultrasound scan for all pregnant women before 24

### Nguồn gốc: Side 29: Key messages — đoạn 3945

weeks of gestation to: o establish gestational age; o improve detection of foetal anomalies and multiple pregnancies; o reduce induction of labour for post-term pregnancy; and o improve pregnancy outcomes.

### Nguồn gốc: Side 29: Key messages — đoạn 3946

• Requirements when requesting ultrasonography include clearly stating the

### Nguồn gốc: Side 29: Key messages — đoạn 3947

reason for the scan and providing vital information such as LMP, if known.

### Nguồn gốc: Side 29: Key messages — đoạn 3948

• Care must be taken when giving scan reports in order to reduce stress and

### Nguồn gốc: Side 29: Key messages — đoạn 3949

protect the positive pregnancy experience.

### Nguồn gốc: Side 29: Key messages — đoạn 3950

• Ultrasonography reports must be interpreted alongside other clinical

### Nguồn gốc: findings. — đoạn 3951

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 12. What dosage of iron and folate is given to treat anaemia in a pregnant woman and for h — đoạn 3984

given? Answer: Two tablets (120 mg iron and 400 mcg folic acid) every day until haemoglobin is normal and then continued for three months.

### Nguồn gốc: 13. What are the advantages of HIV, HBV and syphilis testing for a pregnant woman? — đoạn 3985

Answer: • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: 13. What are the advantages of HIV, HBV and syphilis testing for a pregnant woman? — đoạn 3986

Woman will know her status and take steps to protect herself and her family

### Nguồn gốc: Woman will receive treatment if tested positive — đoạn 3987

Woman can be given ARVs to prevent mother-to-child transmission of the disease Baby can be protected after birth by immunization (for example against HBV)

### Nguồn gốc: 14. A pregnant woman with TB must be treated using multiple anti-TB medicines given as dir — đoạn 3988

treatments (DOTs). True or false? Answer: True – she is treated with a combination of four drugs (RIPE): isoniazid, ethambutol, rifampicin and pyrazinamide for at least six months, given as DOTs.

### Nguồn gốc: 15. Name one drug that is recommended to treat a syphilis-positive pregnant woman? — đoạn 3989

Answer: Pregnant women should be tested for syphilis during the first trimester and those positive should be given, as soon as possible, a single dose of 2 400 000 IU of benzathine benzylpenicillin.

### Nguồn gốc: 16. Name three antiretroviral medicines given to prevent mother-to-child transmission of H — đoạn 3990

Answer: HIV-positive women should receive a single daily dose of TDF + 3TC (FTC) + EFV for life, and should be advised that the baby will also be given antiretrovirals (NVP or AZT) after birth and whilst breastfeeding. They will also be offered early infant diagnosis for HIV.

### Nguồn gốc: 17. A baby born to an HBV-positive mother must be given hepatis B immunoglobulin and hepat — đoạn 3991

birth. True or false? Answer: True – all babies must now receive HB vaccine to protect them from infection irrespective of the mother’s status. Those with infected mothers additionally receive immunoglobulins to mop up any maternally transmitted infections.

### Nguồn gốc: 17. A baby born to an HBV-positive mother must be given hepatis B immunoglobulin and hepat — đoạn 3992

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

Answer: • • • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Determination of adequacy of liquor volume — đoạn 3994

Identification of uterine tumours that may obstruct childbirth.

### Nguồn gốc: recommendations? — đoạn 3995

Answer: One ultrasound scan before 24 weeks of gestation (early ultrasound, best around 22–24 weeks) is recommended for pregnant women, to estimate gestational age, improve detection of foetal anomalies and multiple pregnancies, reduce induction of labour for post-term pregnancy, and improve the woman’s pregnancy experience.

### Nguồn gốc: False — đoạn 3997

### Nguồn gốc: Rapid HIV test — đoạn 4091

The rapid test indicates whether HIV-1 or HIV-2 antibodies are present. Results are available within 20 minutes. If one line appears on the strip, it means that the person is not infected with HIV (with 99.8% accuracy). If two lines appear, the person is likely infected (99.3% accuracy).

### Nguồn gốc: Rapid malaria test — đoạn 4092

The RDT is used to determine if a person with malaria-like symptoms actually has malaria. The malaria parasites in the blood of an infected individual produce chemicals (proteins) called antigens. The RDT detects these malaria antigens in a person’s blood.

### Nguồn gốc: Rapid syphilis test — đoạn 4093

The rapid syphilis tests currently available are treponemal tests. Their only disadvantage is that they cannot distinguish between active and past treated infections as the antibodies to treponemal antigens are retained for years. The treatment of all test-positive women will therefore result in overtreatment. Given the serious consequences of missed treatment, the benefits of treatment far outweigh the harm of overtreatment.

### Nguồn gốc: Rapid hepatitis B test — đoạn 4094

Hepatitis B rapid screening test is a test for the qualitative detection of the hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) in the whole blood. Although the specific instructions for processing each RDT may differ, most tests are performed with only three or four steps and often include the following:

### Nguồn gốc: Rapid hepatitis B test — đoạn 4095

1. remove the test kit from its wrapper and place on a flat surface;

### Nguồn gốc: Rapid hepatitis B test — đoạn 4096

2. add a specified amount of patient sample (whole blood, plasma or serum) to the sample well;

### Nguồn gốc: Rapid hepatitis B test — đoạn 4097

3. add a specified amount of diluent buffer (or reagent) to the sample well; and

### Nguồn gốc: Rapid hepatitis B test — đoạn 4098

4. read the results after a specified time (usually 15–20 minutes).

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Skill 3: Measuring blood pressure — đoạn 4133

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Skill 3: Measuring blood pressure — đoạn 4134

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8: Skill 3: Measuring blood pressure — đoạn 4140

### Nguồn gốc: 8. Keep the upper arm at the heart level with the lower arm passively supported (for examp — đoạn 4141

lap).

### Nguồn gốc: 10. Inflated the cuff to at least 30 mmHg above the point at which the radial pulse disapp — đoạn 4142

cuff slowly at a rate of 2 to 3 mmHg per second. (Deflation rates greater than 2 mmHg per second can cause the systolic pressure to appear lower and the diastolic pressure to appear higher). The first and last audible sounds should be recorded as systolic and diastolic pressure. In pregnant women, the fifth Korotkoff sound has been recommended as the diastolic measurement.

### Nguồn gốc: Using the pregnancy wheel — đoạn 4163

The pregnancy wheel is made up of two wheels:

### Nguồn gốc: An outer bigger wheel with a calendar — đoạn 4164

year outline at its rim. Dates are at weekly intervals.

### Nguồn gốc: An inner smaller wheel with pregnancy — đoạn 4165

duration at weekly intervals, from week 0 to week 42. It has a mark/arrow indicating the LMP, which begins the dating (gestation week 0); and another indicating EDC (40 weeks gestation).

### Nguồn gốc: An inner smaller wheel with pregnancy — đoạn 4166

To determine EDC, position the mark for LMP on the inner wheel to correspond to the given LMP date on the outer wheel calendar. Locate the EDC date on the outer wheel by looking for the date that corresponds to the EDC marker on the inner wheel.

### Nguồn gốc: An inner smaller wheel with pregnancy — đoạn 4167

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Mrs B comes to the antenatal clinic on 15 May this year. She tells you that her last no — đoạn 4169

period started on 10 March. What is her EDC? (Answer: 17 December) What is her gestational age? (Answer: 9 weeks)

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Mrs D comes to the antenatal clinic on 15 May this year. She tells you that her last no — đoạn 4170

period started on 1 January. What is her EDC? (Answer: 8 October)

### Nguồn gốc: Background information — đoạn 4191

The Lorgoligi clinic is located in Lorgoligi town, in the Zangara district of the northwest region of country A. The main occupation of the area is farming. The expected number of new pregnancies at the clinic in 2019 was

### Nguồn gốc: 4850. Total live births recorded during the period was 3499. — đoạn 4192

Antenatal clinic at the facility is held from Mondays to Fridays. The clinic is manned by three midwives and a doctor who sees referrals. They have two consulting cubicles and a large open waiting area where general health talks/group counselling sessions are held. Women visiting the clinic frequently complain that they spend too much time at the clinic.

### Nguồn gốc: 4850. Total live births recorded during the period was 3499. — đoạn 4193

The clinic routinely offers IPTp-SP, iron/folate supplementation and screening for anaemia and infectious diseases. Unfortunately, they sometimes face stock-outs that affect these services. Using the information and data provided below, answer the following questions.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. List three care services that will be most affected if pregnant women in Lorgoligi do n — đoạn 4194

pregnancy for antenatal care.

that women are not anaemic before delivery? Explain your answer?

### Nguồn gốc: Region: Northwest — đoạn 4201

Total life births at the facility (January–June 2019) = 3499.

10845

### Nguồn gốc: Total — đoạn 4203

Answer sheet: Review participants’ answers and supplement as needed with the following answers 1.

### Nguồn gốc: Total — đoạn 4204

What is the ANC coverage for the six-month period of January to June 2019 (first and fourth visits)? Answers: first visit: 1866/3499 x 100 = 53.3%; fourth visit: 1551/3499 x 100 = 44.3%

### Nguồn gốc: Total — đoạn 4205

2.

### Nguồn gốc: Total — đoạn 4206

What proportion of women attending the clinic from January to June were teenagers? Answer: 433/1866 x 100 = 23.2%

3.

Answer: Yes, one out of every five pregnant women attending the clinic is less than 18 years of age.

### Nguồn gốc: Is teenage pregnancy a problem in Lorgoligi town? — đoạn 4209

4.

What proportion of women booked for antenatal care during their first trimester? Answer: 488/1866 x 100 = 26.1%

### Nguồn gốc: Is teenage pregnancy a problem in Lorgoligi town? — đoạn 4211

5.

Answers:

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4214

6.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4215

What proportion of women were anaemic at 36 weeks gestation (January to June) Answer: 156/746 x 100 = 20.9%

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4216

7.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4217

Has the strategy for anaemia prevention and treatment in pregnancy been successful in ensuring that women are not anaemic before delivery? Explain your answer? Answer: Not in Lorgoligi. About 29% of mothers were anaemic at booking. If the principle is that no woman should enter labour with Hb < 11 g/dL, then care providers should have achieved a 0% anaemia prevalence rate at 36 weeks. The decrease in anaemia prevalence rates from booking was unsatisfactory.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4218

8.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4219

What proportion of women attending the clinic were tested for syphilis over the six-month period? Answer: 7/1866 x 100 = 0.37%

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4220

9.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4221

What possible reasons can be given for the low test-positive rates for syphilis at the facility? Answer: Even though 1834 women were counselled, only seven tests were performed, with four (42.9%) positive. It appears that the facility experienced stock-outs of test kits as none was available or used from March to June.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4222

10.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4223

Do you think the clinic requires additional consulting cubicles and staff? Explain. Answer: Yes, the clinic requires additional staff and cubicles. The clinic attended to 10 845 client visits in the sixmonth period, averaging about 90 clients per day (10 845/20 working days x 6 months). With only two consulting cubicles, a midwife manning each cubicle attends to about 45 clients a day. If 10 minutes are spent with each client, then it will take seven hours 30 minutes for each midwife to attend to all 45 clients.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4224

This is a very heavy workload, which explains the long client waiting times and dissatisfaction with services. It could also lead to staff burnout and poor-quality care.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Malaria prevention, including IPTp – 1 — đoạn 4225

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4235

Slides 13 and 14: Key messages

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4236

• Present slides 13 and 14 to summarize the key messages as follows:

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4237

• When performing clinical skills, care providers should respect the rights of

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4238

their clients and be accountable for their actions.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4239

• Care providers must explain procedures/tests (planned and/or performed)

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4240

to the antenatal client using language and terms that the client can easily understand.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4241

• Recommended infection prevention practices must be applied when

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4242

performing all skills.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4243

• When performing procedures and tests (such as RDTs), the provider must

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4244

adhere to the recommended test protocols and steps to ensure valid and accurate test results.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4245

• All tests/procedures and their outcomes must be recorded on the woman’s

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4246

maternal health record book and clinic-based registers.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4247

• Regular and periodic analyses of clinic data must be conducted at the clinic

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4248

to inform clinic performance and management decisions.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4249

• Measuring the extent to which evidence-based interventions are used in

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4250

routine clinical practice provides a detailed picture of the strengths and weaknesses of antenatal service delivery at the facility.

### Nguồn gốc: Key messages (10 minutes) — đoạn 4251

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4252

Prepare the following materials for this session:

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4253

• flip chart with paper and markers

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4254

• training course materials: facilitator guide, participant manual

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4255

• laptop

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4256

• overhead projector

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4257

An essential component of this course is the provision of continuing support to participants to enable them to implement their new knowledge and skills towards creating a positive pregnancy experience for their clients. In this session, the facilitator will work with participants to plan for follow-up and support.

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4258

Note to the facilitator:

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4259

• Agree with the responsible Programme Manager beforehand on the follow-up schedule and procedures based on

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4260

the prevailing situation in the country.

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4261

• The course director or another supervisor should tell participants how and when the follow-up will be conducted.

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4262

• Be prepared to tell participants what to expect in terms of follow-up. (Will a facilitator visit them in their facility,

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4263

or will they come to a meeting to discuss their progress?)

### Nguồn gốc: Implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4264

• The rest of the session describes the work that they should do in the interim to prepare for follow-up.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 1: Title slide — đoạn 4265

• Present slide 1 to welcome participants to this final session of the course.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 1: Title slide — đoạn 4266

• Explain that this is not an academic session but a preparation for

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 1: Title slide — đoạn 4267

actual implementation of lessons learned at their respective facilities, and for related follow-up and support.

### Nguồn gốc: Aim — đoạn 4268

To help participants plan how they will apply the newly acquired knowledge and skills to provide ANC when they return to their own workplaces and, during follow-up, discuss important questions and challenges with a facilitator or other experienced providers.

### Nguồn gốc: Aim — đoạn 4269

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 4273

2. describe the difficulties that they anticipate with ANC implementation and

how they will overcome them; and

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 4275

3. describe how they will prepare an antenatal case study for presentation at a

### Nguồn gốc: Objectives — đoạn 4276

follow-up visit or session several weeks after the training.

• describe how they have implemented the ANC action plan in their own

facilities;

### Nguồn gốc: 4. During follow-up, participants will: — đoạn 4279

• present a case study of a pregnant woman and how ANC was provided

### Nguồn gốc: 4. During follow-up, participants will: — đoạn 4280

to her;

### Nguồn gốc: 4. During follow-up, participants will: — đoạn 4281

• discuss the lessons learned from the case study, emphasizing the

### Nguồn gốc: 4. During follow-up, participants will: — đoạn 4282

importance of ANC in achieving positive pregnancy outcomes; and explain the importance of reflecting on practices to evaluate and improve care.

### Nguồn gốc: Challenges in implementing quality ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4283

• •

### Nguồn gốc: Challenges in implementing quality ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4284

Explain that planning how to implement the newly acquired knowledge and skills is essential for being able to apply them once you return to your facility. As expected, there will be several challenges to overcome during implementation of the newly learned skills.

### Nguồn gốc: Challenges in implementing quality ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4285

Slide 5 – 7: Challenges in implementing ANC for a positive pregnancy experience

### Nguồn gốc: Challenges in implementing quality ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4286

• Several factors can influence the successful implementation of quality

### Nguồn gốc: Challenges in implementing quality ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4287

antenatal care. They include the availability of specific skills such as those just acquired by the participants.

### Nguồn gốc: Challenges in implementing quality ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4288

• Ask participants to list other challenges that they may encounter when

### Nguồn gốc: Challenges in implementing quality ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4289

trying to implement their new skills in their settings. Note their answers on the flip chart.

### Nguồn gốc: Challenges in implementing quality ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4290

• Present slide 5 to summarize and enrich the list developed.

### Nguồn gốc: Challenges in implementing quality ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4291

• Using slides 6 and 7, discuss each of the challenges listed with some

### Nguồn gốc: Challenges in implementing quality ANC for a positive pregnancy experience — đoạn 4292

examples.

### Nguồn gốc: Job guideline/policy restrictions — đoạn 4293

• National policies and guidelines may restrict the performance of certain

### Nguồn gốc: Job guideline/policy restrictions — đoạn 4294

procedures to a certain category of health care workers. For example, doctors may be the only persons allowed to administer intravenous medication in some areas; laboratory technicians may be the only persons allowed to conduct HIV testing. Enabling midwives to perform the relevant procedures, such as HIV tests, may require some national policy revisions.

### Nguồn gốc: Staff skills and roles — đoạn 4295

• Health care workers may not feel confident to perform certain ANC

### Nguồn gốc: Staff skills and roles — đoạn 4296

tasks because they do not feel competent. This gap may be overcome by competency-based training. On the other hand, some health workers may be accustomed to a certain role and be reluctant to change – even if they have the necessary skills. In this case, a supervisor may need to revise job descriptions/roles and have a meeting to discuss the new roles and the

### Nguồn gốc: rationale for the change. — đoạn 4297

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Space and infrastructure requirements — đoạn 4298

Many traditional antenatal clinics lack the necessary space and infrastructure to accommodate all ANC components within the cubicle or ANC area. Infection prevention requires that rapid HIV tests and syphilis tests, for example, are performed on a surface other than the consulting table.

### Nguồn gốc: Time and workload constraints — đoạn 4299

The comprehensive nature of ANC requires that many more services are offered to the client at each contact by one care provider: abdominal examination, rapid tests, dispensing medications, and counselling. These can add to the workload, particularly in very busy clinics with few staff.

### Nguồn gốc: Logistics management — đoạn 4300

Resources are very limited in many countries of sub-Saharan Africa. Frequent stock-outs are a challenge. Procedures that require certain supplies such as test kits or reagents may not be performed when the resources are not available.

### Nguồn gốc: Preparing an action plan for quality ANC implementation in your — đoạn 4301

facility

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8–9: Developing an action plan for ANC implementation — đoạn 4302

• Discuss the key considerations in developing a simple action plan. List and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8–9: Developing an action plan for ANC implementation — đoạn 4303

include the following questions:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8–9: Developing an action plan for ANC implementation — đoạn 4304

• WHAT change is needed?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8–9: Developing an action plan for ANC implementation — đoạn 4305

o List the changes that are needed in the set-up of the facility, including in consulting cubicles, laboratory services, medicines, supplies, or record-keeping. o List the changes needed in the way staff members are assigned to tasks and/or to antenatal clients.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8–9: Developing an action plan for ANC implementation — đoạn 4306

• Also list the changes needed in the way staff members provide ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8–9: Developing an action plan for ANC implementation — đoạn 4307

o HOW can the change happen? o WHO will take responsibility for implementing the change? o WHO will help implement the change? o WHERE will the action take place? o WHEN will the action take place? o HOW and WHEN will it be evaluated?

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8–9: Developing an action plan for ANC implementation — đoạn 4308

• Present the sample chart in slide 9 to describe the format for an

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 8–9: Developing an action plan for ANC implementation — đoạn 4309

implementation plan of action.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Preparing the action plan — đoạn 4310

• Present the instructions for the class activity and develop action plans using

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Preparing the action plan — đoạn 4311

slide 10.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Preparing the action plan — đoạn 4312

• Divide the class into groups based on facility or type of facility.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Preparing the action plan — đoạn 4313

• Task each group to develop an action plan using the key considerations

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Preparing the action plan — đoạn 4314

presented earlier.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Preparing the action plan — đoạn 4315

• Give 20 minutes for the activity.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Preparing the action plan — đoạn 4316

• Remind the groups that they will have to provide a copy of their plans to the

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 10: Preparing the action plan — đoạn 4317

course organizers for use in follow-up.

### Nguồn gốc: Review of action plans — đoạn 4318

• When participants present their action plans, it is an opportunity to see what they have learned about

### Nguồn gốc: Review of action plans — đoạn 4319

antenatal care and what they have realized about their own clinics and practices.

### Nguồn gốc: Review of action plans — đoạn 4320

• Read over the plans with them and make suggestions as you feel appropriate.

### Nguồn gốc: Review of action plans — đoạn 4321

• Ask them to discuss the difficulties they anticipate and how they will overcome them.

### Nguồn gốc: Review of action plans — đoạn 4322

• Give them support for their ideas and enthusiasm.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing for follow up after training — đoạn 4324

• Inform participants that a follow-up visit by the facilitator will take place four

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing for follow up after training — đoạn 4325

to six weeks after completion of this course.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing for follow up after training — đoạn 4326

• Facilitators may elect to visit participants at their respective places of work

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing for follow up after training — đoạn 4327

or may organize a special session bringing together a group of participants.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing for follow up after training — đoạn 4328

• At these follow-up visits or sessions, each participant will present:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 11: Preparing for follow up after training — đoạn 4329

o an update on progress made in implementing their ANC action plan at their own facility; and o a case study of a pregnant woman and how she was provided ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: Note — đoạn 4330

•

### Nguồn gốc: Note — đoạn 4331

• • •

### Nguồn gốc: Note — đoạn 4332

Participants must ensure that they are ready for the follow-up visit by doing the following during the next four to six weeks: o Track implementation of their action plan o Prepare case studies: refer participants to the “Guidelines for presenting an antenatal case study” in their manual Remind participants that they should be prepared to discuss questions about case management, outcome, process and lessons learned.

### Nguồn gốc: Note — đoạn 4333

Refer them to the discussion guide (Summary of case management) in their manual and ask them to individually read the information in the box. Encourage them to ask questions and clarify any concerns.

### Nguồn gốc: Follow-up visit — đoạn 4334

•

### Nguồn gốc: Follow-up visit — đoạn 4335

•

### Nguồn gốc: Follow-up visit — đoạn 4336

•

### Nguồn gốc: Follow-up visit — đoạn 4337

The follow-up visit by a facilitator(s) should occur four to six weeks after completion of this course. Facilitators may elect to visit participants at their respective places of work or may organize a special session bringing participants together.

### Nguồn gốc: Participant role and activities — đoạn 4338

o At the follow-up visit or session, participants will review the implementation of their action plans and present a case study to demonstrate how they have applied their newly acquired ANC skills and knowledge. Facilitator role and activities. The facilitator must: o observe the organization and set-up of the ANC clinic; o engage with other key stakeholders in the facility, including management; o observe the participant in her role as care provider to a client. This step is important as it provides an opportunity for the participant to receive individualized guidance; and o review progress made in implementing the action plan, encourage and support the participant.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: The follow-up visit — đoạn 4339

• Present slide 12.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: The follow-up visit — đoạn 4340

• Describe the roles and responsibilities of participants during the site visit:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: The follow-up visit — đoạn 4341

what participants must do and how.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: The follow-up visit — đoạn 4342

• Task three participants to read out loud each segment of the slide and

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: The follow-up visit — đoạn 4343

emphasize the key points.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: The follow-up visit — đoạn 4344

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: The follow-up visit — đoạn 4345

Feedback discussions and way forward:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: The follow-up visit — đoạn 4346

• Feedback discussions on site during the follow-up visit provides an opportunity for participants to:

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 12: The follow-up visit — đoạn 4347

o share their experiences and discuss their action plan reports and case studies; o identify commonalities in the problems identified and solutions offered; and o initiate discussions under the following headings:

### Nguồn gốc: Lessons learned from case studies — đoạn 4348

A. In cases where the woman had a positive outcome:

### Nguồn gốc: Lessons learned from case studies — đoạn 4349

• Which actions helped make the woman’s pregnancy safer?

### Nguồn gốc: Lessons learned from case studies — đoạn 4350

• What made these actions possible?

### Nguồn gốc: Lessons learned from case studies — đoạn 4351

B. • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Lessons learned from case studies — đoạn 4352

In cases where the woman had a negative outcome:

### Nguồn gốc: Why were the actions not taken? — đoạn 4353

What can be done to make sure that the correct actions are taken in the future?

### Nguồn gốc: Lessons learned from action plan implementation — đoạn 4354

• Which strategies or actions facilitate successful changes in ANC practices?

### Nguồn gốc: Lessons learned from action plan implementation — đoạn 4355

• Which approaches make change more difficult?

### Nguồn gốc: Lessons learned from action plan implementation — đoạn 4356

• Make a list of recommendations for improving ANC practices.

### Nguồn gốc: Lessons learned from action plan implementation — đoạn 4357

• Identify one thing about your own practices that you intend to change to further contribute to making

### Nguồn gốc: Lessons learned from action plan implementation — đoạn 4358

pregnancy safer. End the discussions by congratulating participants on their achievements. Encourage them to continue to work to overcome challenges encountered and offer solutions if necessary.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Key messages (1) — đoạn 4359

• Present slides 13 and 14. Ask three participants to read out loud the key messages to conclude and end the session.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Key messages (1) — đoạn 4360

• Planning how you will implement your new skills and knowledge is essential

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Key messages (1) — đoạn 4361

for being able to apply them once you return to your facility.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Key messages (1) — đoạn 4362

• Several factors can influence the successful implementation of ANC for a

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Key messages (1) — đoạn 4363

positive pregnancy experience. Challenges that may need to be addressed include: o job guideline/policy restrictions o shifting staff roles and responsibilities o space and infrastructure requirements o transfer of knowledge and skills to the workplace o time and workload constraints o logistics management.

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Key messages (1) — đoạn 4364

• A follow-up visit is planned to occur 4–6 weeks after completion of this

### Nguồn gốc: Slide 13: Key messages (1) — đoạn 4365

course. At this visit, participants will present reports on progress made in implementing their action plans and a case study indicating how well they were able to use the newly acquired knowledge and skills in client

### Nguồn gốc: management. — đoạn 4366

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: End of course: reflections and closure — đoạn 4367

### Nguồn gốc: End of course: reflections and closure — đoạn 4368

Slides 15 and 16: End-of-course reflections

### Nguồn gốc: End of course: reflections and closure — đoạn 4369

• Present the reflections on slides 15 and 16 to end the course, and to motivate

### Nguồn gốc: End of course: reflections and closure — đoạn 4370

and encourage participants.

### Nguồn gốc: End of course: reflections and closure — đoạn 4371

• Participants need to follow the steps below for follow-up after the training

### Nguồn gốc: End of course: reflections and closure — đoạn 4372

event:

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Remember the key lessons that they learned from this course, which will — đoạn 4373

influence their ANC practice. (It may be a small thing, but small things can be very important building blocks for change).

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Prioritize five (5) lessons learned in this course that you plan to share with — đoạn 4374

colleagues at the workplace and describe how this will be done.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Identify key things about the ANC clinic where you work, which you intend — đoạn 4375

to change to improve the quality of antenatal care to ensure a positive pregnancy experience for your clients. Implement the change!

### Nguồn gốc: 4. List the key challenges that may hinder this change and describe how you — đoạn 4376

plan to overcome them.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Note: The impact of this training course will also be evaluated by comparing — đoạn 4377

your achievements to the intentions stated in your action plan.

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Prepare the consulting cubicle and ensure the following supplies and — đoạn 4570

• gloves

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Prepare the consulting cubicle and ensure the following supplies and — đoạn 4571

• rapid test kits: HIV, syphilis

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Prepare the consulting cubicle and ensure the following supplies and — đoạn 4572

• urine sample container and dipsticks

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Prepare the consulting cubicle and ensure the following supplies and — đoạn 4573

• blood pressure machine

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Prepare the consulting cubicle and ensure the following supplies and — đoạn 4574

• 0.5% chlorine solution

### Nguồn gốc: 1. Prepare the consulting cubicle and ensure the following supplies and — đoạn 4575

• leak-proof container or plastic bag

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Use an antiseptic hand rub or wash your hands thoroughly with soap and — đoạn 4576

water and dry with a clean dry cloth or allow to air dry.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4577

• Welcome and receive the client and support person promptly and

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4578

respectfully.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4579

• Perform quick check – if there is an emergency, conduct rapid

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4580

assessment and management. If stable, continue with ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4581

• Introduce yourself (if meeting client for the first time).

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4582

• Encourage support person to stay with client and oﬀer them seats.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4583

• Ask the woman why she has come to the clinic.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4584

• Explain all procedures and ask permission before any examinations or

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4585

tests.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4586

• Always keep the woman informed of what you are doing.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4587

• Be respectful and ensure privacy during history, examination and

### Nguồn gốc: 3. Welcome the client — đoạn 4588

counselling.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Encourage the woman and her support person to ask questions; and — đoạn 4589

listen to what they have to say.

### Nguồn gốc: History — đoạn 4590

Note: Be flexible with the order in which the questions are asked. For example, it may be better to link some questions with specific aspects of the woman’s physical examination. Allow the learners to ask appropriate follow-up questions while taking the ANC history and physical examination as needed.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Ask if she is having a medical, obstetric, social, or personal problem or — đoạn 4591

other concern, and if she has had any problems during this pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Ask if she has received care from another caregiver during this pregnancy. — đoạn 4592

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 5. Ask if she has received care from another caregiver during this pregnancy. — đoạn 4593

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care

### Nguồn gốc: 6. Ask about the first day of her last menstrual period and about her — đoạn 4595

menstrual cycles. Calculate her present gestation and EDC.

### Nguồn gốc: 8. Ask if she has used a family planning method before. If she has, ask which — đoạn 4596

method(s) and whether she liked the method(s).

### Nguồn gốc: 9. Ask if she plans to use family planning after this baby is born. If so, ask — đoạn 4597

which method.

### Nguồn gốc: 10. Ask if she has felt the baby move. If she has, ask the woman when the — đoạn 4598

baby first moved and whether she has felt it move in the last day.

### Nguồn gốc: 13. Ask how many pregnancies and how many deliveries she has had. Ask if — đoạn 4599

she has had any of these problems during a previous pregnancy or during/following childbirth:

### Nguồn gốc: 13. Ask how many pregnancies and how many deliveries she has had. Ask if — đoạn 4600

• convulsions during pregnancy or during/after childbirth

### Nguồn gốc: 13. Ask how many pregnancies and how many deliveries she has had. Ask if — đoạn 4601

• caesarean section, uterine rupture, or uterine surgery during childbirth

### Nguồn gốc: 13. Ask how many pregnancies and how many deliveries she has had. Ask if — đoạn 4602

• tears through the sphincter and/or rectum during childbirth

### Nguồn gốc: 13. Ask how many pregnancies and how many deliveries she has had. Ask if — đoạn 4603

• postpartum haemorrhage

### Nguồn gốc: 13. Ask how many pregnancies and how many deliveries she has had. Ask if — đoạn 4604

• stillbirth, preterm, low-birth-weight babies; babies who died before 1

### Nguồn gốc: 13. Ask how many pregnancies and how many deliveries she has had. Ask if — đoạn 4605

month of age; three or more spontaneous abortions.

### Nguồn gốc: 14. Ask if she has breastfed before. If yes, ask how long she breastfed and — đoạn 4606

whether she had any problems.

### Nguồn gốc: 15. Has she ever been diagnosed with any chronic illnesses, such as sickle cell — đoạn 4607

anaemia, diabetes, hypertension, asthma, tuberculosis, syphilis, HIV, heart disease, etc.?

### Nguồn gốc: 18. Ask if she is taking any drugs/medications (including traditional/local — đoạn 4608

preparations, herbal remedies, over-the-counter drugs, vitamins, or dietary supplements). Ask if she is using any bleaching creams.

### Nguồn gốc: 19. Ask if she has received the complete series of five tetanus immunizations — đoạn 4609

(TT / Td). If she has, ask if it has been less than 10 years since her last booster.

### Nguồn gốc: 19. Ask if she has received the complete series of five tetanus immunizations — đoạn 4610

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued — đoạn 4611

Continued... Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care

### Nguồn gốc: 21. Ask if she walks long distances, carries heavy loads or does heavy physical — đoạn 4612

labour.

### Nguồn gốc: 23. Ask what she normally eats in a day, and what she has eaten in the past — đoạn 4613

two days. Ask if she eats any non-food substances such as dirt or clay.

### Nguồn gốc: 24. Has she had a baby within the last year? If so, ask if she is currently — đoạn 4614

breastfeeding.

### Nguồn gốc: 25. Ask if she smokes, drinks alcohol, or uses any other possibly harmful — đoạn 4615

substances.

### Nguồn gốc: 27. Ask if: — đoạn 4616

• anyone has ever prevented her from seeing family or friends, stopped

### Nguồn gốc: 27. Ask if: — đoạn 4617

her from leaving her house, or threatened her life;

### Nguồn gốc: 27. Ask if: — đoạn 4618

• she has ever been injured, hit, or forced to have sex by someone; or

### Nguồn gốc: 27. Ask if: — đoạn 4619

• she is frightened of anyone.

### Nguồn gốc: Interim history (return visits) — đoạn 4620

Remember that questions about her present pregnancy should be asked at every ANC visit.

### Nguồn gốc: 28. Ask if she is having a medical, obstetric, social, or personal problem or other — đoạn 4621

concern.

### Nguồn gốc: 30. Ask if she has received care from another caregiver since her last visit. If so, — đoạn 4622

ask who provided the care, what care was provided, and what the outcome of the care was.

### Nguồn gốc: 32. Ask if her daily habits or lifestyle (workload, rest and dietary intake) — đoạn 4623

changed since her last visit.

### Nguồn gốc: 34. Ask if she has taken the drugs/medications prescribed and followed the — đoạn 4624

advice/recommendations provided at her last visit.

### Nguồn gốc: 35. Ask if she has had any reactions to or side-eﬀects from immunizations or — đoạn 4625

drugs/medications given at her last visit.

### Nguồn gốc: 35. Ask if she has had any reactions to or side-eﬀects from immunizations or — đoạn 4626

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

Continued... Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care

### Nguồn gốc: 36. Observe her general well-being: — đoạn 4628

• Her gait and movements (walks steadily and without a limp)

### Nguồn gốc: 36. Observe her general well-being: — đoạn 4629

• Her facial expression (is alert and responsive)

### Nguồn gốc: 36. Observe her general well-being: — đoạn 4630

• Her general cleanliness (no visible dirt, no odour)

### Nguồn gốc: 36. Observe her general well-being: — đoạn 4631

• Her skin (free from lesions and bruises)

### Nguồn gốc: 36. Observe her general well-being: — đoạn 4632

• Her conjunctiva (are pink, not white or very pale pink in colour)

### Nguồn gốc: 37. Measure her blood pressure while the woman is seated or lying down — đoạn 4633

with the knees slightly bent and relaxed:

### Nguồn gốc: 37. Measure her blood pressure while the woman is seated or lying down — đoạn 4634

• If diastolic BP is >90 mmHg, ask the woman if she has severe headache,

### Nguồn gốc: 37. Measure her blood pressure while the woman is seated or lying down — đoạn 4635

blurred vision or epigastric pain, and check her urine for protein.

### Nguồn gốc: 38. Explain the steps in the physical examination and obtain the woman’s — đoạn 4636

consent.

### Nguồn gốc: 39. Ask her to empty her bladder. Have her provide a urine sample for urine — đoạn 4637

testing.

### Nguồn gốc: 40. Have the woman undress in private. Ask her to remove only enough — đoạn 4638

clothing to complete the examination.

### Nguồn gốc: 41. Provide her with a cloth to cover the parts of her body that are not being — đoạn 4639

examined.

### Nguồn gốc: 42. Help her onto the examination surface and assist her to a comfortable — đoạn 4640

position.

### Nguồn gốc: 43. Conduct visual breast examination (first visit/as needed): — đoạn 4641

• Ask the woman to sit on the examination surface, uncover her body

### Nguồn gốc: 43. Conduct visual breast examination (first visit/as needed): — đoạn 4642

from the waist up, and place her arms at her sides.

### Nguồn gốc: 43. Conduct visual breast examination (first visit/as needed): — đoạn 4643

• Visually inspect the overall appearance of the woman’s breasts

### Nguồn gốc: 43. Conduct visual breast examination (first visit/as needed): — đoạn 4644

(contours, skin, nipples and note any abnormalities)

### Nguồn gốc: 43. Conduct visual breast examination (first visit/as needed): — đoạn 4645

• If nipples appear inverted, test for protractility by placing the thumb

### Nguồn gốc: 43. Conduct visual breast examination (first visit/as needed): — đoạn 4646

and fingers on either side of the areola and gently squeezing

### Nguồn gốc: 43. Conduct visual breast examination (first visit/as needed): — đoạn 4647

• If the nipple goes in, it is inverted.

### Nguồn gốc: 44. Ask the woman to lieon her back with her knees slightly bent and uncover — đoạn 4648

her abdomen.

### Nguồn gốc: 45. Check her abdomen for scars: — đoạn 4649

If there is a scar, ask whether it is from a caesarean section or other uterine surgery.

### Nguồn gốc: 46. Measure fundal height: — đoạn 4650

• If 12–22 weeks, palpate and estimate the weeks of gestation by

### Nguồn gốc: 46. Measure fundal height: — đoạn 4651

determining the distance between the top of the fundus and the symphysis pubis.

### Nguồn gốc: 46. Measure fundal height: — đoạn 4652

• If more than 22 weeks, use a tape measure to determine the

### Nguồn gốc: 46. Measure fundal height: — đoạn 4653

number of centimetres from the upper edge of the symphysis pubis to the top of the fundus. Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 46. Measure fundal height: — đoạn 4654

Continued...

### Nguồn gốc: Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued — đoạn 4655

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care

### Nguồn gốc: 47. Carry out fundal palpation: — đoạn 4656

• Stand at the woman’s side, facing her head.

### Nguồn gốc: 47. Carry out fundal palpation: — đoạn 4657

• Place both hands on the sides of the fundus at the top of the abdomen.

### Nguồn gốc: 47. Carry out fundal palpation: — đoạn 4658

• Using the flat part of your fingers, apply gentle but firm pressure to

### Nguồn gốc: 47. Carry out fundal palpation: — đoạn 4659

assess consistency and mobility of the foetal part.

### Nguồn gốc: 48. Carry out lateral palpation: — đoạn 4660

• Move your hands smoothly down sides of uterus to feel for foetal back.

### Nguồn gốc: 48. Carry out lateral palpation: — đoạn 4661

• Keep the dominant hand steady against the side of the uterus, while

### Nguồn gốc: 48. Carry out lateral palpation: — đoạn 4662

using the palm of the other hand to apply gentle but deep pressure to explore the opposite side of the uterus.

### Nguồn gốc: 48. Carry out lateral palpation: — đoạn 4663

• Repeat the procedure on the other side of the uterus.

### Nguồn gốc: 49. Carry out suprapubic (pelvic) palpation: — đoạn 4664

• Turn and face the woman’s feet.

### Nguồn gốc: 49. Carry out suprapubic (pelvic) palpation: — đoạn 4665

• Place your hands on either side of the uterus with your palms below

### Nguồn gốc: 49. Carry out suprapubic (pelvic) palpation: — đoạn 4666

the level of the umbilicus and your fingers pointing to the symphysis pubis.

### Nguồn gốc: 49. Carry out suprapubic (pelvic) palpation: — đoạn 4667

• Grasp the foetal part snugly between the hands to feel shape, size,

### Nguồn gốc: 49. Carry out suprapubic (pelvic) palpation: — đoạn 4668

consistency and mobility.

### Nguồn gốc: 49. Carry out suprapubic (pelvic) palpation: — đoạn 4669

• Observe the woman’s face for signs of pain/tenderness during

### Nguồn gốc: 49. Carry out suprapubic (pelvic) palpation: — đoạn 4670

palpation.

### Nguồn gốc: 50. Listen to the foetal heart rate: — đoạn 4671

• Place the foetal stethoscope (after 20 weeks) or Doppler on the

### Nguồn gốc: 50. Listen to the foetal heart rate: — đoạn 4672

woman’s abdomen (on the same side that you palpated the foetal back).

### Nguồn gốc: 50. Listen to the foetal heart rate: — đoạn 4673

• If using a foetal stethoscope, place your ear in close, firm contact with

### Nguồn gốc: 50. Listen to the foetal heart rate: — đoạn 4674

the stethoscope.

### Nguồn gốc: 50. Listen to the foetal heart rate: — đoạn 4675

• Remove your hands from the foetal stethoscope and listen to the foetal

### Nguồn gốc: 50. Listen to the foetal heart rate: — đoạn 4676

heart for a full minute, counting beats while watching the second hand of a clock.

### Nguồn gốc: 50. Listen to the foetal heart rate: — đoạn 4677

• Feel the woman’s pulse at the wrist, simultaneously, to ensure that

### Nguồn gốc: 50. Listen to the foetal heart rate: — đoạn 4678

foetal heart tones, and not maternal pulse, are being measured.

### Nguồn gốc: 51. Tell the woman what you are going to do before each step of the — đoạn 4679

examination.

### Nguồn gốc: 52. Ask the woman to uncover her genital area and cover or drape her to — đoạn 4680

preserve privacy and modesty.

### Nguồn gốc: 53. Ask the woman to separate her legs while continuing to keep her knees — đoạn 4681

slightly bent. Turn on and direct the light toward the genital area.

### Nguồn gốc: 54. Use an antiseptic hand rub or wash your hands thoroughly with soap and — đoạn 4682

water and dry with a clean, dry cloth or allow to air dry.

### Nguồn gốc: 56. Touch the inside of the woman’s thigh before touching genital area. — đoạn 4683

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 56. Touch the inside of the woman’s thigh before touching genital area. — đoạn 4684

Continued...

### Nguồn gốc: Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued — đoạn 4685

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care

### Nguồn gốc: 57. Separate the labia majora with two fingers, check the labia minora, the — đoạn 4686

clitoris, the urethral opening, and the vaginal opening.

### Nguồn gốc: 58. Palpate the labia minora: — đoạn 4687

• Look for swelling, discharge, tenderness, ulcers, and fistulas.

### Nguồn gốc: 58. Palpate the labia minora: — đoạn 4688

• Feel for irregularities and nodules.

### Nguồn gốc: 59. Check Skene’s gland for discharge and tenderness: — đoạn 4689

• With your palm facing upward, insert your index finger into the vagina

### Nguồn gốc: 59. Check Skene’s gland for discharge and tenderness: — đoạn 4690

and gently push upward against the urethra and milk gland on each side, and then directly, on the urethra.

### Nguồn gốc: 60. Check the Bartholin’s glands for discharge and tenderness: — đoạn 4691

• Insert your index finger into the vagina at the lower edge of the

### Nguồn gốc: 60. Check the Bartholin’s glands for discharge and tenderness: — đoạn 4692

opening and feel at the base of each labia majora.

### Nguồn gốc: 60. Check the Bartholin’s glands for discharge and tenderness: — đoạn 4693

• Using your finger and thumb, palpate each side for swelling or

### Nguồn gốc: 60. Check the Bartholin’s glands for discharge and tenderness: — đoạn 4694

tenderness.

### Nguồn gốc: 62. Immerse both gloved hands in a 0.5% chlorine solution: — đoạn 4695

• Remove the gloves by turning them inside out.

### Nguồn gốc: 62. Immerse both gloved hands in a 0.5% chlorine solution: — đoạn 4696

• If disposing of the gloves, place them in a leak-proof container or plastic

### Nguồn gốc: 62. Immerse both gloved hands in a 0.5% chlorine solution: — đoạn 4697

bag.

### Nguồn gốc: 62. Immerse both gloved hands in a 0.5% chlorine solution: — đoạn 4698

• If reusing them, submerge them in a 0.5% chlorine solution for 10

### Nguồn gốc: 62. Immerse both gloved hands in a 0.5% chlorine solution: — đoạn 4699

minutes to decontaminate.

### Nguồn gốc: 63. Use an antiseptic hand rub or wash your hands thoroughly with soap and — đoạn 4700

water and dry with a clean, dry cloth or allow to air dry.

### Nguồn gốc: 65. Share your findings with the woman. — đoạn 4701

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 65. Share your findings with the woman. — đoạn 4702

Continued...

### Nguồn gốc: Annex 4: Learning guide for ANC assessment continued — đoạn 4703

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care

### Nguồn gốc: 66. Perform a haemoglobin test (at contact 1 (<12 weeks), contact 3 (26 — đoạn 4704

weeks) and contact 6 (36 weeks), and as needed based on signs and symptoms):

### Nguồn gốc: 66. Perform a haemoglobin test (at contact 1 (<12 weeks), contact 3 (26 — đoạn 4705

• If the haemoglobin level is less than 7 g/dL, refer the woman to a

### Nguồn gốc: 66. Perform a haemoglobin test (at contact 1 (<12 weeks), contact 3 (26 — đoạn 4706

hospital.

### Nguồn gốc: 66. Perform a haemoglobin test (at contact 1 (<12 weeks), contact 3 (26 — đoạn 4707

• If the haemoglobin level is 7–11 g/dL, give iron/folate, 1 tablet twice

### Nguồn gốc: 66. Perform a haemoglobin test (at contact 1 (<12 weeks), contact 3 (26 — đoạn 4708

daily.

### Nguồn gốc: 67. Perform a syphilis screening test (first contact): — đoạn 4709

• If the result is positive, treat for syphilis with benzathine penicillin.

### Nguồn gốc: 67. Perform a syphilis screening test (first contact): — đoạn 4710

• Plan to treat the newborn.

### Nguồn gốc: 67. Perform a syphilis screening test (first contact): — đoạn 4711

• Encourage the woman to bring her sexual partner for treatment.

### Nguồn gốc: 67. Perform a syphilis screening test (first contact): — đoạn 4712

• Advise her on the correct and consistent use of condom to prevent reinfection after treatment.

### Nguồn gốc: 68. Inform the woman that HIV testing is recommended for all pregnant — đoạn 4713

women.

### Nguồn gốc: 68. Inform the woman that HIV testing is recommended for all pregnant — đoạn 4714

• If she chooses to be tested,

### Nguồn gốc: 68. Inform the woman that HIV testing is recommended for all pregnant — đoạn 4715

− conduct HIV testing using a rapid test kit, inform the client of the test result, and provide post-test counselling according to protocol; or − refer her to HIV testing services if not available at the clinic. − All HIV-negative pregnant women should be retested at 36 weeks or according to national PMTCT guidelines.

### Nguồn gốc: 69. Test for blood group and Rh on first contact. If Rh negative, the woman is — đoạn 4716

a candidate for anti-D immune globulin.

### Nguồn gốc: 71. If the woman lives in an area with a high prevalence of diabetes — đoạn 4717

/gestational diabetes, test her urine for glucose:

### Nguồn gốc: 71. If the woman lives in an area with a high prevalence of diabetes — đoạn 4718

• If the urine is positive for glucose, refer for diagnostic tests and

### Nguồn gốc: 71. If the woman lives in an area with a high prevalence of diabetes — đoạn 4719

treatment.

### Nguồn gốc: 71. If the woman lives in an area with a high prevalence of diabetes — đoạn 4720

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: 71. If the woman lives in an area with a high prevalence of diabetes — đoạn 4721

Learning guide for antenatal assessment (history, physical examination, testing) and care

### Nguồn gốc: Care provision — đoạn 4723

Note: Individualize the woman’s care by considering all findings gathered during assessment.

### Nguồn gốc: 72. Provide advice and counselling about diet and nutrition: — đoạn 4724

• Eat a balanced diet with a variety of foods.

### Nguồn gốc: 72. Provide advice and counselling about diet and nutrition: — đoạn 4725

• Eat an extra serving of staple food per day.

### Nguồn gốc: 72. Provide advice and counselling about diet and nutrition: — đoạn 4726

• Eat smaller, more frequent meals if necessary.

### Nguồn gốc: 72. Provide advice and counselling about diet and nutrition: — đoạn 4727

• Take 1 iron/folate tablet daily.

### Nguồn gốc: 73. Develop a birth plan with the woman, including all preparations for — đoạn 4728

normal birth and plans in case of emergency:

### Nguồn gốc: 73. Develop a birth plan with the woman, including all preparations for — đoạn 4729

• skilled provider and place of birth

### Nguồn gốc: 73. Develop a birth plan with the woman, including all preparations for — đoạn 4730

• transportation/emergency transportation and funds/emergency funds

### Nguồn gốc: 73. Develop a birth plan with the woman, including all preparations for — đoạn 4731

• decision-making and support person

### Nguồn gốc: 73. Develop a birth plan with the woman, including all preparations for — đoạn 4732

• items for clean and safe birth and for the newborn

### Nguồn gốc: 73. Develop a birth plan with the woman, including all preparations for — đoạn 4733

• emergency signs during pregnancy and signs of labour.

### Nguồn gốc: 74. Provide advice and counselling about common discomforts of pregnancy, — đoạn 4734

as needed.

### Nguồn gốc: 75. Provide advice and counselling about the use of potentially harmful — đoạn 4735

substances:

### Nguồn gốc: 75. Provide advice and counselling about the use of potentially harmful — đoạn 4736

• smoking, drinking, alcohol, drugs/medications.

### Nguồn gốc: 75. Provide advice and counselling about the use of potentially harmful — đoạn 4737

• Inform the health worker before use of prescription drugs.

### Nguồn gốc: 76. Provide advice and counselling about hygiene: — đoạn 4738

• hand washing

### Nguồn gốc: 76. Provide advice and counselling about hygiene: — đoạn 4739

• safe drinking water

### Nguồn gốc: 76. Provide advice and counselling about hygiene: — đoạn 4740

• food handling/safety

### Nguồn gốc: 76. Provide advice and counselling about hygiene: — đoạn 4741

• body, clothing, bedding, environment.

### Nguồn gốc: 77. Provide advice and counselling about rest and activity: — đoạn 4742

• decreasing heavy work

### Nguồn gốc: Annex 6: Anti-malarial drug dosing/regimes for malaria in pregnancy — đoạn 5742

(Note: refer to national guidelines for treatment of malaria in pregnancy if available). 1.

### Nguồn gốc: Artemisinin-based oral combination therapy (ACT), such as artemisinin-lumefantrine, artesu — đoạn 5743

artesunate-amodiaquine combination, are recommended for use only in the second or third trimester of pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: First trimester — đoạn 5744

First episode: • Quinine 10 mg/kg booking body weight orally every eight hours, plus clindamycin 300 mg every six hours for seven days. If clindamycin is not available, treat with quinine monotherapy. • An artemisinin-based combination therapy (ACT) can be used if quinine is not available, or if quinine plus clindamycin fails, or if adherence to seven-day treatment with quinine cannot be guaranteed.

### Nguồn gốc: First trimester — đoạn 5745

• Symptomatic treatment: paracetamol 1 g three times a day for five days; antiemetic treatment may be given if necessary.

### Nguồn gốc: First episode — đoạn 5746

Oral artemisinin-based combination therapy (ACT) is recommended. Based on national protocols and assuming a body weight of 50kg or more, treatment options and doses are: • artemether (80 mg) plus lumefantrine (480 mg) twice daily for three days; • artesunate (200 mg) plus amodiaquine (540 mg) once daily for three days; • artesunate (200 mg) plus mefloquine (440 mg) once daily for three days; • dihydroartemisinin (160mg) plus piperaquine (1280 mg) once daily for three days; and • artesunate (200 mg) once daily for three days, plus sulfadoxine/pyrimethamine (1500/75 mg) single dose only on day one.

### Nguồn gốc: First episode — đoạn 5747

Note: Quinine is associated with an increased risk of hypoglycaemia in late pregnancy. It should be used in combination with clindamycin only if effective alternatives are not available. Subsequent episodes: Artesunate + clindamycin or quinine with clindamycin.

### Nguồn gốc: First episode — đoạn 5748

2.

### Nguồn gốc: Severe/complicated malaria — đoạn 5749

Pregnant women with severe malaria are prone to hypoglycaemia, pulmonary oedema, anaemia and coma. Foetal death and premature labour are common. • They should be managed at a CEmONC facility. • Parenteral antimalarial drugs should be given in full doses without delay.

### Nguồn gốc: All trimesters — đoạn 5750

Parenteral artesunate: • is the treatment of choice in all trimesters. It causes more rapid parasitic depletion and does not cause hypoglycaemia. • Loading dose: artesunate 2.4 mg/kg body weight IV/IM every 12 hours for at least 24 hours. • Maintenance dose: artesunate 1.2 mg/kg body weight IV as a single bolus once daily beginning on the second day of treatment.

### Nguồn gốc: All trimesters — đoạn 5751

• Continue maintenance dosing until the woman is conscious and /or able to swallow; then give a complete oral treatment with ACT for three days.

### Nguồn gốc: All trimesters — đoạn 5752

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

### Nguồn gốc: All trimesters — đoạn 5753

Or Parenteral quinine: should be given either IV or IM until the patient can swallow.

### Nguồn gốc: IV administration — đoạn 5754

• Quinine hydrochloride, 10 mg/kg body weight (max. 600 mg) IV 8 hourly in 5-10 ml/kg of 5% dextrose over four hours; repeat 8 hourly. • If required for over 48 hours, reduce the dose to 5-7 mg/kg to avoid toxicity. • Change to oral medication when patient can swallow: oral quinine with clindamycin tablets for seven days or ACT for three days.

### Nguồn gốc: IV administration — đoạn 5755

• If parenteral artesunate or quinine is not available, give intramuscular artemether if the patient is not able to swallow. Note: If at BEmONC, give the initial dose of quinine IM over the anterior aspect of the thigh and refer to a CEmONC facility.

### Nguồn gốc: Other supportive measures — đoạn 5756

• intravenous line with fluids, preferably 5% dextrose • bladder catheter • oxygen • antipyretics (paracetamol is the drug of choice) • anticonvulsive medication if convulsions occur • blood transfusion if Hb <7 gm/dL • treatment of hypoglycaemia.

### Nguồn gốc: Other supportive measures — đoạn 5757

Antenatal care for a positive pregnancy experience | Health worker training | Facilitator’s manual | 2024

Annex 8: Clinical and laboratory staging of HIV/AIDS for adults and adolescents

### Nguồn gốc: Clinical stage 2 — đoạn 5821

• • • • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Clinical stage 3 — đoạn 5822

moderate unexplained weight loss (<10% of presumed or measured body weight) recurrent respiratory tract infections (sinusitis, bronchitis, otitis media, pharyngitis) angular cheilitis herpes zoster recurrent oral ulcerations papular pruritic eruptions seborrhoeic dermatitis fungal nail infections of fingers.

### Nguồn gốc: Clinical stage 3 — đoạn 5823

Conditions where a presumptive diagnosis can be made based on clinical signs or simple investigations: • • • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Clinical stage 3 — đoạn 5824

severe weight loss (>10% of presumed or measured body weight) unexplained chronic diarrhoea for more than one month unexplained persistent fever (intermittent or constant for longer than one month) oral candidiasis, oral hairy leukoplakia pulmonary tuberculosis (TB) diagnosed in the last two years severe presumed bacterial infections (pneumonia, empyema, pyomyositis, bone or joint infections, meningitis, bacteraemia) acute necrotizing ulcerative stomatitis, gingivitis, or periodontitis.

### Nguồn gốc: Conditions where confirmatory diagnostic testing is necessary — đoạn 5825

•

### Nguồn gốc: Clinical stage 4 — đoạn 5826

unexplained anaemia and/or neutropenia (<500/mm3) and/or thrombocytopenia (<50 000/mm3) for more than one month.

### Nguồn gốc: Clinical stage 4 — đoạn 5827

Conditions where a presumptive diagnosis can be made on the basis of clinical signs or simple investigations: • • • • • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: Pneumocystis carinii — đoạn 5828

recurrent or severe radiological bacterial pneumonia chronic herpes simplex infection (or labial, genital or anorectal of more than one-month duration) oesophageal candidiasis extrapulmonary tuberculosis

### Nguồn gốc: Kaposi sarcoma — đoạn 5829

central nervous system toxoplasmosis

### Nguồn gốc: HIV encephalopathy — đoạn 5830

Conditions where confirmatory testing is necessary: • • • • • • • • • • • • •

### Nguồn gốc: HIV encephalopathy — đoạn 5831

extrapulmonary cryptococcus, including meningitis disseminated non-TB mycobacterial infections progressive multifocal leukoencephalopathy (PML) candida of trachea, bronchi or lungs cryptosporidiosis isosporiasis visceral herpes simplex infection cytomegalovirus (CMV) infection (retinitis or of an organ other than liver, spleen or lymph nodes) any disseminated mycosis (histoplasmosis, coccidiomycosis, penicilliosis) recurrent non-typhoidal salmonella septicaemia lymphoma (cerebral or B cell non-Hodgkin) invasive cervical cancer visceral leishmaniasis

### Nguồn gốc: HIV encephalopathy — đoạn 5832

### Nguồn gốc: Note — đoạn 5847

Annex 9: Skills practice – obstetric assessment of the abdomen for foetal growth and well-being (optional)

### Nguồn gốc: Learning guide — đoạn 5848

This guide describes the steps and tasks that a care provider should employ to examine the abdomen of a pregnant client at an antenatal contact. It is specifically designed to assess foetal growth and well-being and identify any obstetric risk conditions. The guide includes steps for: A.

### Nguồn gốc: Learning guide — đoạn 5849

assessing foetal growth by measuring fundal height; and B. determining foetal lie and presentation.

### Nguồn gốc: A. Assessing foetal growth by measuring fundal height — đoạn 5850

The two techniques recommended for assessing uterine size and foetal growth are: • measurement of symphysiofundal height with a measuring tape (for gestations greater than 22 weeks); and • assessment of fundal height by palpation using abdominal landmarks.

### Nguồn gốc: A. Assessing foetal growth by measuring fundal height — đoạn 5851

1.

### Nguồn gốc: A. Assessing foetal growth by measuring fundal height — đoạn 5852

Place the zero line of the tape measure on the upper edge of the symphysis pubis.

### Nguồn gốc: A. Assessing foetal growth by measuring fundal height — đoạn 5857

2.

### Nguồn gốc: A. Assessing foetal growth by measuring fundal height — đoạn 5858

Stretch the tape measure across the contour of the abdomen to the top of the fundus (located by palpation) using the abdominal midline as the line of measurement.

### Nguồn gốc: A. Assessing foetal growth by measuring fundal height — đoạn 5859

3.

### Nguồn gốc: A. Assessing foetal growth by measuring fundal height — đoạn 5860

### Nguồn gốc: 1. For a normal healthy pregnancy, a minimum of eight comprehensive — đoạn 5972

contacts are recommended, preferably at: 8–20, 24, 26, 30, 34, 36, 38 and 40 weeks.

### Nguồn gốc: 2. Complete periodic reports on births, deaths and other indicators — đoạn 5973

according to regulations are one of the administrative activities that should be conducted either daily and /or occasionally to ensure the proper running of antenatal clinics.

### Nguồn gốc: 3. There is no need to conduct a quick check for all women attending ANC — đoạn 5974

services.

### Nguồn gốc: 4. Sociocultural and religious beliefs that are harmful during pregnancy are — đoạn 5975

not individual risk factors during pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: 7. Clinical decision-making occurs before development of the care plan and — đoạn 5976

does not need to occur again for that patient or client.

### Nguồn gốc: 8. At the antenatal clinic, all pregnant women should be screened for — đoạn 5977

anaemia at every antenatal visit.

### Nguồn gốc: 9. When checking for anaemia, the health care provider needs to ask the — đoạn 5978

pregnant woman if she has headache.

### Nguồn gốc: 10. The standard supplementary dose of iron and folic acid during — đoạn 5979

pregnancy is: iron 180 mg (three tablets) daily + folic acid 400 mcg daily.

### Nguồn gốc: 12. Group counselling is the best approach as it saves time for the provider; — đoạn 5980

information reaches all members of the audience at the same time; and it allows for experience sharing among pregnant women.

### Nguồn gốc: 13. Developing a birth and emergency plan is important but not a key — đoạn 5981

component of ANC.

### Nguồn gốc: 14. Malaria can aﬀect both the mother and foetus, although complications — đoạn 5982

in the foetus or newborn are rare.

### Nguồn gốc: 15. Malaria is less severe in women during their first or second pregnancies — đoạn 5983

than it is in subsequent pregnancies.

### Nguồn gốc: 17. Anaemia in pregnancy is diagnosed when a woman’s haemoglobin — đoạn 5984

concentration is found to be less than 8 g/dL during the first trimester or lower than 7 g/dL during the second trimester.

### Nguồn gốc: 18. Counselling and testing for HIV, hepatitis B and syphilis (2) can also be — đoạn 5985

oﬀered during labour and the postpartum period.

### Nguồn gốc: 19. Foetal position/lie and placental position/maturity are common — đoạn 5986

indications of ultrasound scanning during the first trimester of pregnancy.

### Nguồn gốc: 20. It is not necessary to have other clinical information to interpret — đoạn 5987

ultrasonography reports.

Annex 12B: Classroom quiz – mid course – sessions 1–6 (answers) The following quiz can be conducted as a classroom question-answer activity. Simply read each question to the class and allow a show of hands for the response. If an incorrect response is given, provide the correct one. Avoid accepting answers from the same trainee(s). Encourage all trainees to participate, as the quiz provides you with an opportunity to assess their level of knowledge and understanding so far (for example, the quiz covers the content of sessions 1 through 5).

### Nguồn gốc: At which antenatal contact should the woman’s haemoglobin be measured? — đoạn 6052

Depending on the progress made in this course, time constraints may prove a challenge. Suggested alternatives are: (i) reduce the number of selected questions asked; (ii) use some questions when summarizing the session as suggested in the previous session; and (3) hand out the quiz to the trainees as a take-home assignment, to be returned the next day for marking.

What is the recommended schedule of antenatal contacts (that is, how many routine visits and at what intervals)? Ans. Eight contacts at 12, 20, 26, 30, 34, 36, 38, 40 weeks.
