3.

### Nguồn gốc: Provision of preventive measures — đoạn 6061

Mention four things that the health care provider should do during all contacts with the woman to maintain privacy and confidentiality? Ans. Any five of the following: • ensure a private place for examination and counselling; • ensure that discussions, especially about sensitive subjects, cannot be overheard; • ensure that the woman has given her consent before discussing matters with her partner or family; • never discuss confidential information about clients with other providers, or outside of the health facility; • organize the examination area so that, during examinations, the woman is protected (with a curtain, screen or wall) from the view of other people; • ensure all records are confidential and kept locked away; and • limit access to logbooks and registers to responsible health care providers only.

### Nguồn gốc: Provision of preventive measures — đoạn 6062

4.

### Nguồn gốc: Provision of preventive measures — đoạn 6063

What are the daily and occasional administrative activities that should be conducted to ensure the proper running of antenatal clinics? Ans. • Records of equipment, supplies, drugs and vaccines should be updated. • Essential equipment should be checked daily to ensure proper functioning.

### Nguồn gốc: Provision of preventive measures — đoạn 6064

• Staff lists and schedules should be established. • Periodic reports on births, deaths and other indicators should be completed according to regulations.

### Nguồn gốc: Provision of preventive measures — đoạn 6065

5.

### Nguồn gốc: Provision of preventive measures — đoạn 6066

Why is it essential for health care providers to follow universal precautions when providing antenatal care? Ans. To protect the woman, her baby, themselves and the community from infection with bacteria and viruses, including HIV.

### Nguồn gốc: Woman — đoạn 6328

Name one drug that is recommended to treat a syphilis-positive pregnant woman? Answer: Pregnant women should be tested for syphilis during the first trimester and those positive should be given a single dose of 2 400 000 IU of benzathine benzylpenicillin as soon as possible.

### Nguồn gốc: Woman — đoạn 6329

### Nguồn gốc: Woman — đoạn 6330

Name three antiretroviral medicines given to prevent mother-to-child transmission of HIV? Answer: HIV-positive women should receive a single daily dose of TDF + 3TC (FTC) + EFV for life, and should be advised that the baby will also be given antiretrovirals (NVP or AZT) after birth and whilst breastfeeding.

### Nguồn gốc: Woman — đoạn 6331

They will also be offered early infant diagnosis for HIV. (NB. Follow most up-to-date guidance as the ARV drug-related recommendations change frequently)

### Nguồn gốc: Woman — đoạn 6332

### Nguồn gốc: Woman — đoạn 6333

A baby born to an HBV-positive mother must be given hepatis B immunoglobulin and hepatitis B vaccine at birth. True or false? Answer: True – all babies must now receive HB vaccine to protect them from infection irrespective of the mother’s status. Those with infected mothers additionally receive immunoglobulins to mop up any maternally transmitted infections.

### Nguồn gốc: recommendations? — đoạn 6344

Answer: One ultrasound scan before 24 weeks of gestation (early ultrasound, best around 22–24 weeks) is recommended for all pregnant women, to estimate gestational age, improve detection of foetal anomalies and multiple pregnancies, reduce induction of labour for post-term pregnancy, and improve the woman’s pregnancy experience.

### Nguồn gốc: The WHO Regional Office for Africa — đoạn 6558

The World Health Organization (WHO) is a specialized agency of the United Nations created in 1948 with the primary responsibility for international health matters and public health. The WHO Regional Office for Africa is one of the six regional offices throughout the world, each with its own programme geared to the particular health conditions of the Member States it serves.
